### [1] [ZGCM-1: A Fully Open and Extremely Efficient Foundation Model for Math and Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13356)
*Jiyan He,Guang Liang,Hao Liu,Haoxiang Guan,Jinbo Sun,Junyi Guo,Wenjun Feng,Yantai Xie,Yifei Shen,Bin Shao,Chuyang Wei,Kai Chen,Kexin Zhou,Minghang Zhu,Shuxin Zheng,Tie-Yan Liu,Taine Zhao,Wenhui Zhu,Xueyin Xu,Xiaoqing Zhang,Yatao Li,Yuxuan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZGCM-1 提出一套面向长上下文与代理化的小模型训练与开发流水线，通过架构+训练+自治运维协同，实现高效训练与跨尺度竞争力，并开源全套资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认为紧凑模型无法被动记忆大规模开源网页，提出通过‘内部思考 + 外部工具’弥补参数容量限制，从而实现高效的大上下文与代理能力。

Method: 从头训练 7B 密集模型，关键设计包括交错门控滑窗与全注意力混合的上下文架构、稳定的 FP8 Muon 优化器、分阶段上下文扩展（16K→64K→256K）与将交互轨迹重构为 MDP 的中期训练，以及由代理群自动化管理集群、数据与评估的 AI 原生研发流程。

Result: 在通用基准上对齐 7B 级别竞争力；在若干数学推理与搜索代理测试上接近或匹配数十倍参数的前沿模型（如 Qwen3-235B-A22B、GLM-5.1）；16K 预训练时间-损失达到约 4.2× 的效率提升；开源全套权重、检查点、代码与数据配方。

Conclusion: ZGCM-1-7B 展示了在架构、训练与自治运维协同下，小参数量模型通过内部推理+外部工具使用能够在长上下文与若干复杂任务上达到接近更大模型的性能，并显著提升预训练效率。

Abstract: In this work, we present ZGCM-1, a fully open 7B dense foundation model trained from scratch with extreme data, system, and algorithmic efficiency. ZGCM-1 is founded on a core premise: compact models cannot passively memorize the open web, but can overcome parametric capacity limits by coupling deliberate internal thinking with active external tool use. To support this paradigm across a 256K context, we develop an end-to-end, high-efficiency open training recipe: Architecture & System Co-design: interleaved gated sliding-window and full attention, and a stable FP8 Muon optimizer; Progressive Curriculum & MDP Mid-Training: context scaling across 16K, 64K, and 256K, and the reformulation of interaction traces into Markov Decision Processes. Furthermore, we establish an AI-native R&D workflow where agent swarms autonomously manage cluster operations, data curation, and rapid diagnostic evaluation. Extensive evaluations show that ZGCM-1-7B is competitive across 7B model family on general benchmarks. On several challenging mathematical reasoning and agentic search suites, it remains competitive with frontier models orders of magnitude larger, such as Qwen3-235B-A22B and GLM-5.1. We also show that our pre-training design offers a ~4.2x efficiency improvement in 16K pre-training time-to-loss. Across the full development lifecycle, we distill eight actionable empirical findings-spanning architectural scaling, SFT quality pruning, long-context generalization, and agentic co-training dynamics. To facilitate community research, we open-source model weights from the pre-training, mid-training, and post-training stages, intermediate checkpoints, training code, per-stage data and data recipes, and W&B logs.

</details>


### [2] [Converge Then Diversify: Decoupling Convergence and Diversity in Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13396)
*Chao Jiang,Yueling Huang,Miqing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CTD：先收敛再多样化的两阶段MOBO方法，在预算紧张或高维场景下相比SOTA有显著优势（72.9%胜出率），并给出两种简单实例化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MOBO同时兼顾收敛与多样性，但在评估预算极紧或维度高时难以兼顾，导致不能有效覆盖帕累托前沿。为此提出先快速收敛再展开多样化的策略以提高有限预算下的效率。

Method: CTD由两阶段组成：第一阶段以促进收敛为目标（使用常见采集函数引导搜索快速靠近帕累托前沿的某一点）；第二阶段以促进多样性为目标（使用常见采集函数在已收敛点附近及前沿上展开分散采样）。文中给出两种基于常用采集函数的具体实现。

Result: 通过大规模实验（446次成对比较）验证，CTD在72.9%的情形下显著优于现有方法，21.1%等效，6.1%更差，尤其在非常紧预算或高维问题上优势明显。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将收敛与多样性分离的两阶段MOBO策略（converge-then-diversify, CTD），先聚焦快速收敛到帕累托前沿上的一个点，再展开多样化搜索以覆盖前沿，从而在极紧预算或高维问题上显著提升性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimisation (MOBO) is a sample-efficient approach for optimising expensive black-box functions with multiple objectives. In MOBO, the goal is to adequately approximate the Pareto front; that is, to obtain a high-quality solution set with 1) good convergence (closeness to the Pareto front) and 2) good diversity (spread across the Pareto front). Existing MOBO methods typically aim to accomplish these two tasks simultaneously, i.e., driving the search towards the Pareto front while maintaining a diverse set of nondominated solutions, such that the solutions, ideally, can gradually approach the entire front. When sufficient search budgets are available, this approach is effective. However, considering both convergence and diversity throughout the search is not easy and requires careful design. Under very tight budgets, there may not be enough solutions generated to be able to simultaneously approach the entire Pareto front. To address this issue, this paper proposes a \textit{converge-then-diversify} (CTD) approach that decouples convergence and diversity into two stages. In the first stage, CTD focuses on convergence, aiming to quickly drive the search toward a single point on the Pareto front. In the second stage, CTD focuses on diversity, aiming to spread solutions across the front. We present two simple instantiations of CTD by using widely adopted acquisition functions in the area. Experimental results show that, across all 446 pairwise comparisons, CTD statistically outperforms state-of-the-art methods in 72.9\% of the cases, performs equivalently in 21.1\%, and is statistically worse in only 6.1\%, with the advantage being particularly evident in settings with very tight evaluation budgets or in high-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [3] [Generalized Agent Iteration: One Formal Framework for Iterative Policy Improvement and Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13406)
*Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Pengyi Li,Yifu Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GAI formalizes agents as modifiable-component systems undergoing cycles of evaluation and improvement, distinguishing classical iterative policy improvement from recursive self-improvement by two binary dials (whether the improver is internal and whether evaluation standard is external). This yields a unified framework to classify, compare, and reason about autonomous evolving intelligence and RSI risks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There is no single formal framework linking classical generalized policy iteration and recursive self-improvement; authors aim to create a unified formalism to compare, analyze, and design autonomous evolving intelligence systems.

Method: Formal definition of an agent as configurable components; modelling learning as cycles of evaluation and improvement; introduction of two dials (improver internality and grounding externality); mapping existing systems onto these axes; characterization of system polarities (anchored, goal drift, fully self-referential).

Result: Proposal of Generalized Agent Iteration (GAI) with precise axes distinguishing RSI from GPI, taxonomy of system polarities, and placement of existing systems on these axes; articulation of conditions that make RSI defective.

Conclusion: GAI unifies iterative policy improvement and RSI, offering a framework to analyze agents as systems of modifiable components where learning cycles of evaluation and improvement vary along two axes: internality of the improver and external grounding of standards; this enables formal comparison and identification of RSI failure modes.

Abstract: When we speak of recursive self-improvement (RSI), are we speaking of a phenomenon, a mechanism, or a prospect? Towards autonomous and evolving intelligence, RSI is being claimed at many scales, while no single framework that formally describes these emerging instances exists. Its counterpart in the classical realm, iterative policy improvement, is characterized by generalized policy iteration (GPI), a framework of broad applicability with well-understood theoretical properties, but only where the update principle and the evaluation base lie outside the agent. In this paper, we propose Generalized Agent Iteration (GAI), a formal framework that describes iterative policy improvement and RSI as two cases of a single learning paradigm. GAI defines the agent as a configuration of modifiable components within a system and models the learning process as a cycle of agent evaluation and agent improvement. Two pivotal dials then distinguish the instances: whether the improving mechanism is part of the agent and whether the standard it is measured against is grounded outside it. The former dial delineates the boundary between GPI and RSI, and the latter determines a system's polarity as anchored, goal drift, or fully self-referential. Moreover, we use these coordinates to place existing systems on the same two axes and make the defects of recursive self-improvement statable one condition at a time. We see this paper as a first step toward exploring a formal characterization of RSI that rests on the classical account, makes existing systems comparable, and provides a principled basis for analyzing and designing new ones.

</details>


### [4] [Vibe Patenting: Evaluating LLM Judges for Professional Patent-Drafting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13422)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Vlad Blaykhman,Ye Wang,Jing Liu,Gene V. Vinokur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM judges help iteratively improve AI patent drafts and reduce the need for expensive agents, but their assessments partially disagree with human experts and depend on chosen metrics, so they’re useful but limited as sole evaluators or optimization signals.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Assess whether LLM-based judges are reliable evaluators and useful optimization signals for complex, high-stakes professional tasks like patent drafting.

Method: Construct Vibe Patenting testbed: generate patent drafts via multiple agent configurations, use a separately-invoked LLM judge to evaluate drafts and provide structured feedback, run iterative revision cycles, compare judge-assessed improvements across inventions and agent types, and validate LLM judge judgments against a professional patent attorney.

Result: Judge-guided revision consistently improves judge-assessed drafting quality; unguided revision plateaus. Iterative judge feedback lets low-reasoning agents close much of the gap to high-reasoning agents. Stronger models and domain-specific agentic workflows further improve quality. Validation against a human attorney shows meaningful but metric-dependent agreement and systematic calibration differences.

Conclusion: LLM judges can effectively guide iterative improvement of AI-generated patent drafts, boosting judged quality and enabling cheaper agents to approach higher-reasoning agents, but their reliability is limited and metric-dependent compared to human experts.

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to evaluate and improve AI-generated outputs, yet their reliability for complex professional work remains unclear. We study this problem through Vibe Patenting, an end-to-end patent-drafting testbed for AI-agent evaluation. A separately-invoked LLM judge evaluates generated patent drafts and provides structured feedback for iterative revision. Across multiple inventions and drafting-agent configurations, judge-guided revision consistently improves judge-assessed quality, while unguided revision tends to saturate. Notably, iterative judge feedback enables a low-reasoning agent to approach the performance of a substantially more expensive high-reasoning agent. Stronger models and increased reasoning generally improve judge-assessed drafting quality, while domain-specific agentic workflows provide further gains. We validate the judge against independent evaluation by a professional patent attorney and find meaningful but strongly metric-dependent agreement and systematic calibration differences. These results highlight both the utility and limitations of LLM judges as evaluators and optimization signals for complex professional workflows.

</details>


### [5] [Toward Self-Adaptive Physical AI: Can LLM Agents Manage Long-Horizon Physical Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13436)
*Varun Kaushik,Yayun Tan,Xiaofan Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A multi-agent LLM framework was tested on agricultural tasks; zero-shot LLMs matched RL performance in the same weather and adapted better to unseen weather shifts, pointing to promising self-adaptive physical agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To build autonomous agents that manage long-term physical tasks without human intervention or costly retraining, and to test whether LLM-based agents can adapt zero-shot to environmental changes.

Method: Design of a multi-agent system that integrates planning, tool calling, observation, and verification; empirical evaluation on agricultural management tasks comparing zero-shot LLM agents and RL agents under varying weather patterns.

Result: Zero-shot LLM agents achieved similar management outcomes to RL agents in-distribution and showed superior adaptability when evaluated under shifted weather conditions, indicating improved generalization to distributional changes.

Conclusion: Zero-shot LLM agents in a multi-agent framework can manage long-term physical tasks comparably to RL under the same environment and adapt better when the environment shifts, supporting feasibility of self-adaptive physical AI agents.

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer a promising path toward autonomously managing long-term physical tasks without human intervention. However, physical tasks require agents to continuously observe the environment, make consequential actions, and remain effective as the environment changes. Existing approaches either require substantial data and retraining, or primarily focus on agents operating in the virtual world. In this work, we explore the feasibility of building a self-adaptive physical AI agent that manages long-term physical tasks in a zero-shot manner and adapts to environmental changes without human intervention. We design a multi-agent framework that integrates planning, tool calling, observation, and verification, and evaluate it on agricultural tasks against reinforcement learning (RL) agents under different weather patterns. Our results show that zero-shot LLM agents can achieve comparable management outcomes to RL agents under the same weather pattern and adapt more effectively than RL when evaluated under a shifted environment, highlighting a promising path toward self-adaptive physical AI agents.

</details>


### [6] [LabAgent: Customize Any Research Hubs for Scientific Discoveries Using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13437)
*Lei Liu,Yikun Zhang,Jialin Chen,Wanjia Zhao,Rex Ying,Wengong Jin,Hua Xu,James Zou,Tianyu Liu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向实验室持续工作的智能体LabAgent，通过执行验证与纠错记录机制提高方法复现性与知识传承，在多项生命科学任务中优于商业通用智能体并成功复现发表结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室人员变动导致已开发方法难以延续与复现，耗费大量人力物力的工作难以传承；需一种系统化手段保证技能可执行、可验证并记录纠错经验以维持连续科研工作。

Method: 提出一种面向实验室的智能体框架“LabAgent”，包含两套机制：一是保证技能可执行与可验证的执行-验证机制，二是记录纠正方法与经验的纠错与知识积累机制，从而实现直接修正或避免相似错误。将该框架应用于生命科学若干下游任务。

Result: 在药物性质预测、生物医学问题分析、蛋白变异效应预测与统计遗传学等领域对比中，LabAgent在所有领域超过商业通用智能体并列第一，且能准确复现已发表图表，显示其在整合与扩展实验室知识上的有效性。

Conclusion: LabAgent能在维持实验室长期工作流与知识继承方面发挥实际作用，通过可执行性验证与纠错经验记录两大机制，提高方法复现性与发现新知识的能力。

Abstract: Scientific research is a continuous process that emphasizes inheritance. Methods developed by predecessors are often expanded upon by new researchers to explore more novel and in-depth scientific questions. However, the change of lab staff, such as student graduation, leads to a lack of personnel capable of replicating methods. Methods that have been developed with significant effort and resources cannot be continued. To address these limitations, we propose LabAgent, a reproduce and discovery harness tailored for a lab's continuous work. LabAgent employs two mechanisms to guarantee that all skills can be executed and verified and to record the corrective methods and experiences, allowing for direct correction or avoidance of similar errors. We applied LabAgent to drug property prediction, biomedical problem analysis, protein variant effect prediction, and statistical genetics in life science domains. LabAgent ranks first over commercial generalist agents in every domain, and demonstrates accurate reproduction of a published figure. Overall, these results demonstrate that LabAgent can effectively integrate and reasonably expand laboratory knowledge.

</details>


### [7] [OrchSLM: Probing the Dynamics of Small Language Model Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13470)
*Chengxi Zhang,Yu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OrchSLM：一个统一的非交互式 SLM 路由框架，把不同编排方法的设计选择参数化，并证明任务结构、模型组合与共识机制等可控因素共同决定编排行为，支持用 SLM 集合替代部分 LLM 工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大模型部署存在延迟、隐私、连通性和计算开销等问题；而许多 agentic 工作负载包含重复且范围受限的子任务，适合由成本更低的 SLMs 专门化处理；但 SLM 的能力和上下文窗口受限，难以支持长时程推理和交互性编排，因而需要非交互式的替代方案。

Method: 设计一个统一的路由框架（OrchSLM），把现有的非交互式编排方法的设计选择参数化，允许独立生成候选解的异构 SLMs 将样本缓存，并由路由器在不再进行模型交互的情况下进行整合与选择；使用该框架作为系统化探针来研究不同设计参数如何影响编排行为。

Result: 通过 OrchSLM 的系统探索，作者揭示了编排性能并非由单一因素决定，而是由任务结构、模型池的多样性与能力分配、以及多智能体共识策略等多重‘旋钮’共同塑造；该框架统一了若干非交互式方法并提供可控性，从而为 SLM 编排策略设计提供理论与实践指引。

Conclusion: OrchSLM 提出并验证了非交互式路由编排范式，表明通过可控设计参数（任务结构、模型池组成、多智能体共识机制等）可以在小模型集合上实现有效的编排，缓解单一大模型在延迟、隐私、成本等方面的问题。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, their reliance on cloud-scale infrastructure poses fundamental challenges for deployment in agentic pipelines, including latency, privacy, connectivity, and substantial computational cost. Small language models (SLMs) offer a compelling alternative: recent studies suggest that many repetitive and narrowly scoped subtasks in agentic workloads may be better served by specialized SLMs than by monolithic LLMs. However, the limited capacity and context windows of SLMs can constrain long-horizon reasoning and interaction-heavy orchestration strategies such as iterative verification and debate. This motivates a complementary, non-interactive paradigm in which heterogeneous SLMs independently generate candidate solutions and a router orchestrates their cached samples without further model interaction. To further understand the mechanisms of such orchestration, we introduce OrchSLM, a routing framework that unifies existing non-interactive orchestration methods and exposes their underlying design choices as controllable parameters. Using OrchSLM as a systematic probe, we reveal how orchestration behavior emerges from diverse knobs, including the task structure, model-pool composition, and multi-agent consensus.

</details>


### [8] [Token Efficient Task Execution via Application Behavior Modeling for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13491)
*Alexandru Ianta,Eleni Stroulia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OdoBot：通过从示例构建应用行为模型来驱动 web-agent，显著降低 token 成本（-44%/-80%），并在 Canvas LMS 实验中至少优于 WebVoyager 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 agent 任务和应用场景的扩展，token 处理成本快速上升，亟需更低成本的 web-agent 解决方案；因此提出利用行为模型减少交互和 token 使用的思路。

Method: 通过分析成功的任务执行示例，构建目标 web 应用的行为模型；在此模型之上设计新的 agent 架构（OdoBot），以较少的 token 交互完成自然语言描述的 web-应用任务，并与 Agent-E 和 WebVoyager 在 Canvas LMS 的 45 个任务上做对比实验。

Result: 在 Canvas LMS 的 45 个任务上，OdoBot 比 Agent-E 与 WebVoyager 分别少用 44% 与 80% 的 tokens，并且在任务成功率上超越了 WebVoyager（与 Agent-E 的成功率对比未详述）。

Conclusion: OdoBot 在保证或提升任务成功率的同时显著降低了处理代价，证明了基于行为模型的 web-agent 架构在节省 token 成本上具备明显优势。

Abstract: The strong performance of AI Agents across an impressive variety of tasks is driving an unprecedented investment in agentic infrastructures, however the cost of processing tokens is fast increasing. Web agents automate the execution of web-application tasks described in natural language, by analyzing the web-application's user interface (UI) and interacting with it. This work introduces OdoBot, a novel web-agent architecture that completes tasks at a fraction of the cost when compared to conventional web agents. This is achieved by leveraging a behavioral model of the underlying application constructed by analyzing successful task-execution demonstrations. Our experiments with 45 tasks on the Canvas Learning Management System (LMS) demonstrate that OdoBot uses 44% and 80% fewer tokens than two state-of-the-art competitor agents (Agent-E and WebVoyager), while also surpassing WebVoyager in terms of task success rate.

</details>


### [9] [From Legal Text to AI-specific Risk Sources: A Systematic Analysis of the EU AI Act's High-Risk Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13535)
*Ronald Schnitzer,Mike Auer,Rumpa Choudhury,Andreas Hapfelmeier,Maximilian Hoeving,Isabelle Painter,Josiane Xavier Parreira,Sonja Zillner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性梳理了欧盟AI法案第2节的高风险要求，发现多数为组织性义务，仅少部分直接覆盖AI特有风险；基于AI相关条款构建了‘EU AI Act Risk Source List’，为将法规要求与现有AI风险分类法对齐提供了结构化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI风险管理依赖结构化的风险分类法，而欧盟AI法案通过高风险要求强制治理AI风险。缺乏将法案中隐含的风险与既有风险分类法映射的清晰方法，导致从业者无法有效将合规义务嵌入日常风险管理实践。

Method: 对欧盟人工智能法案（第2节：高风险AI系统要求）中的条文进行系统性抽取与分类，区分直接针对AI风险源的要求与组织/流程类要求；从AI风险相关的条款中整合并提炼出独立的AI特有风险来源，形成风险来源清单。

Result: （1）完成对第2节要求的系统分类，量化表明多数为组织/文档义务，只有少数直接与AI特有风险源对应；（2）从相关条款中提炼出一份合并的AI风险来源清单（EU AI Act Risk Source List），可作为对现有AI风险分类法与法案要求进行逐条比对与对齐的参考；（3）提出该清单能促进法律义务与风险治理实践的衔接，但须进一步扩展与验证。

Conclusion: 从欧盟人工智能法案第2节的强制性要求出发，只有少数要求直接对应AI特有的风险来源；大多数属于组织性流程与文档义务。因此，现行法案与AI风险分类法在覆盖的风险源上并不完全一致。本文通过系统分类并汇总出一份‘EU AI Act Risk Source List’，为在法规义务与AI风险管理实践之间建立对齐提供了结构化参考。

Abstract: The EU AI Act introduces mandatory requirements for high-risk AI systems with the explicit goal of ensuring the development and operation of trustworthy AI. At the same time, AI risk management practices rely on structured risk taxonomies to systematically identify and treat AI-specific risk sources. As both the AI Act and established risk taxonomies aim to address AI-induced risks, a natural question is whether they align in the risk sources they cover. However, no clear mapping exists between the risks implicitly addressed by the Act's high-risk requirements and established taxonomies, leaving practitioners without a structured basis for aligning regulatory obligations with AI risk management practice. This paper presents a systematic classification of the requirements extracted from the EU AI Act Section 2 (Requirements for high-risk AI systems), revealing that only a minority directly address AI-specific risk sources, while the majority impose organizational process and documentation obligations. From the AI risk-related requirements, a consolidated list of distinct AI-specific risk sources is derived. The resulting EU AI Act Risk Source List takes an important step towards bridging the gap between legal obligation and AI risk management practice, providing a structured reference for explicit comparison between existing AI risk taxonomies and the risk sources implicitly addressed by the EU AI Act. Important Note: This is the authors' preprint. The paper was presented at the 4th International Conference on Frontiers of Artificial Intelligence, Ethics, and Multidisciplinary Applications. A link to the conference's official proceedings will be provided upon publication.

</details>


### [10] [Asclepius: An Adaptive Harness for Long-Horizon Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13543)
*Grace Chang Yuan,Xiaoman Zhang,Sung Eun Kim,Luyang Luo,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Addressing long-horizon execution gaps in LLM agents for emergency-department shifts, Asclepius’ three-part scaffolding yields substantial improvements in critical-action correctness and timeliness, but benefits arise only when all components operate jointly.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Short single-task benchmarks miss long-horizon, resource-constrained execution failures encountered in real deployments; need a testbed and solutions for continuous-time, multi-patient ED management.

Method: Use the Clinical Environment Simulator to run long-horizon ED shift rollouts, define three per-trace counters for long-horizon failure modes, and introduce Asclepius — a self-evolving harness that rewrites operating manuals from trace feedback, an externalized clinical skills library, and three subagents partitioning per-turn decisions. Evaluate on held-out batches and judge outputs from five LLMs across three model families.

Result: On held-out batches, Asclepius improves critical-action correctness by 22% (p=0.024) over a strong baseline while maintaining diagnostic accuracy; on the full ten-batch set, gains reach 25% (critical actions) and 13% (timeliness). Improvements are consistent across multiple LLM judges; reductions in failures require all three components together.

Conclusion: Asclepius significantly reduces long-horizon execution failures in simulated ED shifts, improving timely critical actions while preserving diagnostic accuracy; the three proposed components must act together for decisive gains.

Abstract: LLM agents are predominantly benchmarked on short, single-task trajectories, yet real deployments run for hours under contention, surfacing a different class of failures. We use the Clinical Environment Simulator (CES), in which an agent manages an entire emergency-department shift under continuous time and resource pressure, as a testbed: long-horizon execution failures manifest measurably in a single rollout under structured, multi-dimensional grading. On CES, current agents reach the correct diagnosis in most cases yet fail to deliver complete and timely critical actions, revealing an execution gap. We attribute this gap to three long-horizon failure modes, each operationalized as a per-trace counter: instruction-adherence drift, treatment incompleteness, and a severity-equity gap in timeliness. We then introduce Asclepius, an adaptive agent scaffolding with a self-evolving harness that rewrites the operating manual between shifts from trace-level feedback, an externalized clinical skills library for high-stakes regimen knowledge, and three isolated subagents that partition per-turn decisions across the patient queue. On held-out batches never observed during harness evolution, Asclepius improves critical-action correctness by 22% (p = 0.024) over a strong baseline agent framework while preserving diagnostic accuracy, with consistent gains across five LLM judges from three model families; on the full ten-batch set, improvements reach 25% on critical actions and 13% on timeliness. The three failure modes form a coupled bottleneck: decisive reductions appear only when all three components act together.

</details>


### [11] [Toward a Decision-Assurance Layer for AI-Assisted Flight Planning in Air Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13552)
*Alexandre Barreto,Shou Matsumoto,Jorge Valverde-Rebaza,Cleiton Ataide,Paulo Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATAL——一个基于语义稳定性、结构一致性与规范验证的模型无关决策保证层，用以衡量生成式AI在航管任务中输出的可用性并映射为决策就绪度，实验证明可有效辨别不安全或不一致建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在航管中被非正式用于航路与航班计划相关任务，但其非确定性输出可能导致错误、误导或违反规则的建议，从而在以人为回路的安全关键决策中产生风险，因而需要一种系统化、可度量的保证机制以支持人工复核与合规性要求。

Method: 设计一种模型无关的决策保证架构（ATAL），将三类信号——（1）对提示变动的语义稳定性检验，(2）结构化输出的操作一致性检查，(3）基于领域规则的规范性约束验证——进行聚合并映射为‘决策就绪度等级（DRL）’，为人工操作员提供可操作的信心水平指示；并在仿真/实验环境中对生成式AI的失效模式进行案例化测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，ATAL能有效识别出语义不稳定、结构不一致或违反规范的AI输出，且通过DRL为操作员提供明确的可操作性判断点，减少潜在错误被采纳的概率；此外，架构设计简洁、模型无关，便于在其他需要监管与人类监督的安全关键领域应用。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了AI信任与保证层（ATAL），证明其能在生成式AI辅助的航班计划场景中评估并筛除不可靠输出，从而降低由非确定性模型引发的安全与运营风险，且方法具有跨域可迁移性。

Abstract: Generative AI is increasingly being used informally in Air Traffic Management (ATM) for tasks such as flight plan generation, trajectory interpretation, and constraint checking. Although these tools can reduce workload and accelerate planning, their non-deterministic outputs create safety and operational risks in human-in-the-loop settings. This paper proposes the AI Trust and Assurance Layer (ATAL), a model-agnostic decision assurance architecture that evaluates whether AI-generated flight-planning outputs are sufficiently reliable for operational use. ATAL combines semantic stability under prompt variation, operational consistency of structured outputs, and normative constraint validation against domain rules, and maps these signals to a Decision Readiness Level (DRL) for human operators. An ATM-inspired experimental study shows how unsafe, inconsistent, or misleading outputs can be identified before influencing flight-plan validation or execution. Although demonstrated in aviation, the framework is also transferable to other safety-critical decision-support domains that require human oversight under regulatory constraints.

</details>


### [12] [Planning or Learning: Reliability and Cost in Multi-Asset Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13566)
*Xian Yeow Lee,Chandrasekar Venkatraman,Ahmed Farahat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在统一基准下，规划保证零故障但成本不敏感于故障罚金，RL在可容忍故障时更节约成本——两者各有适用场景，且基准可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业维护场景含多资产与共享资源，需在可靠性与运营成本间权衡，但现有工作缺乏在相同设置下对比规划与RL的系统性实验与可重现基准。

Method: 基于多资产轴承的run-to-failure数据，构建统一环境、成本模型与评估协议；在不同故障处罚强度下，对比规划方法与RL代理的策略行为与成本表现；测试轻量约束手段（如奖励整形、动作屏蔽）以鼓励RL满足可靠性需求。

Result: 观察到显著的行为差异：规划通过硬约束实现零故障且成本随故障处罚变化不显著；RL在低罚金下通过减少预防维护降低成本，但会接受偶发故障且在高罚金下仍难完全消除故障；奖励整形与动作屏蔽可部分引导RL提高可靠性；并给出实用化建议与基准协议。

Conclusion: 规划（planning）将可靠性作为硬约束，产出零故障策略且总成本对故障处罚幅度不敏感；强化学习（RL）以期望成本最小化，常在可容忍少量故障和预防性维护之间权衡，在低惩罚区间成本更低但会出现持续的非零故障，即便惩罚较高也难完全消除故障。两者互为互补：当要求严格可靠性且部署期较短时优先选择规划；可接受少量故障且追求长期成本效率时可选RL。作者还提供了统一的基准协议作为可重用模板。

Abstract: Industrial maintenance systems involve multiple interacting assets and shared resources, making it challenging to balance reliability and operational cost using a single decision framework. While recent work has focused on reinforcement learning (RL) for maintenance scheduling, direct comparisons with planning approaches under identical settings remain limited. In this work, we empirically compare planning and RL for multi-asset bearing maintenance using run-to-failure data. We examine how these methods behave when balancing preventive maintenance against tolerable failures across a range of failure penalty scenarios. We observed a consistent behavioral difference driven by objective formulation. Planning enforces reliability as a hard constraint and produces zero-failure policies whose total cost is largely insensitive to the magnitude of failure penalties. RL agents optimize expected cost and often trade off preventive maintenance against occasional failures as penalties vary, resulting in lower costs under low-penalty regimes but persistent non-zero failures even when penalties are high. We also investigate lightweight constraint mechanisms, including reward shaping and action masking, to encourage RL's reliability. From a practical perspective, planning may be more suitable when strict reliability is required and deployment horizons are short, whereas RL may provide cost-efficient policies when limited failures are acceptable and long-run operational efficiency is prioritized. Overall, this study clarifies the trade-offs between reliability and cost in multi-asset maintenance and suggests that planning and RL are complementary approaches. Beyond these findings, the controlled benchmark protocol itself that unifies environment, cost model, and evaluation across paradigms, offers a reusable template for comparing decision-making approaches in other maintenance settings.

</details>


### [13] [How User-AI Mistreatment Occurs and Matters in Conversational Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13579)
*Fanqi Zeng,Sadid A. Hasan,Chaocheng He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文量化了用户对模型的敌意（方法、频率、模型间差异和时序结构），发现检测器类型与现象不同、道歉与下一轮敌意正相关但跨模型呈反向关系，并提供可复现资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展安全研究视角：除了模型产生的危害，还需理解用户如何对模型施加敌意/胁迫，以便正确解读模型行为、对齐漂移与部署风险。

Method: 对777K条英文LMSYS-Chat-1M对话进行审计，使用两个独立检测器：一个八类面向模型的敌意词汇表（识别侮辱、威胁、越狱胁迫等）和数据集自带的内容审核信号；交叉验证检测器、去重首轮提示、按模型分层统计并分析会话内时序与因果相关性（包括对拒绝回复与越狱对话的控制）。

Result: 两类检测器合并标记约5%的用户回合；经对狭义词汇表联合测量精确度调整后估计直接针对助理的虐待率为0.90%。模型间用户敌意差异达13倍，首轮敌意传播更广且极端差异超过15倍；助理的道歉在会话内与下一轮敌意呈一致正相关（在20/23模型中为正），但跨模型更多道歉与更少敌意相关；释放了词汇表、检测器交叉验证流水线与所有派生表格。

Conclusion: 在大规模评估流量下，针对助理的直接辱骂/胁迫总体罕见（经精确度调整约0.90%），但不同检测器捕捉到弱重叠的现象；用户对模型的敌意在模型之间呈显著差异，主要由各模型吸引到的用户群体驱动；对话内助理的道歉与下一轮敌意增加相关，但整体上更“爱道歉”的模型收到的敌意更少；敌意在时间上有结构性的分布（胁迫集中在开场，情绪化敌意随会话累积）。

Abstract: Safety research often focuses on model-generated harms, but users may also direct hostility, coercion, and adversarial pressure at models. Understanding how and when that occurs is essential for accurately interpreting model behaviour, alignment drift, and real-world deployment risks. In this paper, we audit 777K English LMSYS-Chat-1M conversations with two independent detectors: an eight-category lexicon for hostility directed at the model, and the dataset's moderation signal; and show that they capture different, weakly overlapping phenomena. The lexicon identifies insults, threats, and jailbreak coercion aimed at the assistant, while moderation flags are dominated by toxic-content solicitation rather than hostility at the model. Together, they mark about 5% of user turns; adjusting the narrower lexicon-harassment union for measured precision puts mistreatment aimed at the assistant at 0.90%. These absolute rates describe arena-style evaluation traffic and should not be read as deployment-wide base rates. We find that user hostility varies 13-fold across models, driven largely by who each model attracts rather than by model behaviour: first-turn hostility spreads far wider than post-response hostility, and more than fifteenfold separates the extremes even after deduplicating opening prompts. Within conversations, assistant apologies are consistently associated with higher odds of next-turn hostility under both detectors; the effect survives restricting to non-refused prior turns and to jailbreak-free conversations, and is positive in 20 of 23 models. Yet across models, more apologetic models receive less hostility overall. Finally, hostility also shows temporal structure, with coercive openings front-loading the first turn while affective hostility accumulates over a session. We release the lexicon, the detector cross-validation pipeline, and all derived tables.

</details>


### [14] [FLoKD: Adaptive Knowledge Distillation for Federated Low-Rank LLM over Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13580)
*Xinlu Zhang,Na Yan,Yang Su,Yansha Deng,Toktam Mahmoodi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLoKD trades full-parameter/logits transmission for selectively transmitted LoRA activations, using block scoring and dataset filtering to halve communication while retaining competitive perplexity in federated LoRA fine-tuning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard FL with LLMs is communication-heavy: full-parameter transfer costs are prohibitive, LoRA reduces parameters but still scales with model size, and logits-level distillation is expensive due to sequence length and vocabulary. A middle ground—activations—can preserve supervision signal with lower bandwidth if selective transmission is applied.

Method: Combine LoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning with knowledge distillation over activations: clients send selected intermediate LoRA activations as distillation signals to a central server/peers. Introduce a transformer block importance scoring mechanism to select which blocks’ activations to transmit, and two public dataset selection strategies that filter out samples diverging from local distributions and prioritize samples informative for distillation.

Result: Extensive experiments on WikiText-103, PTB, and Dialog show FLoKD reduces communication by 50–65% relative to baselines and achieves fast convergence to comparable perplexity. Selective block and sample transmission are key contributors to the savings.

Conclusion: FLoKD enables communication-efficient federated LoRA fine-tuning of LLMs by distilling intermediate LoRA activations instead of logits or full parameters, and further reduces cost by selectively transmitting only the most informative transformer blocks and choosing public samples aligned with local data. Empirically, it cuts communication overhead by ~50–65% while converging rapidly to competitive perplexity on multiple generative language datasets.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across a wide range of natural language processing tasks. However, conventional fine-tuning typically relies on centralized data collection, bringing in privacy concerns. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative LLM fine-tuning without sharing raw client data, but its deployment over bandwidth-constrained wireless networks is hindered by the communication overhead of model-parameter transmission. Although Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) reduces the number of trainable parameters, its communication cost still increases with model scale. Knowledge distillation avoids parameter sharing via output logits, but token-level logits in LLMs incur high communication cost due to sequence length and vocabulary size. Reducing logits lowers the cost but weakens supervision and degrades accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose FLoKD, an adaptive knowledge-distillation framework for federated LoRA fine-tuning of LLMs over wireless networks, which communicates intermediate LoRA activations as the distillation signal rather than logits or full parameters. Since transmitting all blocks over the entire public dataset remains costly, we further propose a transformer block importance scoring framework that selectively transmits the most informative blocks, and two dataset selection strategies that discard public samples deviating from the local data distribution and prioritise those most informative for distillation. Extensive experiments across multiple generative language datasets, including WikiText-103, PTB, and Dialog, demonstrate that our proposed framework reduces communication overhead by 50-65% while achieving rapid convergence to competitive perplexity compared to baselines.

</details>


### [15] [$τ$-Elicitation: Benchmarking multi-turn entity extraction in voice agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13602)
*Soham Ray,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: τ-Elicitation reveals that strategy choice and recovery—not raw recognition—are the main failures in exact spoken-entity collection; explicit scaffolding markedly improves correctness but increases call time.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: End-to-end voice benchmarks mask where and why exact capture of entities (names, addresses, identifiers, dates, times) fails; need a focussed benchmark to reveal failures in capture, verification, and recovery under realistic voice conditions.

Method: Introduce τ-Elicitation, a controlled 200-task benchmark across 10 entity types, varying difficulty, caller 'realisms', and three environments; compare a matched text agent to four voice-agent configurations, measure robust exact success, verification and repair behaviors, and test a prescriptive scaffold (spelling, read-back, correction, confirmation).

Result: Text agent passes all tasks; four voice configurations only achieve robust exact success in range 0.14–0.41. Agents tend to increase verification for hard/unfamiliar entities and sometimes for incorrect captures, but not for weakest caller voice; only 24–37% of verified errors get repaired. A prescriptive scaffold raises robust Pass^3 by 14–31 points with a 21–28s per-call latency penalty. Spelling variations and restarts have negligible effect; mispronunciation increases repair effort.

Conclusion: Exact spoken-entity collection is bottlenecked not by recognition alone but by strategy selection and recovery; scaffolding that enforces explicit verification and correction substantially improves robust exact capture at the cost of increased interaction time.

Abstract: Voice agents often need to collect names, addresses, identifiers, dates, and times exactly, yet end-to-end benchmarks obscure where capture fails. We introduce $τ$-Elicitation, a 200-task voice benchmark spanning 10 entity types, controlled difficulty, caller realisms, and three environments. A matched text agent passes all tasks, but four voice configurations achieve robust exact success from 0.14 to 0.41. Agents increase verification for hard and unfamiliar entities and sometimes for incorrect captures, but not for their weakest caller voice; only 24 to 37 percent of verified errors are repaired. A scaffold that prescribes spelling, read-back, correction, and confirmation raises robust Pass$^3$ by 14 to 31 points, at a cost of 21 to 28 seconds per call. Realisms such as spelling variations and restarts do not detectably affect exact success; mispronunciation increases repair effort. These results identify strategy selection and successful recovery as the central bottlenecks in exact spoken entity collection.

</details>


### [16] [GeoSkill:Experience-Driven Hierarchical Skill Learning with Collaborative Revision forGeospatialAgents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13667)
*Han Luo,Xian Xu,Yinhe Liu,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoSkill通过分层技能库与协同轨迹修订机制，把历史执行轨迹提炼为可靠、可复用的技能，显著改善地理空间分析智能体的计划与工具使用表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需支持反复且演进的地理空间分析任务中，智能体应把既往执行经验萃取为可复用的程序化知识；现有带记忆的方法难以同时总结长时序的工具链编排与工具层面的调用约束，且直接依赖LLM自我反思更新经验会引发误归因与不可靠修订。

Method: 提出分层技能库（Hierarchical Skill Bank，包含规划技能库和工具技能库）用于分别抽象高层任务规划经验与工具使用约束；引入协同轨迹驱动的技能修订机制（CTSR），由Judge、Critic、Refiner协同完成错误识别、技能级缺陷定位与有针对性的修改；在开发期从历史执行轨迹学习并验证技能，部署期冻结技能库为检索式只读指导。

Result: 在EarthBench与ThinkGeo数据集上的大量实验表明，GeoSkill能有效将历史执行经验转化为分层技能，提升端到端任务准确率与工具执行的可靠性。

Conclusion: GeoSkill将历史执行经验转化为可复用的分层技能库，从而在部署阶段通过检索式指导改进地理空间任务的端到端准确性和工具执行可靠性，避免了直接用LLM自反性更新导致的误归因与不可靠修改。

Abstract: Geospatial agents are increasingly expected to support recurring and evolving analytical tasks rather than execute isolated workflows. In such settings, effective agents must distill prior execution experience into reusable geospatial procedural knowledge to guide future planning and tool use. However, existing memory-augmented paradigms struggle to summarize both long-horizon tool-chain orchestration experience and tool-level invocation constraints in geospatial analysis, while directly relying on LLM self-reflection to update experience often leads to misattribution and unreliable revisions. To address these challenges, we propose GeoSkill, an experience-driven hierarchical skill learning framework for geospatial agents. GeoSkill comprises two core components: (i) a Hierarchical Skill Bank (HSB), consisting of a Planning Skill Bank and a Tool Skill Bank, which respectively distill high-level task-planning experience and tool usage constraints, enabling structured representation and cross-task reuse of historical execution experience; and (ii) a Collaborative Trace-driven Skill Revision (CTSR) mechanism, where Judge, Critic, and Refiner collaboratively perform error identification, skill-level defect localization, and targeted modification, preventing misattributed and unreliable revisions from polluting the skill bank. GeoSkill learns and validates skills from historical executions during development, and freezes the skill bank for retrieval-only guidance on unseen tasks during deployment. Extensive experiments on EarthBench and ThinkGeo demonstrate that GeoSkill effectively transforms historical execution experience into reusable hierarchical skills, improving both end-to-end task accuracy and tool-execution reliability in geospatial tasks.

</details>


### [17] [Enhancing Event Candidate Acquisition for Event Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13670)
*Ziyang Zhang,Yinan Liu,Boyi Xue,Yingxuan Huang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MACE用多专长LLM代理与协调器在候选检索前精炼事件证据，稳健提升事件链接模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选事件获取容易受短而模糊的事件提及、噪声论元和证据对检索作用不均等问题影响，导致检索召回及精确性下降。

Method: 引入多证据专长的LLM代理（时间、地点、参与者、事件类型），每个代理生成中间查询并暴露给候选检索工具；一个协调器汇总并修订各代理产生的证据集合，最终构建候选事件列表供现有链接模型使用。

Result: 在两个事件链接基准上，将MACE作为候选获取模块接入不同的链接模型均带来一致的精度提升，表明MACE通过改善候选获取有效提升链接性能。

Conclusion: MACE通过在候选检索前对事件结构进行多代理证据精炼，显著提升了候选事件获取质量，从而在不改变下游事件链接模型的情况下提高最终链接准确率。

Abstract: Event linking associates event mentions in text with entries in a knowledge base (KB), or identifies them as out-of-KB events. Although existing methods use different architectures, candidate event acquisition can still be weakened by short ambiguous mentions, noisy arguments, and evidence that is unevenly useful for retrieval. We present MACE, a Multi-Agent Candidate Event acquisition method that refines event structure before linking. MACE uses evidence-specialized LLM agents to acquire time, location, participant, and event-type evidence, exposes intermediate queries to candidate-event lookup tools, and lets a coordinator revise the evidence set before final candidate construction. Experiments on two event linking benchmarks show that adding MACE to different event linking models consistently improves accuracy. These results show that MACE improves event linking through better candidate event acquisition without modifying the event linking model.

</details>


### [18] [Root-Cause Attribution Is a Search Problem: Continual Search for Long-Horizon Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13463)
*Harsh Raj,David Lee,Anas Mahmoud,Renxiong Wang,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Chenguang Wang,Tong Zhao,Yunzhong He,Darvin Yi,Vipul Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长时序、证据稀疏的RCA任务，提出Continual Search——一个迭代引导LLM持续搜索证据的方法，并通过新数据集MegaRCA-Mix和多个基准验证，显著提高了根因归因的准确性，且能让较小模型在同族中表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在长时序任务的大规模部署产生海量执行日志，人工查看不可行；现有基于LLM的一次性RCA在长轨迹上表现差，原因是关键信息稀疏、分散且与明显失败信号不直接相连，把根因定位变成了大规模搜索问题。需要一种能持续寻找证据、避免早期“定论”的自动化方法。

Method: 设计一个迭代化的框架（Continual Search），使原本一次性判断的LLM诊断器在多轮交互中不断被“催促”去发现并利用尚未被检视的证据；同时构建并评估在四个既有基准及新引入的MegaRCA-Mix（50条人注失败试验、长时序、执行密集）上的性能证明。

Result: 在多个基准上均有提升。以MegaRCA-Mix为例，Continual Search使GPT-5.5的F1从0.349提升至0.498（>40%相对提升）。在相同模型家族内，采用Continual Search的低阶模型可超越未采用或较弱搜索策略的高阶模型，表明有效搜索策略可胜过单纯模型规模提升。

Conclusion: 提出的Continual Search方法通过对LLM诊断器进行迭代提示，持续引导其在更长、更稀疏的执行轨迹中搜索未决证据，从而显著提升自动根因归因（RCA）的准确度并改善可操作性。该方法在多个基准与新建的大规模数据集MegaRCA-Mix上均带来稳定改进，甚至使低阶模型在同族内超越高阶模型。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of AI agents in long-horizon tasks yields massive execution logs. Diagnosing failures within these records is crucial for reliability, as it transforms outcome-level signals into actionable interventions. The sheer scale of the data renders human review impractical, driving the need for automated root-cause attribution (RCA). However, automated RCA methods using LLMs suffer from low diagnostic accuracy, especially as execution traces grow larger. They struggle because relevant information is often sparse, distributed across distant actions, and disconnected from the visible failure, reducing root-cause attribution to a massive search problem. Existing RCA methods typically rely on one-shot LLM judgments to diagnose failures from execution traces. While effective for shorter trajectories, these judges tend to settle on a plausible diagnosis early, leaving critical evidence in longer traces unexamined. We introduce Continual Search, an iterative framework that nudges the judge, over successive turns, to keep searching for unresolved diagnostic evidence. We evaluate Continual Search across four existing RCA benchmarks. Recognizing the lack of massive execution traces in current benchmarks, we introduce MegaRCA-Mix to evaluate RCA at scale. MegaRCA-Mix provides a challenging testbed of 50 human-annotated failure trials spanning long-horizon, execution-heavy tasks. Across multiple benchmark suites and model families, Continual Search consistently improves attribution performance. On MegaRCA-Mix, for example, it improves GPT-5.5's F1 score by more than 40\%, from $0.349$ to $0.498$. More interestingly, within the same model family, lower-tier models can even surpass their higher-tier counterparts, demonstrating that effective search supersedes raw model scale.

</details>


### [19] [IBBench-Light: A Paired Evaluation of Task-Conditioned Responses to External Directives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13725)
*Kainan Zhou,Gangzhen Qian,Zhaoyi Li,Hang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IBBench-Light introduces paired evaluation (PECA) for execute-vs-process records, finds models’ paired success differs from marginal averages and is highly sensitive to EOS/stop policies, quantization, and minor wording changes; recommends careful reporting of stopping policy and paired metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To measure conditional task and output-contract success when the same record can be used either as an executable procedure or as text to read, and to expose evaluation sensitivities that marginal metrics miss.

Method: Created IBBench-Light: for each external record (apply procedure vs text), test both uses; generate matched pairs across 12 semantic bases (144 pairs) per model, evaluate 4 quantized instruction models (1,152 greedy responses), compute Paired Exact-Contract Accuracy (PECA); augmented dataset with 1,722 CPU generations and additional bases; compared against bounded IHEval using same SmolLM2 checkpoint and budget.

Result: Qwen: 132/144 success on execute prompts, 109/144 on process prompts, but only 97/144 paired successes; Phi rerun showed EOS choice changed paired success from 0/144 to 62/144; demonstrates large sensitivity to stopping policy and decoding details; benchmark highlights need to report stopping policy, paired counts, and task margins together.

Conclusion: IBBench-Light demonstrates that paired exact-contract evaluation (PECA) reveals model fragility and different conclusions than marginal averages; evaluation outcomes are highly sensitive to decoding/stopping (EOS) choices, instruction quantization, and wording changes.

Abstract: An external record may contain a procedure to apply or text to read, depending on the user's request. IBBench-Light tests both uses against the same record. Twelve semantic bases yield 144 matched pairs per model; four quantized instruction models produced 1,152 archived greedy responses. Paired exact-contract accuracy (PECA) requires both members to satisfy their output contracts. Qwen succeeds on 132 execute and 109 process prompts, but only 97 complete pairs, showing what marginal averages omit. We audit literal-target exposure and case normalization, then add 1,722 logged CPU generations to test directive-absent controls, twelve additional semantic bases, within-base wording changes, and generation stopping. In the pinned Phi rerun, changing the end-of-sequence (EOS) set changes exact paired success from 0/144 to 62/144. A bounded IHEval comparison uses the same SmolLM2 checkpoint and output budget while preserving its published instruction roles and scorer. The benchmark measures conditional task and output-contract success. Its task margins and paired count need to be read together with the stopping policy.

</details>


### [20] [AutoTailor: Automatic, User-Aligned Capability Selection and Adaptation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13548)
*Xinyun Cao,Adriana Szekeres,Fazle Elahi Faisal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTailor通过离线过滤与在线动态重选，构建了小而高效的网页自动化API集合，在提升准确率和减少成本/延迟方面取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动发现的工具集合通常规模大、冗余多且与用户需求对齐差；需要一种方法构建和维护小而高效、对用户需求有良好覆盖的API集合，以降低成本与延迟并提升准确性。

Method: 提出AutoTailor：离线阶段将网页轨迹转为参数化的浏览器自动化程序（MCP API），使用质量过滤器剔除粒度不合适或功能冗余的API，并用使用可能性过滤器优先保留高概率有用的能力；在线阶段使用动态重选监控任务结果与API使用，识别覆盖缺口、添加相关候选并剪除持续未使用能力。评估在106个WebArena任务集上进行。

Result: 离线过滤将初始1,283个API缩减到87个，动态重选最终得到33个API。配合ReAct回退策略，该集合达到90.6%的正确率（基线ReAct为87.5%），平均请求令牌成本下降57.8%，延迟降低29.4%；无ReAct时正确率为60.1%，请求令牌使用下降94.9%。

Conclusion: 静态过滤生成了紧凑且高覆盖率的API集合，动态重选进一步根据实际使用进行裁剪，二者结合在提高任务正确率与响应效率的同时显著降低了令牌使用和延迟。

Abstract: Web agents can utilize reusable tools to reduce the cost and latency of low-level browser interaction, but automatically discovered tool collections can be large, redundant, and poorly aligned with user demand. We present AutoTailor, a meta-agentic framework for constructing and maintaining a compact set of trajectory-derived Model Context Protocol (MCP) APIs. Offline, AutoTailor converts web trajectories into parameterized browser-automation programs, applies a Quality Filter to remove APIs with unsuitable granularity and redundant functionality, and applies a Usage Likelihood Filter to prioritize broadly useful capabilities while preserving semantic coverage. Online, Dynamic Reselection monitors task outcomes and API usage, identifies recurring coverage gaps, adds relevant candidates, and prunes persistently unused capabilities. We evaluate AutoTailor on 106 WebArena Postmill tasks. Offline filtering reduces the initial 1,283 unrefined APIs to 87, and Dynamic Reselection produces a 33-API set. With reasoning and acting (ReAct) fallback, this set achieves 90.6% correctness, compared with 87.5% for ReAct alone, while reducing average total request-token cost by 57.8% and latency by 29.4%. Without ReAct, it achieves 60.1% correctness, marginally matching the performance of unrefined set, while reducing request-token usage by 94.9%. Together, these results show that static filtering produces a compact inventory of APIs expected to support core, high-likelihood tasks, while dynamic reselection further tailors that inventory to observed user needs. This combination improves accuracy and latency while sharply reducing token usage and end-to-end cost, demonstrating the value of user-aligned capability management for efficient web agents.

</details>


### [21] [Trustworthy Agentic AI: A Comprehensive Cybersecurity and Systems Survey on Threat Landscapes, Defense Architectures, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13731)
*Seyedakbar Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文综述并结构化地定义了面向自治智能体的攻击面与可信性要求，提出了防御深度架构（含Dual-LLM隔离、能力化访问控制、内核探针与沙箱），并将技术控制与国际治理框架对齐，填补了智能体系统安全的系统化参考空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型从被动推断转向具有持续内循环与外部执行能力的自治智能体，传统安全边界失效；自然语言同时充当输入、控制代码与通信协议，使不受信数据可被执行，带来广泛系统性风险，迫切需要系统化的安全与治理参考架构。

Method: 对206篇基础研究与监管标准进行系统综述；抽象出有状态5元组的智能体架构；提出6维可信性分类（安全、安全性、隐私、可解释性、公平性、问责制）；逐层分析攻击面并设计包括Dual-LLM隔离、基于能力的访问控制、内核eBPF探针与受限运行时的防御架构；审视评价基准并与国际治理框架对接。

Result: 给出（1）可形式化的智能体体系结构抽象，（2）一套6维可信性分类法，（3）对主要攻击面与防御措施的系统化映射，（4）一个多层零信任防御蓝图与对照的评估基准和治理对齐表。

Conclusion: 本文认为将基础模型扩展为自治、目标驱动的智能体带来了新的安全边界，必须通过多层次、零信任的防御架构来管理自然语言作为控制通道所带来的图灵完备风险；提出了一个系统化的可信性框架并映射到治理标准。

Abstract: The transition from passive foundation models to autonomous, goal-directed agentic AI systems has introduced unprecedented capabilities by coupling recursive cognitive reasoning loops, persistent memory architectures, live tool execution planes, and multi-agent collaboration topologies. However, granting probabilistic neural cores execution authority across filesystems, networks, and cloud infrastructure dissolves classical security perimeters: natural language simultaneously serves as input data, internal control code, and communication protocols, exposing a Turing-complete blast radius where untrusted data represents executable instructions. This survey delivers a comprehensive systems-security reference framework for trustworthy agentic AI, synthesizing 206 foundational studies and regulatory standards. We formalize the general agent architecture as a stateful 5-tuple and establish a 6-dimensional trustworthiness taxonomy covering security, safety, privacy, explainability, fairness, and accountability. We systematically analyze threat surfaces across intra-execution loops and interaction planes, formulate a multi-layered zero-trust defense-in-depth architecture integrating Dual-LLM isolation, Capability-Based Access Control, kernel eBPF probes, and sandboxed runtimes, review standardized evaluation benchmarks, and map technical controls to international AI governance frameworks.

</details>


### [22] [Externalizing Requirement-to-Repair Artifacts as Observable Traces for LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14913)
*Zewen Tao,Shin-nosuke Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: THEMIS 通过需求-代码图与阶段化记录，使需求到补丁的转换可审计、可追踪，并在回顾性评估中展示了较高的理据保留率与跨阶段对应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级修复不仅要生成正确补丁，还需要可检查的记录来解释如何将问题需求转化为代码更改以及编辑后证据，以便审计和可追踪性。

Method: 提出了阶段感知的修复工作流（THEMIS），包含语义解释器、运行时需求-代码图、由图派生的开发者引导、保留的修复理据与补丁以及编辑后审计记录，用于捕获并串联各阶段的证据。

Result: 对 300 个 SWE-bench Lite 案例的回顾性审计：288 个案例可得完整开发者理据，214 个（71.3%）保留了连接所选阶段的完整审计字段；目标符号在 62.6% 的开发者理据和 62.8% 的补丁中重复出现，若计入相关符号则升至 75.8%；在一组配对的 100 案例对比中，关系型工作流解决 19 起，对照条件为 9 起，但结果为初步的工作流层面证据而非对图组件的因果证明。

Conclusion: THEMIS 通过将需求到修复的转换过程外部化，使跨阶段的修复决策可检查，从而支持对修复理据和补丁之间对应关系的系统性审查。

Abstract: Repository-level repair requires not only correct patches but also inspectable records that explain how issue requirements are translated into code changes and post-edit evidence. We contribute THEMIS, a stage-aware repair workflow that externalizes this requirement-to-repair process through semantic interpretation, a runtime requirement-code graph, graph-derived Developer guidance, retained repair rationale and patches, and post-edit audit records. A retrospective audit of 300 SWE-bench Lite cases demonstrates that these artifacts provide broad support for cross-stage inspection: a complete Developer rationale is available for 288 cases, and 214 cases (71.3%) retain a complete audited field set connecting the selected stages. The retained records further enable systematic measurement of cross-stage correspondence: target symbols recur in 62.6% of Developer rationales and in 62.8% of patches, rising to 75.8% when related symbols are included. In a paired 100-case comparison, the relational workflow resolves 19 cases versus 9 for the direct same-input condition; because the two conditions also differ in Analyzer output, graph-derived distillation, and Judge records, we report this as preliminary, workflow-level evidence rather than a causal effect of the graph component. Together, these results show that THEMIS makes otherwise implicit requirement-to-repair transitions inspectable, enabling systematic examination of how repair decisions persist, align, and evolve across stages.

</details>


### [23] [How Many Thoughts Can a Vector Hold? The Capacity of Reasoning by Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13747)
*Hongyu Gu,Chang Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定维度的连续潜在表征中，均匀累积历史信息比只保留当前边界更有效，能以更低维度保存更多证据并提高下游决策鲁棒性；对未知未来用途的加权策略中，均匀累积是理论上最优的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续/递归潜在表征把多步推理的中间计算压缩到固定维度中，但设计上存在根本性问题：在推理过程中应当保留哪些信息？直觉上丢弃历史只保留当前“边界”似乎节省容量，但作者怀疑并证明该直觉在某些情形下错误。另一个实际问题是，当未来使用未知时，如何对潜在记忆加权以便能鲁棒复用？

Method: 理论分析与证明：构建对比模型，比较在相同下游映射下不同超位策略（frontier-only 与 cumulative）所需的表征维数；分析信息成分的相干强化与非相关备选项带来的随机干扰；在可重用加权超位的一般类中证明均匀累积的极小极大最优性；并借由定量分析说明在固定隐藏宽度下的信息保留与注意瓶颈效应。

Result: 证明并展示：1) 累积超位（保留完整历史的叠加）在相同下游映射下可用更低维度实现等价或更好的推理性能；2) 信息性的历史分量相互相干增强，而无关备选项带来随机干扰，这导致累积比只保留边界更易区分有效证据；3) 在可重用加权家族中，优先少数最近或显著项会产生弱表示并成为后续注意的瓶颈，而均匀累积避免此缺陷且是minimax最优；4) 从设计角度，均衡的累积记忆使固定表征预算能支持更可靠、可复用的计算。

Conclusion: 累计的均匀超位（cumulative uniform superposition）在相同下游计算条件下，比只保存当前思路边界（frontier-only）更能有效利用固定维度的潜在状态：它能以更低的表征维度保留更多有效证据、区分更多合理的下游结果，并延迟压缩导致的不可靠性。对于未来用途未知的可重用记忆，加权方案中均匀累积是极小极大（minimax）最优解。

Abstract: Large language models solve hard problems through intermediate computations across multi-step reasoning. Traditional chain-of-thought encodes these computations as tokens. Recent continuous and recurrent methods instead move partial computations into fixed-dimensional latent states, where a single thought can superpose multiple alternatives. This raises a fundamental design question:what should continuous thoughts preserve as reasoning proceeds? An intuitive approach discards past computations and keeps only the current reasoning frontier. Storing more items seems to dilute states and waste limited representational capacity. We show this intuition can be incorrect. Under identical downstream computations, cumulative superposition retaining full reasoning history can require lower representational dimensions than frontier-only superposition holding only current alternatives. At fixed hidden width, this advantage allows latent reasoners to retain more valid evidence, distinguish more plausible downstream outcomes, and delay the point where compressed states turn unreliable. This counter-intuitive effect emerges because informative historical components coherently reinforce each other, while unrelated alternatives bring random interference. This perspective also answers a practical design question: how should models weight memories accumulated inside latent states when their future use is unknown? Across reusable weighted superpositions, prioritizing a small set of recent or salient items produces weakly-represented memories that bottleneck subsequent attention. Uniform cumulative weighting avoids this flaw, and we prove it is minimax-optimal for robust future reasoning. Our results turn superposition from an observed latent-space effect into a design principle: balanced cumulative memory lets a fixed representational budget support more reliable, reusable computations.

</details>


### [24] [OpenAl4S: Code as Action, Science as Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15096)
*Gongbo Zhang,Hao Li,Yu Wang,Mujie Lin,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yicheng Mao,Yimi Wang,Jun Zhu,Minhan Tang,Zhengxiang Jiang,Yusong Wang,Jiayu Yao,Kunpeng Ning,Dawei Pang,Yonghong Tian,OpenAI4S Community,Yuyang Liu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个持久化执行+会话级可溯源的科研代理，实证表明该设计能提升 AI 辅助科研的可靠性，尤其在长时程与高算力任务中；但可重现性（环境说明与完全重跑）仍需进一步工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 助理能加速计算科研，但长期实验需要可检查、可恢复且可重现的工作流，这要求持久化计算状态和完整的由来（provenance）记录。

Method: 设计并实现了一个以“代码即操作、科学即会话”为原则的开源科研代理：持久化 Python/R 内核执行代码单元、通过结构化工具调用进行编排、记录追加式 Action Ledger、每单元执行记录、版本化产物、环境记录与工作区检查点以保存产生结果的过程，并提供可配置的沙箱、权限与代码轨迹筛查。基于 36 个科研场景（逆合成、分子动力学、蛋白设计等）对科学任务准确性、工作流完整性与产出仓库的可重现性进行评估，对比通用编码工具链与三种前沿模型。

Result: OpenAI4S 在 36 项场景上取得总体得分 7.83（对比基线 5.7–6.4），在长时程和计算密集型工作流上增益最大；但所有系统在环境规范与完全重跑能力上表现欠佳。代码以 MIT 协议开源于 github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S。

Conclusion: 将持久化执行环境与会话级可溯源集成，能显著提高 AI 辅助科研工作流的可靠性；OpenAI4S 在长期与高计算量任务上表现出明显优势，但环境说明与完全可重跑性仍是未解决的问题。

Abstract: AI co-scientists could accelerate computational research, but over a long-running study the workflow also has to stay inspectable, resumable and reproducible, which requires persistent computational state and provenance. Here we present OpenAI4S, an open-source scientific research agent built around the principle of \emph{Code as Action, Science as Sessions}. OpenAI4S combines a persistent computing runtime with research-session management: orchestration is handled through structured tool calls, while scientific actions are represented as complete code cells executed in persistent Python and R kernels. An append-only Action Ledger, per-cell execution records, versioned artifacts, environment records, and workspace checkpoints preserve how results were produced and support session recovery, branching, and extension. Configurable sandboxing, permission controls, and code and trajectory screening provide complementary safeguards. We evaluate OpenAI4S on 36 research scenarios spanning retrosynthesis, molecular dynamics, protein binder design, protein mutation, catalyst screening, and mineral spectroscopy, measuring scientific task accuracy, workflow completeness, and reproducibility of the resulting repositories. OpenAI4S achieves an overall score of 7.83, compared with 5.7--6.4 for a general-purpose coding harness evaluated with three frontier models, with the largest gains on long-horizon and computation-intensive workflows. These results suggest that integrating persistent execution with session-level provenance can improve the reliability of AI-assisted scientific workflows. Environment specification and full rerunnability remain weak for every evaluated system, ours included, so reproducibility is still an open problem for scientific agents. The system is available under the MIT license at \href{https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S}{github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S}.

</details>


### [25] [Positioning manuscripts in the scientific landscape with agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13760)
*Jiawen Chen,Zichen Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Quan Sun,Yiyan Zhang,Edric Tam,Jinjie Lin,Didong Li,Yun Li,Bingxin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An agentic, retrieval-augmented LLM system (PASS) effectively predicts likely publication venues and manuscript quality signals by placing manuscripts within their literature context; it outperforms baselines on a 2k preprint biomedical benchmark and is released publicly.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To reduce the time, stress, and uncertainty in manuscript publication by giving researchers automated, literature-aware recommendations of likely publication venues and quality signals derived from manuscript content and surrounding literature.

Method: PASS combines a literature retrieval module that reconstructs a manuscript's local scientific neighborhood, a topic-trajectory reconstruction component, and reasoning over field-specific journal spaces using agentic LLM workflows to score candidate venues and generate quality signals (impact potential, novelty). Evaluations used a leakage-audited benchmark of >2,000 biomedical preprints across 16 fields.

Result: On the benchmark, PASS achieved Top-1 accuracy 50.3% and Top-5 accuracy 86.1%, outperforming state-of-the-art LLM baselines and established tools. The retrieval module was the largest contributor; PASS performed near-fully from abstracts alone. Human evaluation showed strong researcher agreement. A public platform (ratemypaper.ai) was released.

Conclusion: PASS demonstrates that an agentic LLM-based system can reliably position manuscripts within their literature context and predict publication venues, achieving substantial gains over existing LLM baselines and journal-selection tools.

Abstract: Publishing a research manuscript is a routine yet demanding part of scientific life: time-consuming, stressful, and often uncertain in outcome. Recent advances in large language model (LLM)-based agentic AI have shown promise across a range of scientific tasks, and here we ask whether agentic AI can help researchers navigate the publication process itself by reliably inferring a manuscript's eventual publication venue from its content and literature context. We introduce PASS (Publication-oriented Agentic Scientific System), an agentic system that understands manuscripts within their domain-specific literature context and predicts top-matched publication venues. PASS positions each manuscript within its surrounding literature landscape by reconstructing its local scientific neighborhood, tracing its topic trajectory, and reasoning over field-specific journal spaces. Evaluated on a leakage-audited benchmark of over 2,000 preprints across 16 biomedical fields, PASS achieved Top-1 accuracy of 50.3% and Top-5 accuracy of 86.1%, outperforming state-of-the-art LLM baselines and established journal-selection tools. PASS-produced quality scores, such as impact potential and novelty, aligned with independent measures of publication outcome. We also found that the designed literature retrieval module is the strongest performance contributor, particularly for positioning manuscripts relative to nearby work, and that PASS maintained near-full performance from the abstract alone, whereas LLM baselines required the full manuscript text. An independent human evaluation found strong researcher agreement with PASS's manuscript understanding and recommendation rationale. PASS has been released as a public platform (https://ratemypaper.ai/) for broad researcher access.

</details>


### [26] [Partition Scores Are Not System Scores: Deployment-Fidelity Gaps in Decomposed Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13785)
*Jiachen Zhang,Yu Tang,Li Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 定义了部署保真差 G(R)，推导出边际后悔稳定性与分区识别区间，并在五个基准上验证：分区报告普遍高估可部署性能，建议并列报告分区与端到端分数并使用端到端评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献常用 Oracle 式量（如虚拟最佳解、virtual-best等）作为对可部署选择器性能的上界，但在分解化算法选择情形中，分区阶段使用的“家族内最佳算法”是不可直接部署的，必须由学习的家族内选择器替代。需要量化这两者之间的差距并给出何时分区级选择能可靠反映可部署结果的判据。

Method: 从形式化定义出发，推导出部署保真差 G(R) 及其两个推论（边际后悔稳定性条件与分区识别区间）。在五个公开算法选择基准（涵盖表格化 AutoML 与组合 CSP/SAT）上进行实证评估：计算每个分解化流水线的 G(R)、比较分区级与端到端的性能差异、识别符号变化的案例，并提出一个训练端的验证差校正诊断用于恢复可部署符号估计。

Result: 在五个基准上，所有分解化流水线的 G(R) 均为正（从 TabZilla 的 0.012 到 PROTEUS-2014 的 0.13）。在 10 个分解/扁平化决策中有 4 个点估计符号发生变化；例如 PROTEUS-2014 上原先 33 点的分区优势缩小为 20 点的端到端优势。所提的训练端验证差校正诊断在这 4 个符号变化单元上都能恢复点估计的可部署符号（但作者强调这只是报告辅助，不能替代直接端到端评估）。

Conclusion: 本文界定并量化了分区级（partition-level）评分与可部署端到端评分之间的差距，命名为部署保真差 G(R)。作者证明该差距通常为正，并给出两个理论后果：一个每实例的边际后悔（margin-regret）稳定性条件（用于判定分区时的家族选择是否在部署上最优），以及一个严谨的仅基于分区的识别区间（当跨越零时表明分区报告无法保证可部署获胜者）。作者还主张在报告中并列展示分区与端到端分数。

Abstract: Oracle-style quantities, including virtual best solvers, selected-portfolio VBS, virtual-best encodings, and best-in-family summaries, are widely reported as upper bounds on what a deployable selector could achieve. In decomposed algorithm selection, an analogous partition-level score grants an oracle choice of the best algorithm within the selected family; once the family selector is fixed, the deployable system must replace that within-family oracle with a learned within-family selector.
  We define the deployment-fidelity gap G(R) as the difference between partition-level and deployable end-to-end utility and derive two accounting consequences: a per-instance margin-regret stability condition that tells us when a partition-time family choice is deployment-optimal, and a sharp partition-only identification interval that, when it strictly crosses zero, prevents the partition-level report from certifying the deployable winner.
  Across five public algorithm-selection benchmarks spanning tabular AutoML and combinatorial CSP/SAT, every decomposed pipeline has positive G(R), ranging from 0.012 on TabZilla to 0.13 on PROTEUS-2014. Four of ten decomposed-versus-flat decisions have sign-changing point estimates; on PROTEUS-2014, a 33-point partition advantage shrinks to a 20-point end-to-end advantage. A training-side validation gap-correction diagnostic recovers the point-estimate deployable sign on all four sign-changing cells; it is a reporting aid, not a substitute for direct end-to-end evaluation. Partition and end-to-end scores should be reported side by side.

</details>


### [27] [Do Not Restart: Residual Completion for Stateful Agent Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13800)
*Runzhi Deng,Yiming Zhong,Fang Zhao,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CFRC 提出并实现了一种基于“承诺-证据”机制的残余完成方法，能在大幅降低推理成本的同时保持任务正确性与义务清偿的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用路由与级联以降低工具或代理成本时，状态化的模型间交接必须保持先前已接受的选择、已实现的效果以及未完成的义务，否则会导致一致性、可追溯性和安全性问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为“承诺约束的残余完成”（commitment-constrained residual completion），设计 Commitment-Frontier Residual Completion (CFRC) 框架；通过三条规则（target-before-proposal、whole-proposal-before-authority、live-evidence-before-success）维护状态化交接：冻结已接受的残余契约，将后续续体封闭为证据关联图，仅在剩余部分被覆盖并有活跃收据清偿义务时允许执行。并证明了契约相对的部分正确性，及在完全构造契约下向原始残余请求扩展的正确性。

Result: 在五个环境与两组同供应商模型对上，CFRC 在宏观准确率上可与强大的全任务代理媲美，但仅耗费其推理成本的22.0%–34.6%；并在跨供应商设置中展示了更广的迁移性。

Conclusion: CFRC 在保证接受选择、实现效果和未完结义务不被破坏的前提下，能以明显更低的推理成本完成任务分配与模型间路由，从而在实际场景中实现成本-准确率的有利折衷。

Abstract: Routing and cascades reduce tool-agent cost by transferring control across models, but stateful handoffs must preserve accepted choices, realized effects, and unfinished obligations. We formulate this as commitment-constrained residual completion and introduce Commitment-Frontier Residual Completion (CFRC). CFRC enforces target-before-proposal, whole-proposal-before-authority, and live-evidence-before-success: it freezes a residual contract from accepted progress, closes the successor continuation into an evidence-linked graph, and admits execution only when the remainder is covered, with live receipts discharging obligations. We establish contract-relative partial correctness, which extends to the original residual request under complete contract construction. Across five environments and two same-provider model pairs, CFRC achieves comparable macro accuracy to strong full-task agents at only 22.0%-34.6% of their inference cost, with additional cross-provider results demonstrating broader transfer.

</details>


### [28] [Bypass Observation: A Conceptual Design of a Non-Intrusive Layer-Wise Semantic Extraction Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13807)
*Haibin Tong,Jiang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“Bypass Observation”——在Transformer层外挂只读观察头以读出隐藏态（不回馈主干计算），分析了实现变体、计算开销近似及降低策略，并讨论了可解释性和在迭代模型中的应用，但尚缺系统实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的推理发生在高维隐藏态空间，但用户只能看到最终输出——希望增加对中间计算的可观测性以便调试、监控、理解或作为训练信号。

Method: 设计并分析三种只读出头变体：跨层共享的LM head、层特异性heads、以及层/步自适应head；为全词表读出推导出开销近似解析式主要受V/(12d控制；讨论多种降低开销的策略（稀疏读出、低秩分解、缩小词表、top-k读出、选择位置等）。该工作以概念与解析为主，未做系统性实证。

Result: 给出可观测化方案及其计算开销的解析近似（代表估计约30%—240%），阐明其对链式思维与循环/迭代Transformer的影响，并指出该方法的优势与局限（部分、可能误导性的投影；可在推理时保持因果外性但可用于RL训练）。生成了应用场景和减少开销的工程对策。

Conclusion: 提出了一种非侵入性的层级只读观测架构“Bypass Observation”，通过在选定Transformer层附加只读观测头来提高对隐藏态计算过程的可观测性，同时不将读出返回到主计算路径。作者认为这种方法能提高可观测性但仍是隐藏态的部分投影且可能产生误导。

Abstract: Large language models reason in high-dimensional hidden-state spaces, while users observe only final outputs. We introduce Bypass Observation, a non-intrusive layer-wise readout architecture that attaches read-only observation heads to selected Transformer layers without feeding their outputs back into the backbone. We consider three variants: a shared LM head across layers, layer-specific heads, and a layer- or step-adaptive head. For full-vocabulary readout, we derive a closed-form overhead approximation governed primarily by V/(12d), with representative estimates ranging from about 30% to 240%, and discuss cost reductions via sparse observation, low-rank factorization, reduced vocabularies, top-k readout, and selective positions. We argue that Bypass Observation can make model computation more observable while remaining only a partial, potentially misleading projection of hidden states. We further distinguish bypass chain-of-thought from conventional chain-of-thought: conventional reasoning tokens enter the autoregressive computation, whereas bypass readouts remain causally external at inference time, although they can still provide training signals in reinforcement learning. Finally, we discuss applications to looped and recurrent-depth Transformers, where iteration-wise readout may expose convergence, oscillation, and potential halting signals. The proposal is conceptual and analytical; systematic empirical validation remains future work.

</details>


### [29] [ViperQ: Order Flow Pattern Recognition via Auction Market Theory for Reinforcement Learning Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13825)
*Asser Moustafa,Rares-Mihail Neagu,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用20维基于拍卖市场理论的特征训练PPO（带前景理论非对称奖励）的强化学习交易系统，在12个月未见逐笔数据上对TSLA/NVDA报告高ROI；结论是AMT特征对序列决策有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是弥合学术强化学习在金融中常用的价格聚合（OHLCV）或LOB深度表征与实务中关于市场微观结构的理论（AMT/Market Profile），为这些实务理论提供同行评审级的计算实现并验证其可行性。

Method: 论文方法：从成交薄/逐笔数据显式构造20维Z-标准化向量（包含Volume Point of Control、Value Area位置、Low Volume Node标志、Cumulative Volume Delta偏离、tape-velocity签名等AMT原语）；用具有前景理论偏差的非对称奖励函数（按照Kahneman & Tversky的损失规避系数惩罚亏损持仓）训练两个PPO智能体；在未见的12个月拆分上评估表现。

Result: 结果声明：在无杠杆情况下、对未见的12个月机构逐笔数据评估，ViperQ在TSLA上实现+163.6% ROI（最大回撤-27.5%，交易27019次），在NVDA上实现+116.5% ROI（最大回撤-47.8%，交易12892次）。论文据此提出AMT特征是可行的结构化输入并建议在微观结构感知的策略学习上继续研究。

Conclusion: 论文结论为：基于拍卖市场理论（Auction Market Theory，AMT）提取的微观结构特征可以作为强化学习的有效输入，训练出的ViperQ系统在未见的12个月机构逐笔成交数据上对TSLA和NVDA取得高幅度正收益，证明AMT特征在序列决策问题中具有可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning trading systems published in the academic literature overwhelmingly rely on price-aggregate state representations (OHLCV bars) or limit-order-book depth features, leaving microstructure pattern theories from the practitioner literature, namely Auction Market Theory and Market Profile, without a peer-reviewed computational instantiation. We present ViperQ, a reinforcement learning system whose state representation is built explicitly from Auction Market Theory primitives: Volume Point of Control, Value Area position, Low Volume Node flags, Cumulative Volume Delta divergence, and tape-velocity signatures, assembled into a 20-dimensional Z-normalised vector. Two Proximal Policy Optimisation agents are trained with a prospect theory-grounded asymmetric reward function that penalises losing holds at a magnitude consistent with Kahneman and Tversky's loss-aversion coefficient. Evaluated on a held-out twelve-month partition of institutional tick data the agents have never seen, ViperQ achieves +163.6% ROI on TSLA (-27.5% max drawdown, 27,019 trades) and +116.5% ROI on NVDA (-47.8% max drawdown, 12,892 trades) under zero leverage. The results establish Auction Market Theory features as a tractable structured input modality for sequential decision-making on financial time series and motivate further work on microstructure-aware policy learning.

</details>


### [30] [UniCAR-RL: Seeing Better before Thinking Deeper in Visual Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13849)
*Yuzhe Li,Hao Yan,Hao Wang,Xingchen Liu,Ya-Qi Yu,Jihao Wu,Minghui Liao,Wei Chen,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个无标注的三分支RL框架UniCAR-RL，通过解耦感知与推理训练，利用Caption-RL、Reasoning-RL与QA-RL协同优化，有效减少视觉幻觉并提升数学视觉推理性能，且具有跨架构与跨规模的良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在复杂数学视觉推理任务中常因感知粒度不足出现初始视觉幻觉，进而导致级联推理失败。传统端到端RL的稀疏奖励无法将感知错误与逻辑错误区分，难以定向优化感知模块；直接用感知增强的CoT微调成本高且可能引入新的幻觉。需要一种不依赖标注、能针对性优化感知与推理的训练策略。

Method: 提出一种无标注（annotation-free）的强化学习框架UniCAR-RL，包含三条协同训练分支：1) Caption-RL：在验证器（verifier）引导下优化图像描述以增强感知能力；2) Reasoning-RL：基于金标准图像描述（gold description）进行纯逻辑推理训练，以阻断由感知错误引发的级联推理失误；3) QA-RL：保留端到端对齐以维持问答性能。该框架通过解耦感知与推理在训练阶段独立优化两类能力，并利用短答原始数据进行强化学习训练，避免大规模人工标注。

Result: 在仅使用原始短答数据的情况下，UniCAR-RL显著提升了MLLM在数学与视觉推理任务上的表现，并在不同模型架构与规模上展示出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniCAR-RL通过显式分离感知与推理的优化，在无标注环境下有效减少视觉幻觉并提升数学视觉推理性能，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with complex mathematical visual reasoning primarily due to a lack of fine-grained perception, causing initial visual hallucinations to directly trigger cascading reasoning failures. In traditional end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL), sparse rewards fail to decouple perceptual hallucinations from logical missteps, hindering targeted perception optimization. Alternatively, fine-tuning with perception-enhanced CoT data incurs high costs and hallucinations. In this paper, we address these challenges by proposing UniCAR-RL, an annotation-free RL framework. By explicitly decoupling the optimization of perception and reasoning during the training process, it achieves isolation and optimization of both capabilities. Specifically, UniCAR-RL consists of three synergistic branches: 1) a Caption-RL branch that optimizes perception capabilities through verifier-guided reasoning validation; 2) a Reasoning-RL branch that performs logical reasoning based on a gold image description to halt cascading errors; 3) a QA-RL branch that retains native end-to-end alignment to ensure robust question-answering performance. Experiments show that UniCAR-RL substantially improves MLLMs' mathematical and visual reasoning using only raw short-answer data. Furthermore, it demonstrates strong generalization across diverse architectures and scales.

</details>


### [31] [Map Users and Mapmakers: The Scope of Cognitive Attribution from Acquired Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13879)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个五项归因框架，精确划定何时可将认知能力归因给获得表征的系统，并指出从应用到真正获取准则存在不可跨越的证成缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清在把表征归因给系统时的边界，避免因标签或表面表现而对系统隐含移入更广泛的认知能力；回应机器概念与“世界模型”解释的争论并减少模糊归因。

Method: 通过概念框架构建与形式小证明结合：提出五部分归因表（有效追踪、已获得结构的应用、从明确说明的获得、从识别性观察的获得、保持与重用），并用地图/范畴案例、Andrew Ng 对 Othello-GPT 的世界模型解读及当代机器概念指标作具体应用与检验。

Result: 给出一套可操作的五项归因规范：每项包含一个正向能力承诺和一个需要额外证据的附加主张；证明了完整的应用能力仍不能决定获取能力，区分“从说明处获得准则”与“从示例中发现准则”；保存了对识别、分类、推理与有条件获得的归功，同时明确了准则发现与积累的未证成之处。
