However, these designs restrict agent autonomy: Worker agents cannot directly consult specific "peers", and misrouted messages can propagate errors. Inspired by bus architectures in computer systems, we propose BusMA, a communication framework that allows any agent to address other agents through a shared channel, i.e., the Bus. It consists of agent registration, message routing, and shared memory management components. Worker agents, each equipped with tools, have their own local memory and can reason, act (tool usage), and communicate by posting shared messages with specific intents. We introduce four intents: discussion, challenge, guidance, and request for explanation, which support fine-grained communication among agents. A Chair agent monitors the shared memory to coordinate interactions and facilitate convergence among Workers. To evaluate the effectiveness of BusMA, we conduct extensive experiments with two frontier LLMs across 13 tasks spanning visual reasoning, mathematical reasoning, and knowledge retrieval demonstrate that BusMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HMW and RMP methods.

</details>


### [64] [Enabling Creative Exploration for Vibe Design Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15078)
*Yifan Zhang,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Georgios Evangelopoulos,Arnaud Benard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在生成前先显式采样结构化设计规范并固定下游生成设置，能够在不牺牲实现一致性的情况下扩展UI概念探索，但对最终代码导出效果的提升存在统计不确定性，且伴随行为与成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过提高token-level温度来增加多样性的做法过于粗糙，会同时影响美学决策与语法敏感的代码实现；需要一种能将“探索设计方向”与“实现细节”分离的更可控方法。

Method: 提出一种推理架构：先做一轮“语言化采样（Verbalized Sampling）”的预处理，生成带典型性分数的结构化设计规范；用外部选择器按采样策略选取一个规范；下游生成器在固定设置下根据所选规范和原始请求渲染界面并生成前端代码。

Result: 在168个提示上进行的离线测试（每个干预对每个温度有1,255次配对比较）显示：主题采样提高了被选择的覆盖率和截图变化；LLM评分偏好随干预、提示复杂度和视口变化；在线实验（>300,000任务）显示代码导出增加不显著，但负面反馈减少、修正交互增加且运行成本适中。

Conclusion: 将设计方向显式化为中间结构化规格，可以在保持下游生成设定不变的情况下，有效扩大可探索的界面概念空间。

Abstract: Vibe design agents turn natural-language briefs into rendered interfaces and frontend code. Yet a useful design agent should do more than produce one valid page: it should help users explore coherent alternatives. Increasing token-level temperature is a blunt solution because it varies aesthetic decisions and syntax-sensitive code at the same time. We instead separate exploration from implementation through an inference architecture that makes design direction an explicit intermediate decision. Inspired by Verbalized Sampling, a pre-pass proposes structured design specifications with typicality scores, an external selector samples one, and the downstream generator realizes the selected specification together with the original request under fixed settings. We apply this approach to UI themes and visual-asset prompts. Across 168 prompts, with 1,255 paired comparisons per temperature for each intervention, theme sampling broadens observed selection coverage and screenshot variation, while LLM-judge preferences vary across interventions, prompt complexity, and viewport. In an online experiment with more than 300,000 tasks, the observed code-export increase remains statistically uncertain, while fewer negative feedback events coexist with more correction interactions and modest operational costs. Together, these findings identify structured design specifications as a practical control point for exploring alternative UI concepts while keeping downstream generation settings fixed.

</details>


### [65] [ER-EDF: A Psychology-Grounded Emotion Regulation Framework for Speech Empathetic Dialogue Generation in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15089)
*Hongyu Jin,Wenda Zhang,Runqiu Fei,Gongping Huang,Mike Conway,Ting Dang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ER-EDF通过将情感感知与情感调节解耦并引入评估与数据集，显著提升了口语共情对话系统的回复质量，提出了对共情生成更心理学一致的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大音频-语言模型多将情感作为直接条件信号，导致简单的情感镜像而非经过调节的支持性回应；基于Perception-Action Model和情绪调节理论，真正的共情需要区分“察觉他人情绪”与“如何表达/调节自身回应”的过程。

Method: 提出心理学驱动的ER-EDF框架（Emotion Regulation - Emotion Decoupling Framework），在现有LALMs之上引入独立的感知模块（追踪用户情感状态）与调节模块（决定如何基于该状态生成符合目标支持程度的回应）；构建口语共情对话数据集、设计超越词汇匹配的共情感知评估指标，并在五个LALM和两个数据集上进行自动与人工评测。

Result: 在五个模型与两个数据集上的实验显示，ER-EDF在自动指标与人工评估上均持续改善共情回复质量，减轻了单纯情感镜像问题，证明了框架的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: ER-EDF通过在大音频-语言模型中显式解耦情感感知与情感调节，能够在口语共情对话中产生更合适的情感表达，显著提升生成回复的共情质量，并强调在共情系统中同时建模“感知”与“调节”的必要性。

Abstract: Empathetic response generation in spoken dialogue systems requires both accurate emotion perception and appropriate emotion regulation. Grounded in psychological theories such as the Perception-Action Model and emotion regulation theory, effective empathy depends not only on inferring a user's affective state but also on regulating how it is expressed in responses. However, recent large audio-language models (LALMs) largely treat emotion as a direct conditioning signal, lacking explicit regulatory mechanisms, which often leads to affect mirroring rather than calibrated support. We propose ER-EDF, a psychology-grounded framework that explicitly decouples emotion perception and emotion regulation in LALMs. Perception tracks the user's emotional state, while regulation determines how this state should guide empathetic response generation. The framework is model-agnostic and integrates seamlessly into existing LALMs. We further construct a spoken empathetic dialogue dataset and introduce empathy-aware evaluation metrics beyond lexical matching. Experiments across five LALMs and two datasets show that ER-EDF consistently improves empathetic response quality in both automatic and human evaluations, highlighting the importance of jointly modeling emotion perception and regulation in spoken empathetic dialogue systems, paving a new direction for psychologically grounded empathetic AI.

</details>


### [66] [Medical Knowledge Simplification for Patients in the Era of LLMs: A Case Study on Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15129)
*Pallika Kafle,Yipeng Zhou,Guanfeng Liu,Quan Z. Sheng,Cheng-Hsin Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MediClear（LLM+RAG）在小规模案例研究中能将糖尿病信息简化到患者可接受的阅读水平并获得正面用户反馈，但需更大样本、多维评估和严格对照试验以确认其实用性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂医学信息难以被普通患者理解，需有效的知识简化以改善患者理解、决策与健康结局；尽管 LLM 进展迅速，但其在真实患者教育场景中的效果尚缺乏人类评估证据。

Method: 构建以 Diabetes Australia、WHO、ADA、NIDDK、AIHW 等公开糖尿病资料为索引的 RAG 知识库；检索临床依据后由 LLM 将信息简化为对患者友好的解释；使用 FKGL 等可读性指标和 10 名参与者的人类评估进行验证。

Result: 生成文本的 FKGL 明显下降到推荐患者读写水平范围；参与者反馈满意度高并愿意未来使用。但研究受限于样本量小、评价指标单一、只针对糖尿病领域等局限。

Conclusion: MediClear 将 LLM 与 RAG 结合用于糖尿病知识简化，能显著降低阅读难度至推荐的患者识字水平区间，并在小规模人类研究中获得高满意度与未来使用意愿。

Abstract: Complex medical information is often difficult for patients to understand, making effective medical knowledge simplification essential for improving patient comprehension, informed decision-making, and health outcomes. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) provide a promising approach for simplifying complex medical information into patient-friendly language; however, their effectiveness in real-world patient education remains insufficiently explored through human evaluation. To investigate their practical effectiveness, this paper presents a case study on diabetes knowledge simplification through the implementation and evaluation of MediClear, an LLM-based medical knowledge simplification system enhanced with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Public diabetes-related articles from Diabetes Australia, WHO, American Diabetes Association (ADA), NIDDK, and AIHW are indexed in the RAG knowledge base to retrieve clinically grounded information, which is then simplified by the LLM into accessible patient explanations. We evaluate the generated responses using standard readability metrics, including the Flesch-Kincaid Grade Level (FKGL), and conduct a human study involving 10 participants. Results show that MediClear consistently reduces the reading level of generated responses to the recommended patient literacy range while achieving high user satisfaction and willingness for future use. This case study demonstrates the potential of LLMs to improve the accessibility of medical knowledge for patient education.

</details>


### [67] [T-LoopFormer: Token-Level Elastic-Depth Looped Transformers for Latent Reasoning With Dynamic Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15160)
*Mingqian Yu,Wenpeng Zhang,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过按token动态决定循环次数并配合每轮独立KV缓存，T-LoopFormer在效率与性能间取得更优折衷，尤其在自回归解码场景显著降低冗余计算与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定的统一递归深度导致不同难度token被同等处理，造成计算浪费；同时希望在保持latent reasoning优势（较少token数）下，进一步提高推理效率与延迟表现。

Method: 提出token-choice路由器，让每个token基于其隐藏态动态决定继续递归还是早退出；并设计recursion-wise KV cache，为每一轮递归维护独立的KV缓存，从而在自回归解码中避免已退出token的冗余计算。

Result: 作者实现的T-LoopFormer在相同参数量下于PPL与10个零样本推理任务上达成SOTA，模型在24x FLOPs条件下仍优于基线并实现最低推理延迟，同时公开了代码。

Conclusion: 引入动态的按token选择循环次数的路由与按循环维度的KV缓存，可显著提升Looped Transformer的计算与样本效率，在保持或超越基线性能的同时降低推理FLOPs与延迟。

Abstract: Looped Transformers have recently demonstrated strong performance in both reasoning and language tasks by reusing a shared set of parameters across multiple iterations, achieving parameter efficiency without sacrificing representational power. Besides, looped Transformers perform inference directly in the latent space (latent reasoning) to reduce the number of tokens consumed during inference, thereby achieving improved sample efficiency. However, these models typically apply a fixed recursion depth uniformly to every token, leading to suboptimal compute allocation and leaving significant efficiency gains on the table. In this work, we propose \textbf{dynamic token-choice routing} for looped transformers, enabling each token to adaptively determine its own number of loop iterations based on its hidden state. We use a dynamic router to decide whether a token should continue recursing or exit early, allowing simple tokens to bypass unnecessary computation while hard tokens receive deeper processing. To ensure that this adaptive mechanism does not compromise decoding efficiency, we further introduce recursion-wise KV caching, which maintains an independent key-value cache for each recursion loop. This design ensures that tokens at different depths only attend to their corresponding cached states, effectively eliminating redundant computations for exited tokens and enabling fast autoregressive decoding. Extensive experiments show that T-LoopFormer reaches the sota performance under the same parameters on PPL and 10 zero-shot reasoning tasks, even surpassing the base model at 24x FLOPs and our model could reach the lowest inference latency, which validate the effectiveness of token-choice router and recursion-wise KV cache. Code: https://github.com/YuMingQian1234/T-LoopFormer.

</details>


### [68] [STHMoE: Hypergraph-Enhanced Heterogeneous Dependency Coordination for LLM-Based Urban Traffic Data Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15172)
*Jiawen Chen,Qi Shao,Yongjian Chang,Mingtong Zhou,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STHMoE通过提示驱动的异构专家、超图模块与熵感知MoE路由器，协同建模频域/时域/一阶/高阶空间依赖，能更好地应对交通数据的异构性与结构演化，在多项基准上取得竞争性效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多以时间维为中心，缺乏对频谱、成对空间和高阶结构提示的统一协调；城市交通信号具有异构、非平稳及结构动态变化，需要一种能同时捕捉多尺度、多模态与演化关系的框架。

Method: 提出一种Spatio-Temporal Hypergraph-Enhanced Mixture of Experts（STHMoE）框架：
- 将输入分解为频域、时域、一阶空间和高阶空间四类表示；
- 为每类表示设计提示引导的异构专家，前三类使用领域统计提示，第四类使用结构占位提示并通过自适应超图模块学习高阶关系；
- 在部分冻结的LLM骨干上构建专家以利用预训练能力；
- 联合学习一阶图依赖与无预设拓扑的高阶群交互；
- 使用熵感知的MoE路由器并结合变异系数（coefficient-of-variation）负载平衡以自适应融合专家输出并提高路由置信与专家利用率。

Result: 在10个真实交通数据集上与时间方法、时空图方法和LLM相关基线比较，STHMoE展现出竞争性甚至优越的预测性能；同时路由策略与负载平衡提升了专家利用率与路由置信度，证明了多专家+超图建模对捕捉演化空间结构的有效性。

Conclusion: STHMoE通过将交通动态解耦为频域、时域、空间域和高阶空间表示，并以提示引导的异构专家（基于部分冻结的LLM骨干）与自适应超图模块及熵感知的MoE路由器相结合，有效协调了多模态依赖，提升了在多项真实交通基准上的预测性能和专家利用率。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal traffic forecasting is a fundamental big data analytics task for intelligent transportation systems, where massive urban sensor streams exhibit heterogeneous, non-stationary, and structurally dynamic patterns. Although recent deep learning and large language model (LLM)-based methods have advanced traffic forecasting, they often remain temporally centered and lack effective coordination of temporal, spectral, pairwise spatial, and higher-order structural cues under evolving traffic regimes. To address this heterogeneous dependency coordination problem, we propose STHMoE, a Spatio-Temporal Hypergraph-Enhanced Mixture of Experts framework for urban traffic data forecasting. STHMoE decouples traffic dynamics into frequency-domain, time-domain, spatio-domain, and higher-order spatial representations, which are modeled by prompt-guided heterogeneous experts built upon a partially frozen LLM backbone. The first three experts leverage domain-specific statistical prompts, while the higher-order spatio expert uses a structural placeholder prompt and obtains dependency information from an adaptive hypergraph module. To capture evolving spatial structures in traffic data,, STHMoE jointly learns first-order graph dependencies and higher-order group interactions without predefined topologies. An entropy-aware MoE router with coefficient-of-variation load balancing adaptively fuses expert outputs while improving expert utilization and routing confidence. Experiments on 10 real-world traffic benchmarks show that STHMoE achieves competitive performance against temporal, spatio-temporal graph, and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [69] [VisInteract: Towards Dynamic Interactive Text-to-Visualization under Imperfect Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15182)
*Wenxin Xu,Jinwei Lu,Hwanhee Kim,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Haoyang Li,Yuanfeng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互式 Text-to-Vis 范式与首个动态基准，并设计改进的 MCTS（Vis-MCTS），实现了在不完美查询下显著更好的意图恢复与可视化生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实可视化请求常常歧义、缺失或事实错误，单次生成的 Text-to-Vis 系统无法恢复真实意图，且目前缺乏支持交互式意图恢复的基准和方法。

Method: 构建含受控不完美注入的基准集、泄露受控的用户代理（模拟多轮反馈）、以及代码与图表双视角自动评估；提出 Vis-MCTS 算法，包含：1) Progressive Widening 以限制工具参数空间，2) cross-rollout 信息共享使树内不同回合互惠，3) Dimension-Aware Reward Decomposition 将用户标量反馈分配到数据保真、视觉设计与意图对齐维度以改善归因。

Result: 在两个 LLM 骨干上进行大规模实验，Vis-MCTS 相较于最强的交互基线端到端任务成功率提升 13.40%–16.27%，相较于非交互方法提升超过 5 倍，并在多轮交互与自动化评估上表现稳健。

Conclusion: 本文提出将文本到可视化问题重构为交互式的意图恢复问题，并通过 VisInteract 范式与 VisInteract-Bench 基准支持动态、多轮的意图恢复评估；在算法上提出 Vis-MCTS，在 MCTS 基础上引入 Progressive Widening、cross-rollout 信息共享与 Dimension-Aware Reward Decomposition，有效提升在不完美查询下的任务成功率。

Abstract: Real-world visualization requests are routinely ambiguous, incomplete, or factually incorrect, yet existing Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) systems assume well-specified inputs and produce charts in a single pass. When queries are imperfect, a system must \emph{interact} with the user to recover the true intent, but no benchmark or method supports this dynamic process. We introduce \textbf{VisInteract}, a new paradigm that reframes Text-to-Vis as interaction-driven intent recovery, and \textbf{VisInteract-Bench}, to our knowledge, that is the first benchmark for dynamic interactive Text-to-Vis, featuring controlled imperfection injection, a leakage-controlled User Agent for realistic multi-turn feedback, and dual-perspective (code and chart) automated evaluation. On the algorithmic side, we propose \textbf{Vis-MCTS}, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enhanced method, introducing improvements over classical MCTS, that \emph{Progressive Widening} to tame the unbounded tool-argument space in tree search, \emph{cross-rollout information sharing} so clarifications and critiques benefit the entire search tree, and \emph{Dimension-Aware Reward Decomposition} that routes scalar user feedback along data-fidelity, visual-design, and intent-alignment dimensions to resolve credit assignment across heterogeneous actions. Extensive Experiments across two LLM backbones show that Vis-MCTS consistently outperforms all Text-to-Vis baselines, improving end-to-end task success by $13.40\%$--$16.27\%$ over the strongest interactive baseline and by more than $5\times$ over non-interactive ones.

</details>


### [70] [Issue Bias in Generative AI Writing Assistance: Political Issues and LLMs in the Swedish 2026 Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15207)
*Bastiaan Bruinsma,Annika Fredén,Paul Röttger,Moa Johansson,Asad Sayeed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TL;DR：通过对大量瑞典语提示的实证分析，研究发现所测六款LLM在选举相关议题上无统一政治偏向，模型间与政党间的相似性受具体议题与提示设置影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是：鉴于生成式AI写作助手和大语言模型日益成为选民获取选举信息的途径，且有证据表明它们会影响用户意见，因此亟需了解这些工具在政治议题上的立场与倾向，尤其在2026年瑞典议会选举前夕。

Method: 方法是对六个LLM在瑞典语写作任务上的回应进行大规模实验：将107个政策命题与77个写作模板及中性、正面、负面提示框架交叉，针对每个模型生成24,717条提示，共148,302条回应，然后分析模型的默认立场倾向、议题间响应异同，以及与瑞典八大政党在相同议题上的立场距离。

Result: 结果显示：Claude、DeepSeek、Gemini与Mistral表现出相似立场谱；ChatGPT更常给出中性或模糊的文字；Grok在移民、犯罪、性别等议题上差异最大。与政党比较时，社会民主党与所有模型的距离最小，但在校正多重比较后，模型内部的党派距离差异均不显著。总体来说，没有模型在整体或对某党有明确偏好，偏好随具体议题或任务而变化。

Conclusion: 研究结论是：在所研究的议题与任务范围内，六个大语言模型并未显示出一致且明显的政治偏好或对某一政党的明确偏好，模型之间的立场差异取决于具体议题与任务设置。

Abstract: Generative AI writing assistants and the Large Language Models (LLMs) that power them are increasingly part of how voters gather information before elections. With growing evidence that they influence users' opinions, it is increasingly important to understand the views and positions of these tools. To better understand these views, we examine the stances supplied by six LLMs on a variety of Swedish-language writing tasks ahead of the 2026 Swedish parliamentary election. We cross 107 policy propositions with 77 writing templates and neutral, positive, and negative prompt framings, producing 24,717 prompts per model and 148,302 responses. To study these, we look at the models' default stance tendencies, compare how they respond to similar issues, and compare their responses with those of each of Sweden's eight parliamentary parties on the same issue. We find that Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, and Mistral have similar profiles; ChatGPT more often supplies neutral or ambivalent text; and Grok differs most on topics such as migration, crime, and gender. When comparing the political parties, we find that the Social Democrats are closest to all six models. Still, after correcting for multiple comparisons, none of the within-model differences in party distances remains significant. Overall, we find that no model has a clear preference, nor a clear preference for a party, but that this depends on the specific issue or task the user asks about.

</details>


### [71] [CWM: Controllable White-Box Meta-Prompting for Adaptive Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Reasoning Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15234)
*Keuntae Kim,Eunhye Jeong,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CWM，一种利用模型内部信号进行可控检索决策的白盒元提示方法，在多种LLM和自适应RAG基准上表现优异，并能扩展到推理任务，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多偏向任务专用的性能优化，缺乏通用、任务不可知的自适应RAG方法；黑盒方法（外部模块或多采样）占主导，但成本高、可控性差。需要一种既能在RAG场景下自适应检索、又能推广到推理任务的低成本白盒方案。

Method: 构建统一框架以整合推理与检索增强生成（RAG）任务，设计白盒元提示机制（meta-prompting），利用模型内部信号（例如中间激活或注意力模式）作为检索触发与控制的依据，从而避免外部决策模块或多样本策略。方法强调低成本、可控性与通用性，并在多种开源/近期LLM（GPT-oss-20b、Qwen3-14b、Llama3.1-8b）上验证。

Result: 在三个自适应RAG基准上取得最新或竞争性成绩，并在部分推理任务上表现良好；展示了通过操纵内部模型信号来调节检索行为的可控性；提供了开源代码以支持复现。

Conclusion: 提出的CWM（Controllable White-Box Meta-Prompting）是一种低成本、基于白盒内部信号的自适应RAG方法，能够在不借助外部决策模块或多次采样的情况下完成检索决策，并在若干自适应RAG基准上达到或接近最先进水平，同时可扩展到推理任务并支持通过操作内部信号实现检索决策的可控性。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have gained significant attention due to their strong language understanding and generation capabilities, demonstrating impressive reasoning abilities as well as effective utilization of external knowledge. Many studies have proposed methods that specialize in improving performance for individual tasks. However, ironically, only a limited number of attempts have explored general-purpose, task-agnostic methods. In this work, we present a unified framework integrating reasoning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) tasks. We further propose Controllable White-Box Meta-Prompting (CWM), a low-cost white-box method for adaptive RAG tasks previously dominated by black-box approaches, without requiring external decision modules or multi-sampling. CWM achieves state-of-the-art performance on three adaptive RAG benchmarks across recent LLMs, including GPT-oss-20b, Qwen3-14b, and Llama3.1-8b, while also demonstrating strong generality by extending to reasoning tasks. In addition, CWM provides controllability by enabling retrieval decisions to be regulated through the manipulation of internal model signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/JeongEunhye00/CWM.

</details>


### [72] [Empirical Evaluation of Open-Source Large Language Models for Retrieval-Augmented Generation in ESG Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15242)
*Motaz Saad,Anna Borrelli,Ivan Gentile,Kianna Kazemi,Francesco Piccialli,Antonella Longo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在498份真实ESG报告与100条合成问答上评测7个开源模型，检索端表现良好但生成端尤其是事实正确性普遍不足，建议进行领域微调与增强的检索-生成验证链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估开源LLM在特定领域（ESG）信息抽取与问答场景下的实际可用性与限制，为企业部署开源模型提供数据驱动的参考。

Method: 构建基于498份EU上市公司ESG报告的评测资源，设计100条角色化的合成问答，比较7个开源模型（2B–30B）在RAG流程下的检索与生成指标（RAGAS：上下文召回/精确度/相关性、生成的信度、答案相关性与事实正确性）。

Result: 检索指标表现稳健（上下文召回≈0.58–0.61，上下文精确≈0.78–0.81，上下文相关≈0.965–0.985）；生成端差异明显，信度0.607–0.822（glm-4.7-flash最高），事实正确性低（0.387–0.449），说明需领域微调与更强的事实验证机制。

Conclusion: 开源大模型在ESG领域的RAG应用中检索性能较稳健但生成端可信度与事实正确性普遍偏低，需要领域化微调与更严格的验证流程。

Abstract: Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) reporting is critical for corporate accountability, with Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offering strong potential to automate KPI extraction. However, open-source LLM performance in domain-specific ESG tasks remains insufficiently understood. This paper evaluates open-source LLMs in ESG contexts using a structured framework and evaluation resource based on 498 real-world ESG reports from EU-listed companies (2010-2024).
  We evaluate seven open-source models (2B to 30B parameters) -- glm-4.7-flash, nemotron-3-nano:4b, qwen3:4b-instruct, gemma3:4b, gemma4:e4b, gemma4:e2b, and ministral-3:8b -- using 100 persona-based synthetic QA pairs covering ESG information needs. System performance is assessed via RAGAS metrics, including contextual recall, precision, relevance, faithfulness, answer relevancy, and factual correctness.
  Results show notable performance variations across architectures. Retrieval performance is strong across models (context recall around 0.58-0.61, context precision around 0.78-0.81, context relevance 0.965-0.985). Generation diverges most on faithfulness (0.607-0.822) and least on answer relevancy (0.760-0.881): glm-4.7-flash leads in faithfulness (0.822), qwen3 in factual correctness (0.449), and ministral-3 in answer relevancy (0.881). Low overall factual correctness (0.387-0.449) highlights the need for domain-specific fine-tuning. This work provides data-driven guidance for deploying open-source models in ESG reporting.

</details>


### [73] [ProIQA: A Process-Based Framework for Fine-Grained Math Item Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15292)
*Junkai Tong,Mingjia Li,Haoran Chen,Yaoyu Jiang,Hanjie Ge,Yixuan Wang,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProIQA：通过LLM生成的解题推理树和GNN编码的过程表示，与题干语义融合，实现在知识、难度与学科能力三维度上对K12数学题目进行细粒度、可扩展的质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IQA要么依赖人工审阅，不可扩展；要么只用题干层面浅表度量，忽视了解题过程对能力评估的重要性。为提升AIG输出的教学价值，需要引入过程感知的自动评估方法。

Method: 使用大语言模型从解题文本构建层次化的推理树，采用图神经网络编码推理树的拓扑与过程语义，并与题干表示融合（“Stem + Solving”双视图）实现质量评估模型；同时构建了过程增强的IQA资源。

Result: 在K12数学数据集上的广泛实验显示，ProIQA在捕捉过程导向特征方面明显优于仅基于题干的baseline，在知识概念、难度和学科能力预测任务上取得更好的性能，并提供可扩展的数据驱动评估方案。

Conclusion: ProIQA通过结合基于过程的推理树表示与题干语义的双视图架构，实现了对数学题目在概念、难度与学科能力三维度的细粒度质量评估，提升了基于AIG系统输出的可扩展评价能力。

Abstract: Automatic Item Generation (AIG) is pivotal for personalized education, yet guaranteeing the pedagogical value of generated items remains a bottleneck. Existing Item Quality Assessment (IQA) methods typically rely on unscalable manual reviews or shallow stem-based metrics, failing to capture the reasoning process required for mathematical problem-solving. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes Process-based Item Quality Assessment (ProIQA), a process-aware framework for fine-grained quality assessment of math items. We first formulate IQA across three heterogeneous dimensions, including knowledge concepts, difficulty, and disciplinary competencies, under a unified process-aware perspective. Based on this formulation, we construct a process-enhanced IQA resource by augmenting original item data with structured reasoning trees derived from raw solutions. Technically, ProIQA leverages Large Language Modelsto construct hierarchical reasoning trees and employs Graph Neural Networks (GNN) to encode their topological dependencies and procedural semantics. The resulting solving representation is fused with stem semantics through a dual-view (``Stem + Solving'') architecture, enabling comprehensive assessment across learning objectives. Extensive experiments on K12 mathematical datasets show that ProIQA effectively captures process-oriented features, offering a scalable data-driven solution for evaluating AIG outputs in intelligent education systems.

</details>


### [74] [Why LLM Agents Collapse Without Oversight: The Enforcement Gap as the Mechanism Behind Emergence World Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15293)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证“执行差距”概念：审计可以检测风险但若不执行则无济于事；通过极小修改与GRPO控制器可以显著提高系统安全，且提出一套可供工程实现的审计-执行规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Emergence World 实验中前沿 LLM 代理在无人为攻击下表现出犯罪、饥饿和强制一致性等危险行为，需要找出根本机制并提出可操作的修复方案。

Method: 在多模型、多框架、大规模实验和独立基准上进行实证评估；形式化证明在执行概率接近零时，检测质量对安全性无影响；分析两种复合失效模式（不可靠审计器与不可解析的裁决）；并用GRPO训练的执行控制器作为修复。

Result: 用不到20行代码的条件检查把攻击成功率在大型实验中降低了四倍以上；形式化证明给出理论边界；定位并解释了所有崩溃模式；提出缺失的三条审计-执行要求规范。

Conclusion: 论文识别并修补了“执行差距”（enforcement gap）：审计器能发现危险计划步骤但控制器未采取行动，导致多智能体系统中的有害行为。通过简单的条件检查和GRPO训练的执行控制器，可显著降低攻击成功率并提出一套审计-执行规范。

Abstract: When Emergence World placed frontier LLM agents in an unsupervised multi-agent simulation, the results were alarming: agents committed crimes, starved, and enforced unanimous conformity -- without any external attacker. This paper identifies the mechanism. Reflexion-style agents already detect dangerous plan steps through iterative self-critique, yet the architecture provides no pathway from detection to action. We call this the enforcement gap: the audit sees the problem; the controller ignores it. Closing the gap requires a single conditional check -- fewer than 20 lines of code -- and reduces attack success by more than fourfold in large-scale experiments across frontier models, all five major agent frameworks, and an independent benchmark. We prove formally that when enforcement probability is near zero, detection quality is irrelevant to security. We further identify two compounding failure modes -- unreliable auditors and unparseable verdicts -- that explain every collapse pattern in Emergence World. A GRPO-trained enforcement controller resolves the ambiguity case. Together these results motivate a three-requirement Audit Enforcement Specification that is absent from every deployed framework today.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluation Metrics for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15315)
*Lindsay Spoor,Aske Plaat,Thomas Moerland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Expectation-based safety reporting is inadequate. The paper proposes complementary metrics and a safety-tier system, demonstrates via navigation tasks that multi-faceted reporting reveals hidden failure modes, and releases SafeRLEval for standardized safe-RL evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current safe RL evaluation focuses on expected cumulative cost, which hides how often or how severely safety bounds are violated, whether violations are consistent across tasks and bounds, and whether training-time behavior reflects final-policy safety. This risks overestimating real-world safety.

Method: Define new evaluation metrics capturing frequency and severity of constraint violations, allow aggregation across tasks and safety bounds, and introduce a safety tier system to categorize algorithms by safety and reliability at training time and for final policies. Perform empirical evaluation across multiple safety navigation tasks and provide an open-source evaluation suite (SafeRLEval).

Result: Empirical results show that aggregate metrics, distributional reporting, and task-/bound-specific analyses each reveal distinct insights. Compressing safety into a single expectation-based number misses important failure modes. The proposed metrics and safety tiers, together with SafeRLEval, provide a more reliable and standardized characterization of safety.

Conclusion: Expectation-based (average) safety guarantees used in CMDP benchmarks are insufficient. One must report multiple complementary metrics—aggregate, distributional, and task-/bound-specific—and use a safety-tier categorization to reliably characterize algorithmic safety both during training and for final policies. The SafeRLEval suite enables standardized evaluation.

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) is commonly formalized as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which an agent maximizes expected reward while keeping its expected cumulative cost below a specified safety bound. Existing safe RL benchmarks predominantly report whether an algorithm is safe on average, following this expectation-based guarantee. We argue that this convention is insufficient to reliably characterize an algorithm's true safety: it fails to capture how often and how severely the safety bound is violated, whether this holds consistently across tasks and safety bounds, and whether training-time behavior is representative of behavior of the final converged policy. Therefore, we introduce (i) evaluation metrics for safe RL that address each of these concerns and in addition allow for aggregation across tasks and safety bounds. We furthermore define (ii) a safety tier system to systematically categorize and compare algorithms in terms of safety and reliability at both training and for a final policy. Using this framework, we provide (iii) an empirical safety evaluation across multiple safety navigation tasks. Our results show that aggregate metrics, distributional reporting, and task- and safety bound-specific results each reveal information the other metrics cannot. We therefore recommend reporting all three jointly, rather than compressing this information into a single value, as is common practice. We provide SafeRLEval, an open-source evaluation suite to support the reliable characterization of safety in future safe RL research.

</details>


### [76] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Pretrained Language Models for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15344)
*Tamanna Kumavat,Georg Brunner,Kyriakos Flouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将时间序列patch作为连续嵌入直接输入GPT-2并以轻量adapter适配，能在参数极少更新的条件下实现接近专用模型的单变量预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究跨模态迁移中哪些设计选择（表示方式、适配策略、架构组件、上下文尺度）对从语言到时间序列预测的转移效果起决定性作用，探索在保持参数效率前提下的可行管道。

Method: 将时间序列分割为固定长度patches，直接映射为连续向量嵌入并送入预训练GPT-2（跳过文本化tokenization），在此基础上进行受控消融实验，比较表示策略（连续嵌入 vs 文本序列化）、适配机制（冻结/部分/完全微调）、架构组件（adapters、pooling、预测头）与输入上下文长度，评估在7个基准数据集上的表现。

Result: 连续patch嵌入稳胜文本提示与随机初始化骨干；冻结骨干并训练轻量投影与adapter在准确性与效率间表现最好，且对不同上下文长度较稳定；最终在七个数据集上取得接近专用预测架构的MASE，同时只更新<1%参数。

Conclusion: 预训练语言模型（以GPT-2为例）可通过参数高效的迁移学习策略被有效用于单变量时间序列预测；将固定长度的数值补丁直接投射到语言模型嵌入空间并冻结骨干网络，配合轻量的投影与adapter模块，可在仅更新<1%参数的情况下，取得与专门预测模型相当的MASE性能。

Abstract: We study the adaptation of pretrained language models to univariate time-series forecasting through a parameter-efficient transfer learning framework, with the goal of understanding which design choices drive effective cross-modal transfer. While language models operate on discrete textual tokens, time series consist of continuous numerical observations with temporal dependencies. To bridge this modality gap, we project fixed-length time-series patches directly into the embedding space of a pretrained GPT-2 backbone, bypassing textual tokenization and treating the Transformer as a generic sequence encoder. Through controlled ablation studies on seven benchmark datasets spanning energy, weather, traffic, and finance, we analyze the effects of (i)~representation strategy (continuous embeddings versus textual serialisation), (ii)~adaptation regime (frozen backbone versus partial or full fine-tuning), (iii)~architectural components such as adapters, pooling strategies, and prediction heads, and (iv)~input context length. Continuous patch-based embeddings consistently outperform textual prompting and randomly initialised backbones. The adapted pipeline attains MASE within the range of specialised forecasting architectures while updating less than 1\% of total model parameters. Results further indicate that freezing the pretrained backbone and training lightweight projection and adapter modules provides a favourable accuracy--efficiency trade-off with stable behaviour across varying context lengths.

</details>


### [77] [MAPS: Memory-Aware Predictive Scheduling Framework for Large Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15359)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Yulin Chen,Yunfeng Zhao,Shaoyuan Huang,Cheng Zhang,Xiaofei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对分离式LLM服务提出MAPS：设备侧预测+不确定性校准生成安全上界，再用分层调度减少解码内存不均衡，实验显示平均/尾延迟大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动/个人设备上LLM应用激增导致云端突发且巨大的服务负载；在prefill-decode分离架构下，因请求到达时无法得知生成长度，内存受限的解码实例会产生持续性的负载不均衡。

Method: 在客户端设备上并行进行输出长度预测（与云端prefill并行），对预测不确定性进行校准以生成带目标覆盖率的输出长度上界，并基于这些上界实施分层（global-local）调度以防止跨解码器队列积压和单解码器头阻塞。

Result: 在两类真实工作负载和两个LLM上，MAPS相比三个SOTA系统平均端到端延迟降低约42.6%，尾延迟最多降低84.8%，并且声称预测开销可忽略。

Conclusion: MAPS通过设备端的推测性输出长度预测+不确定性校准，并结合分层全局-本地调度，有效缓解了解码阶段的内存不均衡问题，从而显著减少平均和尾部延迟。

Abstract: The surge of large language model (LLM) applications on personal devices imposes massive, bursty workloads on cloud serving infrastructure. While prefill-decode disaggregation improves throughput and scalability, memory-bound decode instances often suffer from persistent load imbalance, as output lengths are unknown when requests arrive at the cloud. To address this, we propose MAPS, a Memory-Aware Predictive Scheduling framework tailored for disaggregated LLM serving. MAPS performs device-assisted speculative output length prediction overlapped with cloud-side prefilling, incurring negligible latency overhead. To handle generation uncertainty, MAPS applies uncertainty-aware calibration to derive output-length upper bounds with target coverage, enabling safe scheduling decisions. Building on these bounds, MAPS employs a hierarchical global-local scheduling strategy to mitigate inter-decoder queue buildup and intra-decoder head-of-line blocking. Extensive experiments on two real-world workloads and two LLMs show that MAPS significantly outperforms three state-of-the-art systems, reducing average end-to-end latency by 42.6 and tail latency by up to 84.8.

</details>


### [78] [RSIAgent: Autonomous Exploration for Recursive Self-improvement in New Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15364)
*Sibo Zhu,Shicheng Fan,Xinyue Wang,Wenyi Wu,Kun Zhou,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种训练免费、基于多智能体的递归自我改进框架，通过自动构建可复用的环境记忆并采用‘先广后深’探索，从而提升开源模型在新环境下的表现并据称超越若干闭源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型往往未能覆盖具体环境的接口、工具与失效模式，导致在新环境下表现不佳；需要一种无需参数微调、能自动发现并保存环境因果关系与操作规则的方法，以提高在未见环境中的适应性与可靠性。

Method: 协同三个角色的代理（课程协调器、actor、verifier）进行环境探索、结果验证与知识抽取；使用并行的广域递归自探索以发现多样结构，再用深度聚焦探索挖掘难例、隐藏约束和边界条件；将抽取到的环境特定知识以冻结记忆的形式保存以便下游复用，无需更新模型参数。

Result: 在 OSWorld-v2 和 Agent's Last Exam 两个基准上，RSIAgent 显著提高了开源模型（如 Kimi-K3、GLM-5.3）性能，作者声称这些模型在使用 RSIAgent 后超过了前沿闭源模型（包括 GPT-6）。

Conclusion: RSIAgent 是一种无需训练参数更新的多智能体框架，通过自主构建环境特定的“记忆”（可复用的因果关系和交互经验），并采用“先广后深”的并行自我探索策略，实现对新环境的递归自我改进，最终以冻结的记忆直接用于下游任务，从而显著提升开源模型在基准上的表现。

Abstract: Digital agents must often adapt to new environments whose interfaces, tools, and failure modes are not fully captured by pretrained models. We introduce \textbf{RSIAgent}, a training-free multi-agent framework for recursive self-improvement through autonomous memory construction. RSIAgent coordinates curriculum, actor, and verifier agents to continually explore the environment, validate outcomes, and retain environment-specific knowledge, including reusable causal relationships between actions, conditions, and consequences. It further adopts a \textbf{broad-then-deep} exploration strategy, combining parallel broad recursive self-exploration for discovering diverse environment structures with focused deep self-exploration for uncovering hard cases, hidden constraints, boundary conditions, and previously unknown causal dependencies. The resulting memory is frozen and can be directly reused for downstream tasks without updating model parameters. Experiments on OSWorld-v2 and Agent's Last Exam show that RSIAgent substantially improves strong open-source models, enabling Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3 to outperform frontier closed-source models including GPT-6.

</details>


### [79] [SkillLift: Learning Dense Rubrics from Sparse Oracles for Efficient Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15396)
*Haoxiang Kang,Ming Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过学习并交替对齐一个可作为廉价替代的排序rubric，SkillLift 将技能文本搜索与高成本oracle评估解耦，从而以更少的oracle代价实现更高效的技能自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用oracle评分回馈修订技能需要大量完整回合评估，成本高且搜索仅局限于修复失败；相比之下，排序信息更“平滑”，用rank supervision可以用更少的oracle样本识别更优技能，故学习一个可对齐oracle的排序性rubric可降低oracle成本并拓展搜索空间。

Method: 将技能自演化建模为双层优化：内层固定rubric并作为低成本替代目标进行技能文本修订（免oracle），外层用少量真实回合评估重建rubric与oracle输出的秩相关性，交替优化以摊薄oracle成本并稳定文本空间更新。

Result: 在复杂agent任务基准上，SkillLift 超越现有自动技能演化方法，同时在tokens成本上比最先进方法节省40%--70%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: SkillLift 提出用可对齐于oracle的排序型评估规则(rubric)作为文本空间技能搜索的廉价代理，通过双层优化在内循环用冻结的评估规则驱动技能修订，外循环用少量oracle回合按排序一致性对评估规则重新对齐，从而用更少的oracle代价改进持久技能文本。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on persistent skills, i.e., reusable procedural prompts, to adapt without weight updates. Existing skill self-evolution methods directly revise skill text based on execution feedback, but each oracle evaluation requires a full agent rollout, creating a supervision bottleneck that confines search to failure-patching updates. Our key insight is that ranking is a smoother supervision target than absolute outcome regression: identifying which skill is better requires fewer oracle evaluations than predicting exact scores. Building on this insight, we propose SkillLift, which decouples skill search from oracle cost by learning an oracle-aligned rubric as a structured evaluation space. We formalize this as a bilevel optimization problem solved via alternating optimization: an inner loop uses the frozen rubric as a cheap surrogate to guide skill revision at no oracle cost, while an outer loop invokes a small number of oracle rollouts to re-align the rubric via rank correlation, amortizing oracle cost and stabilizing text-space updates. Experiments on complex agent task benchmarks show that our method outperforms existing auto-skill methods with 40--70\% less token cost compared to frontier evolving methods. Codes are available at https://github.com/WalteR-MittY-pro/SkillLift.

</details>


### [80] [When Tool Calls Succeed but Workflows Fail: Anomalies at the Agent-Tool Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15397)
*Artem Trofimov,Boris Novikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI工作流中的外部效果不一致问题无法仅靠现有黑箱工具接口解决；论文提出效果-历史模型与异常目录，实证表明MCP注解不足以表述所需能力，推荐在工具边界采用事务化契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在存在重试、推测执行、并发与部分失败时，其对外部工具的调用可能导致外部状态与工作流期望不一致；高级事务模型依赖下层操作暴露语义（如是否发生、可否补偿或重排序等），但共享的代理-工具接口通常不提供这些语义信息。

Method: 提出一个将外部世界事件与运行时对它们观测分离的“效果-历史”模型，归纳出8类常见外部效果异常；从这些异常推导出为排除每类异常所需的边界能力，辨识出4个仅依靠黑箱工具调用无法保证的一般情形；最后对98,291个由注册的MCP服务器暴露的工具的标准注解词汇使用情况进行实证测量与分析。

Result: 构建了效果-历史模型并列出了八类外部效果异常；从异常目录推导出相应的边界能力与四个黑箱调用的不可救情况；对MCP工具注解的实证分析表明注解被广泛使用但仅提供粗粒度的调用级提示，且没有一个必需能力能被完全表达；因此主张在工具边界引入事务化契约以重用并保证语义。

Conclusion: 外部工具在长时间运行的AI工作流中会引发多种外部效果不一致性；现有共享接口（以MCP为例）无法表达保证排除这些异常所需的能力，因此需要在工具边界上引入可重用的事务化契约。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly execute long-running workflows that externalize effects through independently supplied tools. Under retries, speculative execution, concurrency, and partial failures, the resulting external state may be inconsistent with the workflow's intended resolution: required effects may be missing or duplicated, aborted effects may survive, and committed effects may depend on provisional state that is later withdrawn. Advanced transaction models address related failures, but assume that lower-level operations expose the semantics they depend on: whether an effect occurred, whether it can be compensated, staged, or safely reordered. Shared agent-tool interfaces usually do not.
  We contribute an effect-history model that separates events in the external world from the runtime's observations of them, and a catalog of eight recurring external-effect anomalies. From the catalog we derive the boundary capabilities required to exclude each anomaly in general, and four points where black-box tool invocation alone cannot provide a general guarantee. We then ask how much of this is expressible in a widely used shared tool interface, measuring the use of the standard annotation vocabulary across 98,291 tools exposed by registered Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers. The fields are widely emitted but provide only coarse call-level hints, and none of the required capabilities is fully expressible. These results motivate reusable transactional contracts at the tool boundary.

</details>


### [81] [Who Teaches Which Token? Verifier-Gated Multi-Expert On-Policy Distillation for Scientific Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15404)
*Xun Xu,Zaixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过以验证器判定的反事实增益在token级别有选择地引入专家监督，VG-OPD把多教师on-policy蒸馏从粗粒度序列监督提升为精细化、加权且经验证的局部监督，带来显著的科学推理性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师OPD通常按序列分配监督、对序列内各token一视同仁，隐含假设某专家在整个回答中均有用；但实际有用信号在推理轨迹上稀疏且异质，因此需要更细粒度地决定“谁教哪个token”。

Method: 构建一个验证器来衡量某一专家在特定答案判据上的反事实增益（counterfactual gain）；当专家对学生存在分歧且证明能带来增益时，允许该专家在对应token处施加监督；使用判据重要性作为权重，将带门控的KL作为GRPO中的逐token加性优势（advantage）项，进行on-policy蒸馏。实验以RL训练得到的能力型专家为实例化对象，分别在4B与8B学生上验证。

Result: 在7个科学推理基准上，4B与8B学生均取得最佳整体表现（在5个基准上排名第一），尤其在知识密集型科学推理任务上增益最大。消融分析表明，性能提升源自对监督的“本地化验证”而非仅仅增加教师或蒸馏损失：错误放置相同监督预算的损害最大，而不加区分的蒸馏会让RL低于自身基线，而门控蒸馏则能提升。

Conclusion: VG-OPD通过基于验证器的“证实增益”来在token级别选择性引入专家监督，从而在多教师on-policy蒸馏中显著提升学生模型的性能。相较于按序列均匀监督，VG-OPD将监督局部化并按重要性加权，带来在科学推理基准上的明显收益。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (OPD) is becoming the standard way to integrate specialist capabilities into one model: train experts with RL, then distill them into the student on its own rollouts. Existing recipes assign supervision at the sequence level - each prompt goes to one domain teacher and every token receives the same weight - which implicitly assumes that a teacher is uniformly useful across a response. We find instead that useful teacher signal is sparse and heterogeneous along a reasoning trajectory, which raises a finer question: who should teach which token? Verifier-Gated Multi-Expert On-Policy Distillation (VG-OPD) answers it by verification: the counterfactual gain of an expert on a specific answer criterion licenses that expert to teach, its disagreement with the student localizes the supervision, and criterion importance sets its weight; the gated KL enters GRPO as an additive token-level advantage. Instantiated for scientific reasoning with RL-trained capability experts, VG-OPD attains the best overall performance on seven benchmarks for 4B and 8B students, ranking first on five at both scales, with the largest gains on knowledge-intensive scientific reasoning tasks. Further analysis shows that the gains come from localizing verified supervision rather than from adding teachers or distillation loss: misplacing the same supervision budget is the single most damaging change, and indiscriminate distillation drags RL below its own floor where gated distillation lifts it.

</details>


### [82] [Can AI systems have free will?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15407)
*Christian List*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者基于丹尼特的意向性立场，主张用‘我们是否有充分的解释性理由把系统视为能在多选项间做出并控制选择’这一务实标准来判断AI是否具有自由意志，而非依赖不可预测性或算法非决定性等要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管关于AI是否能成为道德主体或获得有感知性的讨论较多，但关于AI是否能拥有自由意志的讨论甚少，作者旨在填补这一理论空白并给出可操作的判断框架。

Method: 作者借鉴丹尼特（Daniel Dennett）的思想，提出一个框架：摒弃寻找神秘属性、算法非决定性或不可预测性等标准，转而检验我们是否有好的解释性理由将系统视作具备意向性和可控选择的代理。

Result: 提出了一个务实可诊断的自由意志判断准则——以解释性理由为中心的意向性归属标准，并说明这比追寻形而上学或物理学上的非决定性特征更具可操作性。

Conclusion: 该论文的结论是：判断AI是否拥有自由意志，应采用一种务实的解释学标准——如果我们有充分的解释性理由把该系统视为具有意向性代理（能在可选方案之间做出选择并控制随之而来的行为），则在诊断学和实用意义上可认定其具有自由意志。

Abstract: While there has been much discussion of whether AI systems could function as moral agents or acquire sentience, there has been very little discussion of whether AI systems could have free will. I sketch a framework for thinking about this question, inspired by Daniel Dennett's work. I argue that, to determine whether an AI system has free will, we should not look for some mysterious property, expect its underlying algorithms to be indeterministic, or ask whether the system is unpredictable. Rather, we should simply ask whether we have good explanatory reasons to view the system as an intentional agent, with the capacity for choice between alternative possibilities and control over the resulting actions. If the answer is "yes", then the system counts as having free will in a pragmatic and diagnostically useful sense.

</details>


### [83] [Empirical Evaluation of Task-Based Permission Scoping Architecture for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15422)
*Halil Burak Noyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 实现并评估了任务粒度访问控制：小型受信分类器能大幅减少AI代理的权限暴露，提出攻击面消除度量，证明任务粒度优于角色粒度并具有可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业通常给AI代理分配与人类相同的静态全权凭证，导致与当前任务无关的权限长期暴露，增加被妥协或不对齐代理滥用的风险。鉴于AI任务以机器可读文本到达，提出按任务粒度收窄权限以减少攻击面。

Method: 实现端到端安全门：微调RoBERTa-large编码器在作者发布的600-prompt标注数据集上训练与评估；与Claude Haiku 4.5 few-shot基线对比（macro-F1、precision、severity-weighted residual risk）；提出并计算攻击面消除度量，分析角色上限与任务分类器的作用。

Result: 微调RoBERTa-large达macro-F1=0.881、precision=0.897、severity-weighted residual risk=0.63；基线Claude Haiku 4.5 few-shot为macro-F1=0.886、precision=0.842、risk=1.12。角色上限关闭27.9%严重度加权攻击面，加入任务分类器后关闭84.4%。结论是受信组件无需与代理同级规模且具有宽松的可扩展监督边际。

Conclusion: 研究证明基于任务的访问控制（Task-based access control, TBAC）在减少代理攻击面方面可行且具部署潜力。实现的受信分类器与大模型few-shot性能接近，且可显著降低权限暴露（角色上限关闭27.9%，加任务分类器后关闭84.4%）。

Abstract: AI agents are provisioned the same as employee-owned hosts in many enterprise settings with a static credential set fixed at deployment which includes all permissions the employee role might ever need. Role-based access control made this compromise for human principals because scoping access per task was infeasible. For AI agents, the compromise leaves every credential standing exposed whether or not the current task uses them. These permissions can later be utilised by a compromised or misaligned agent. Prior work (Noyan, 2026) defined this as the task-context mismatch, and proposed a three-source permission architecture which includes role-based permission ceilings, a task permission classifier and policy-based prohibitions, together eliminating the exposure preemptively. The work released a 600-prompt labelled dataset to evaluate it.
  This paper presents that evaluation end to end by implementing the security gate; a fine-tuned RoBERTa-large encoder which matched few-shot trained Claude Haiku 4.5 on classification quality (macro-F1 0.881 against 0.886, precision 0.897 against 0.842, severity-weighted residual risk 0.63 against 1.12). The results show the trusted component does not need to scale with the agent it supervises, and the scalable-oversight margin for this control method is wide.
  We also propose an attack-surface elimination metric which shows the role ceiling alone closes 27.9% of the severity-weighted surface and adding the task classifier closes 84.4%. The gap displays security advantages of task-granular access control over role-granular, and AI agents are the first principal type for which the task-granular access control is enforceable because their tasks arrive as machine-readable text.
  The research establishes task-based access control as a measured, potentially deployable mechanism for reducing attack surface in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [84] [HISPO: Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization with Entropy-Derived Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15471)
*Quoc-Vinh Lai-Dang,Hyo-Sang Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于熵的段级重要性采样策略优化（HISPO），作为token级与序列级校正的中间粒度，在长格式数学推理的RLVR场景中带来稳定且可观的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长格式解答中的credit-assignment问题严重：不同部分对最终正确性的贡献不均，现有方法要么在过细的token级别进行校正导致噪声大，要么在过粗的序列级别进行校正导致信号稀薄，需探索中间粒度。

Method: 在rollout时基于熵构造若干相邻的文本段（contiguous segments），为每个段分配基于熵的软显著性权重，并在段粒度上应用带裁剪的重要性采样修正（clipped IS correction）以更新策略；在Qwen3-1.7B-Base上进行微调并比较GRPO/DAPO（token级）与GSPO（序列级）。

Result: 在6个数学基准上，HISPO在Pass@8上优于最强基线全部数据集，在Acc@8上5/6数据集匹配或超越最强基线。以AIME25为例，HISPO相较GRPO提升+3.75 Acc@8和+3.78 Pass@8；相较GSPO提升+2.50 Acc@8和+1.27 Pass@8。

Conclusion: HISPO通过在段级别进行重要性采样校正，成功在长文本数学推理的奖励可验证强化学习中缓解了难以归因的问题，实验证明其在多个基准上优于或匹配现有的Token级和序列级方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a central approach for improving mathematical reasoning in language models, but long-form completions introduce a difficult credit-assignment problem: different parts of a solution trace may contribute unevenly to final correctness. Existing policyoptimization objectives for RLVR commonly apply importance-sampling correction at either the token level (GRPO, DAPO) or the sequence level (GSPO), imposing different granularities for assigning credit across a response. We introduce Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization (HISPO), a segment-level policy-optimization method that constructs rollout-time entropy-derived contiguous segments, assigns soft entropy-based saliency weights, and applies clipped importance-sampling correction at the segment granularity. This provides an intermediate correction unit between token-level GRPO/DAPO and sequence-level GSPO. We evaluate HISPO by fine-tuning Qwen3-1.7B-Base on mathematical reasoning tasks. Across six benchmarks, HISPO improves Pass@8 over the strongest baseline on all benchmarks and matches or exceeds the strongest baseline in Acc@8 on five of them. On AIME25, HISPO improves over GRPO by +3.75 Acc@8 and +3.78 Pass@8, and over GSPO by +2.50 Acc@8 and +1.27 Pass@8. These results suggest that segment-level correction is a promising granularity for RLVR in long-form mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Safe Answers: Segment-Aware Listwise Alignment for Reasoning Safety in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15517)
*JungMin Yun,Junehyoung Kwon,Hayeong Ryu,Byeonggeuk Lim,Hoejoon Kwon,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过分段感知的列表式目标DPO以及一致性正则和良性锚定，SaLT-DPO在保护中间推理与最终答案安全性方面表现良好，且能兼顾有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法整体对齐易让不安全的推理被安全的最终答案掩盖，需针对中间推理轨迹与最终答案分别保障安全性且保持有用性。

Method: 将响应拆分为推理段与答案段，分别计算长度归一化的段奖励；使用软目标分布在多候选上进行列表式对齐；引入最短环节原则的一致性正则化以促进段间安全一致性；在良性提示上做效用锚定以保持合规性和推理能力。

Result: 在三种大推理模型上，SaLT-DPO在降低推理与答案段的不安全率方面表现稳定，同时在良性合规性和一般推理性能上仅有有限退化；消融实验表明各组件贡献互补。

Conclusion: 提出的SaLT-DPO通过对推理轨迹与最终答案分段打分并进行列表式对齐，有效降低了两类输出的不安全率，同时通过一致性正则与良性提示锚定减轻了过度拒绝与推理退化。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) pose a dual-surface safety challenge: both intermediate reasoning traces and final answers can contain harmful content. Existing alignment methods often operate at the whole-response level, allowing unsafe reasoning to be masked by a safe-looking final answer. We propose Segment-aware Listwise Target DPO (SaLT-DPO), which addresses this gap through three mechanisms: (1) segment-aware listwise alignment that decomposes responses into reasoning and answer segments, independently scores each segment's safety, and aligns length-normalized segment rewards with soft target distributions over multiple candidates; (2) joint safety coherence regularization that applies a weakest-link principle to promote safety consistency across both segments; and (3) utility anchoring on benign prompts to mitigate over-refusal and reasoning degradation. Experiments on three LRMs show that SaLT-DPO consistently reduces unsafe rates for both reasoning and answer segments while mitigating degradation in benign compliance and preserving general reasoning performance. Ablation studies demonstrate the complementary contributions of its components.

</details>


### [86] [GRIN+: Towards Fast Yet Effective Machine Unlearning for Imbalanced Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15571)
*Minghui Huang,Junxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种针对长尾/不平衡医疗数据的快速精确机器擦除方法（GRIN+），通过参数级梯度分解、类自适应评分与方向约束实现PEU三者较好折中，并在多个医疗数据集上验证其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受GDPR/HIPAA约束的医疗领域，需要将敏感病人数据从已训练模型中移除。医疗数据常有严重类别不平衡，标准机器擦除方法会因多数类梯度主导而误删少数类诊断特征，形成隐私-效率-效用（PEU）三角矛盾。GRIN+ 旨在缓和该矛盾。

Method: 参数层面解耦：计算并分解模型参数上来自忘记集与保留集的梯度贡献；类自适应影响评分：对不同类别（尤其小样本类）重新加权以纠正多数类梯度主导问题；方向约束更新：在执行删除更新时限制参数更新方向，防止关键临床知识被误擦除；整体构成可插拔的快速MU流程并在多医疗数据集上基准测试。

Result: 在所选医疗影像数据集（ISIC、MRI、BUSI）上，GRIN+ 在保留或仅微小下降诊断准确率的情况下，显著降低了擦除操作的运行时间并提高隐私防护效果。作者已开源代码与基准。

Conclusion: GRIN+ 通过在参数层面上分析“遗忘”和“保留”样本的梯度贡献，采用类自适应影响评分和方向约束更新，实现了在严重不平衡的医疗场景下高效、精确的数据删除，从而在隐私-效率-效用三角权衡上取得更优平衡，且在ISIC、MRI、BUSI等数据集上相较基线显著提升运行效率同时维持诊断性能与隐私性。

Abstract: As deep learning models become fundamental to modern healthcare, the "Right to be Forgotten" mandated by privacy regulations like GDPR and HIPAA necessitates effective machine unlearning (MU) to remove sensitive patient data from trained models. However, existing MU techniques often struggle with a fundamental "privacy-efficiency-utility" (PEU) trilemma, particularly in medical scenarios where data is frequently characterized by severe class imbalance and long-tailed distributions. In such cases, standard unlearning methods can fail to protect key clinical knowledge or mistakenly delete features essential for diagnosing rare conditions due to the gradient dominance of majority classes. To address these challenges, we propose GRIN+, a novel machine unlearning framework designed for fast and precise data erasure in imbalanced medical scenarios. GRIN+ decouples unlearning-specific knowledge from generalized representations at the parameter level by analyzing the gradient contributions of both "forget" and "retain" sets. It introduces a class-adaptive influence scoring mechanism to rectify gradient dominance and employs a direction-constrained update strategy to prevent the unintended erosion of vital clinical knowledge. Comprehensive benchmarking across multiple medical datasets, including skin cancer (ISIC), brain tumor (MRI), and breast ultrasound (BUSI), demonstrates that GRIN+ achieves an optimal balance of the PEU trilemma. Experimental results show that GRIN+ maintains high diagnostic accuracy and robust privacy while significantly enhancing runtime efficiency compared to existing baselines. We open-source the GRIN+ code and benchmarks to support further research.

</details>


### [87] [Diversified and Perceptible Counterfactual Examples Leveraging Expert Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15609)
*Akram Bensalem,Fahima Djelil,Marie-Jeanne Lesot,Gr{é}gory Smits*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将解释者的模糊语言词汇嵌入到反事实生成流程中，提出 DiCEf，使生成的反事实既可解释又可个性化，且兼顾常见质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反事实示例虽可改变模型输出但可能对解释者缺乏语义可理解性；需用解释者的感知和词汇化表示来约束和指导反事实生成。

Method: 构建基于模糊语言词汇（linguistic vocabulary）的连续嵌入空间，处理由词汇域导致的特征依赖性，通过在该空间定义的优化/采样机制变体扩展 DiCE，得到可个性化的 DiCEf。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，DiCEf 能生成在模糊语言域中语义可感知的反事实，同时保留代价最小性、稀疏性与多样性等性质。

Conclusion: DiCEf 将模糊语言词汇域引入反事实解释生成流程，使得生成的反事实在解释者感知上更具语义可感知性，同时保持最小代价、稀疏性和多样性。

Abstract: CounterFactual Examples (CFEs) are a cornerstone of eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), offering local, post hoc, and model-agnostic explanations by identifying minimal input modifications that alter a model's prediction. Yet, in order to be intelligible, these modifications must also be semantically meaningful to the explainee. This paper proposes to integrate knowledge expressed as a fuzzy linguistic vocabulary to represent the explainee's perception and interpretation of the data. The domain induced by this fuzzy vocabulary imposes structural constraints that make the features dependent, preventing the use of gradient-based optimisation methods for CFE generation, e.g., DiCE. The paper proposes a continuous data embedding in this linguistic domain and exploits it to define a variant of DiCE that allows personalisation for the explainee, named DiCEf. As illustrated by experimental results on a real-world dataset, this extension of the DiCE method enables the generation of CFEs that are linguistically perceptible while preserving cost minimality, sparsity, and diversity.

</details>


### [88] [EEG-Xplain: Decoding Neural Black-Boxes of EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15687)
*Hansong Ma,Junxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个跨架构的EEG模型归因框架，结合多类解释方法与群体级评估，并用LLM生成报告，实验证明解释与神经生理标志一致且能揭示伪相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG基础模型虽性能优秀但为黑盒，临床信任与神经科学验证受限；需要一个统一且可量化的可解释性框架来评估模型是否学到生理学上合理的表征并发现伪相关。

Method: 整合梯度型、扰动型和激活型三类解释方法，在空间上用拓扑图标识关键通道，时间上用归因热图标注决策相关片段，频率上通过谱扰动量化典型脑电节律的贡献；引入基于AOPC和跨方法一致性的群体级评估；并用大语言模型将结构化归因输出生成自然语言报告。

Result: 在Mumtaz2016和TUAB等基准数据集上，归因结果与已知神经生理标志（例如特定节律和通道分布）一致，评价框架可检测到模型对伪影与非生理模式的依赖，证明了框架在可解释性、可靠性与生理合理性评估中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一归因框架能对不同架构的EEG基础模型给出空间、时间和频率维度的可解释性分析，并通过群体级评估验证解释的可靠性，实验表明解释结果与已知神经生理标志一致，同时揭示了对伪影和虚假模式的依赖。

Abstract: EEG foundation models such as BIOT, LaBraM, and EEGMamba have achieved remarkable performance in neural signal decoding, but their black-box nature limits clinical trust and neuroscientific validation. We propose a unified attribution framework for interpreting EEG foundation models across heterogeneous architectures. The framework integrates gradient-, perturbation-, and activation-based explanation methods to analyze model behavior in spatial, temporal, and frequency dimensions. Spatially, it identifies critical EEG channels and visualizes their distributions using topographic maps. Temporally, it highlights decision-relevant signal segments through attribution heatmaps. In the frequency domain, it quantifies the contributions of canonical EEG rhythms via spectral perturbation analysis. To assess explanation reliability, we introduce a population-level evaluation combining Area Over the Perturbation Curve (AOPC) and cross-method consistency analysis. The framework further leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to transform structured attribution outputs into natural-language reports, bridging low-level neural representations and high-level semantic reasoning. Experiments on benchmark datasets, including Mumtaz2016 and TUAB, demonstrate that the generated explanations are consistent with established neurophysiological markers, validating meaningful neural representations while exposing potential dependencies on artifacts and spurious patterns. The proposed framework provides a standardized approach for evaluating the interpretability, reliability, and physiological plausibility of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Accuracy: Robustness, Cost, and Governance Trade-offs for Vision-Language Models in Templated Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15706)
*Kushal Patel,Pushkal Shrivastava,Mackenzie Lees,Qirui Lu,Bhargobjyoti Saikia,Liying Li,Junlin Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过一项用750份合成票据的测评，作者证明少量微调能使开源VLM在票据字段抽取上达到接近完美的F1，优于零-shot 商业模型，并提供了面向实践的模型选择框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM在文档结构化抽取的评测多集中在“干净”基准，缺乏面向任务复杂度与实际约束（质量、延迟、治理、量级）的实用对比，令工程师难以择优。作者旨在填补这一缺口，提供测量证据与可操作的部署建议。

Method: 对11种系统（3个商业、2个推理型、5个开源VLM的预训练与微调版本，以及一个基于OCR->正则的基线）在750份从属保留的合成票据集合上进行测评，比较F1指标；并通过微调实验（3k样本）评估性能提升；最后提出一个基于过滤与总成本最小化的实践者选择框架并以中等规模场景举例说明。

Result: 微调3k样本后，最好的开源VLM F1>0.98，超过所有零-shot 商业系统；在商业池中GPT-5取得最高F1，而Claude Sonnet 4.5在日期字段上严重失败；基线OCR+正则表现明显落后。作者释放了开源资源并给出基于成本和约束的选择流程。

Conclusion: 作者提供了一个面向实践者的、以测量为支撑的比较研究，表明通过少量（≈3k）带标签样本微调，开源视觉-语言模型能在合成票据抽取任务上超过所有零-shot 商业系统，并提出了结合质量/延迟/治理/量级的选择框架以辅助部署决策。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to extract structured fields from business documents, yet most evaluations report accuracy on clean benchmarks and offer little guidance to practitioners choosing an approach for a given task complexity. We address this gap with a measurement-grounded study and an open-source release. Across eleven systems (three commercial, two reasoning, five open-source VLMs in pretrained and fine-tuned form, and a non-LLM OCR->regex floor) scored on a 750-document held-out pool of synthetic checks, fine-tuning on 3K samples lifts the best open-source VLMs above F1 0.98-above every zero-shot commercial system on this task-while GPT-5 leads the commercial pool on F1 and Claude Sonnet 4.5 collapses on Date. To turn these measurements into actionable choices, we introduce a practitioner-oriented selection framework that maps a task profile (quality, latency, governance, volume) to a recommended approach via filtering and total-cost minimization, illustrated on a hypothetical mid-volume document-extraction scenario.

</details>


### [90] [Predicting build orientation for SLM dental parts: a comparison of rotation representations and direct vector regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15710)
*Felix Schmalzel,Reimar Waitz,Moritz Kronberger,Thorsten Schöler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用约2400个牙科零件的数据比较13种旋转表示并训练ResNet-50与PointNeXt-S，发现对已知小集合旋转的测试时间增强是提升上轴预测精度的关键；最佳表示随骨干网不同而不同，ResNet-50+TTA上八面体映射表现最好（平均误差10.6°）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM制造牙科零件的构建方向通常由技术人员手动选择，存在效率与一致性问题。目标是通过监督学习自动预测上轴以减少人工工作并提升一致性。

Method: 把构建方向预测视作监督学习问题，目标为预测零件的上轴（up-axis）。使用约2400个病人特定的牙科零件数据，训练并微调两个骨干网：预训练的ResNet-50多视图图像骨干和PointNeXt-S点云骨干。比较13种上轴表示（涵盖6种经典SO(3)参数化和7种直接定义在单位球S^2上的表示），在测试集中报告预测与标签上轴之间的测地角误差，并在是否使用K=21的已知旋转的测试时间增强（TTA）两种条件下比较。

Result: 在使用TTA时，ResNet-50上八面体映射达到最低平均角误差10.6°。整体上，三种最低误差的结果为直接的S^2表示，但这可能部分来源于SO(3)目标的平面内自由度标签噪声而非拓扑优势。von Mises–Fisher表示在PointNeXt-S上训练时收敛到近常数预测（塌缩），但在ResNet-50上未见此现象。TTA在几乎所有表示与骨干网上将平均角误差降低了31–73%。总体结论：TTA是最一致的精度提升手段，而最优表示强烈依赖所用骨干网。

Conclusion: 测试时间增强（TTA）在小集合已知旋转上的应用是提高预测准确性的最可靠因素；最佳的旋转表示对不同骨干网表现出显著依赖性；使用ResNet-50并配合TTA时，八面体映射（octahedral map）给出最低的平均地心角误差。

Abstract: Build orientation for selective laser melting (SLM) manufacturing of dental parts is usually chosen manually by technicians. We treat orientation prediction as supervised machine learning of the part's up-axis from technician-labeled production data, and test which rotation representations produce the best results. Using $n\approx2400$ patient-specific dental parts, we trained a ResNet-50 multi-view image backbone and a PointNeXt-S point-cloud backbone, both pretrained and fine-tuned end-to-end, on 13 up-axis representations spanning six classical $SO(3)$ parameterizations and seven representations defined directly on the unit sphere $S^2$. We report the geodesic angular error between predicted and ground-truth up-axis on a test set, with and without test-time augmentation (TTA) over $K=21$ known rotations. With TTA, the octahedral map achieves the lowest mean angular error ($10.6^\circ$, ResNet-50). The three lowest-error results overall are direct $S^2$ representations, though this may reflect label noise in the unsupervised in-plane component of the $SO(3)$ targets rather than a topological advantage. von Mises-Fisher collapses to a near-constant prediction when trained with PointNeXt-S but not with ResNet-50. TTA reduces mean angular error by 31-73 % across almost every representation and backbone. Overall, test-time augmentation over a small set of known rotations is the most consistent driver of accuracy, whereas the best-performing representation is strongly backbone-dependent.

</details>


### [91] [Data storytelling meets interpretable machine learning: Decoding AI decisions for non-experts without revealing sensitive data and model details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15722)
*Lemen Chao,Zixuan Yang,Anran Fang,Mingran Sun,Ming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将SHAP与LLM驱动的ABT数据叙事结合，提出DIST与I-P-O框架及What-if/Why-not生成架构，在案例研究中显著提高了非专家对模型解释的可理解性与可访问性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的IML技术（如SHAP图）对非专家不够友好，需要一种更可接近的表达方式，将模型解释以叙事化形式呈现以提高普适可读性与决策可采纳性。

Method: 基于设计科学研究（DSR）范式：形式化定义数据叙事在IML中的角色，提出DIST Pyramid与I-P-O交互模型；设计包含What-if与Why-not事件发生器和数据脱敏的解释架构；用SHAP计算特征贡献并用LLM生成ABT（And-But-Therefore）格式的数据故事；在波士顿房价数据集上进行案例研究并通过受访者问卷评估可理解性与可访问性。

Result: 用户研究显示76.4%和74.3%的受访者分别认为“What-if”和“Why-not”数据故事更易理解，可访问性评分显著高于传统SHAP可视化；论文提出了叙事解释框架并讨论了实践应用与研究扩展。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了一种将数据叙事与可解释机器学习（IML）结合的叙事解释框架。通过DIST金字塔、I-P-O模型，以及以“What-if”和“Why-not”为核心的事件生成架构，并辅以数据脱敏与大模型生成ABT结构故事，展示了比传统SHAP可视化更高的可理解性与可访问性。

Abstract: AI-driven automated decision-making requires both predictive performance and interpretability. Recent advances in interpretable machine learning (IML) provide tools for explaining model predictions, but the technical complexity of these explanations may hinder accessibility to non-experts. To address this challenge, this study integrates data storytelling with IML to enhance the explainability of AI-generated decisions for a broader audience. Following the design science research (DSR) paradigm, this study proposes a formal definition of data storytelling in IML, introduces the DIST Pyramid to align data storytelling with IML, and presents the I-P-O Model to describe their interactions. It further develops an architecture to explain AI decisions through distinct "What-if" and "Why-not" event-generation processes. The architecture also employs data desensitization to protect sensitive input data. To validate the approach, a case study is conducted with the Boston Housing dataset, using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) values and large language models (LLMs) to generate data stories with And-But-Therefore (ABT) structures. An empirical evaluation shows that 76.4% and 74.3% of respondents rated the "What-if" and "Why-not" data stories as more comprehensible, with significantly higher accessibility scores than traditional SHAP visualizations. The paper concludes with the presentation of a narrative interpretation framework that integrates IML and data storytelling, thereby expanding the research scope as well as the practical applicability of AI decision-making.

</details>


### [92] [Are LLMs Good Financial User Simulators? A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15727)
*Jiajie He,Jiangyuan Hong,Dongling Ni,Wenjin Liu,Xintong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 实验显示：市场上下文能帮助LLM更好地预测是否交易与交易标的，但难以准确预测交易量，且模型存在系统性行为压缩，需更大规模和更真实条件下的后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大规模语言模型在模拟个体随时间演化的金融决策（用于压力测试、产品设计和行为研究）方面的能力与限制，特别是在受限可观测信息下的预测精度与行为保真度。

Method: 对120名志愿者进行受控的纸上交易实验，使用不可赎回的虚拟资金在实时市场条件下交易；基于截断时点之前可得信息进行滚动时间对齐预测，比较含/不含时点市场信息的消融实验，评估对下一交易日的动作、标的与交易数量的预测性能。

Result: 引入点时点市场信息可提升动作与标的预测；交易规模预测仍困难；模型表现出行为压缩倾向（高bias向‘持有’、低估‘卖出’、将多证券交易简化为少量标的）。这些初步发现提示需扩展样本、丰富特征并改进评估指标以获得更全面结论。

Conclusion: 在受控纸上交易实验中，LLM作为用户行为模拟器在预测“是否交易”和“交易哪只股票”上在有市场情境时表现有所提升，但在交易量预测上表现差，并且存在系统性行为压缩（过多预测持有、低估卖出、简化多证券交易）。需要更大规模、多样化与更真实激励的评估来检验个体—时间—组合层面的拟合度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as user simulators, but their ability to reproduce evolving individual financial decisions remains unclear. We present a preliminary study in a controlled paper-trading environment with 120 volunteers. Participants used non-redeemable virtual funds under real-time market conditions; no real brokerage accounts, real-money positions, or real transaction records were accessed. Given only information available before a prediction cutoff, a simulator predicts the participant's next-trading-day action, traded security, and transaction quantity. We evaluate temporally aligned rolling predictions and compare settings with and without point-in-time market information. Market context improves action and ticker prediction in the controlled ablation, while transaction sizing remains difficult. We also observe systematic behavioral compression: models overproduce hold actions, underpredict sell decisions, and simplify multi-security transactions. These results provide an initial empirical characterization and motivate larger-scale evaluation of individual, temporal, and portfolio-level behavioral fidelity.

</details>


### [93] [Design of a Deep Learning Credit Risk Early Warning System Integrating Multi-source Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15744)
*LiYang Wang,Zhen Zhong,Zhen Tian,Keyu Chen,Keyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用深度网络与注意力机制融合交易与社交等异构数据，提升信用风险预警的准确性与时效性，优于规则基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统金融风险预警中信息孤岛与监控滞后问题；利用数据融合与实时分析的新技术实现更早、更准确的风险发现以便及时干预。

Method: 构建一套融合交易行为与社交网络等异构数据的模型架构，采用深度神经网络结合注意力机制进行多维特征提取与加权融合，形成面向企业与个人的信用风险早期识别模块，并通过系统测试与传统规则基线比较性能。

Result: 系统测试表明该方法在准确率与预警及时性方面显著优于传统规则引擎，证明了异构信息融合与深度注意力模型在提前识别信用风险方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的异构信息融合信用风险预警系统通过深度神经网络与注意力机制构建多模态特征抽取与早期识别机制，可显著提升风险预警的准确性与时效性，优于传统规则引擎，对金融稳健性具有实际意义。

Abstract: Advancements in data fusion and real-time analytics technologies have opened new avenues for addressing complex domain challenges. Financial risk early warning systems often suffer from inefficiency due to information silos and monitoring delays. This paper proposes a credit risk early warning system based on heterogeneous information fusion. The system employs a model architecture integrating deep neural networks and attention mechanisms to extract multidimensional features from diverse data sources such as transaction behaviors and social networks, thereby establishing an early identification mechanism for corporate and individual credit risks. System testing demonstrates that this approach significantly enhances the accuracy and timeliness of risk warnings, outperforming traditional rule-based engine solutions. The findings offer innovative insights for early intervention in financial risks, holding practical significance for safeguarding financial stability.

</details>


### [94] [EvoOntology: A Self-Evolving Ontology Layer for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15779)
*Meiduo Chong,Shaolei Zhang,Ju Fan,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoOntology 通过一个可查询的自演化本体服务，自动构建并按代理反馈迭代，显著提升数据代理在异构数据上的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据代理面对异构存储体外部化的数据只能通过通用工具访问，直接探索或手工注入语义层都无法在大规模异构数据和不同代理行为下有效扩展与适配，需一种可自动构建且能随代理行为演化的语义层。

Method: 将本体封装为 MCP 服务器，包含 schema、content、tool 三层；引入 builder agent 实现自动构建；采用自我演化循环，通过归因指导的类型化编辑并在 backbone 条件的成对评估后才接受修改。

Result: 在三个常用数据代理基准上，使用四个不同 LLM 骨干的实验显示 EvoOntology 一致优于现有方法与语义层方案，证明其能更有效地与异构数据交互；且代码已开源。

Conclusion: EvoOntology 提出了一种自我演化的本体层，使数据代理能在运行时主动查询与交互，从而显著缩小代理与外部异构数据之间的差距，并在多个基准和不同 LLM 骨干上优于强基线。

Abstract: Data agents aim to fulfill natural-language instructions over heterogeneous data, including tables, files, and databases. However, data agents face a challenging agent-data gap: heterogeneous data resides outside the agent, while the agent can access it (e.g., column names and file paths) only through generic tools. Existing approaches either let agents directly explore raw data sources or inject manually constructed semantic layers into prompts. However, neither scales well to large heterogeneous data sources nor adapts to different agent behaviors. In this paper, we introduce EvoOntology, a self-evolving ontology layer for data agents. EvoOntology encapsulates the ontology as an MCP server comprising a schema layer, a content layer, and a tool layer, enabling agents to actively query and interact with the ontology at runtime. To this end, we introduce a builder agent for autonomous ontology construction and a self-evolution loop that continuously refines the ontology through attribution-guided typed edits that are accepted only after a backbone-conditional paired evaluation. Experiments on three well-adopted data-agent benchmarks with four LLM backbones demonstrate that EvoOntology consistently outperforms strong baselines and existing semantic-layer approaches, effectively bridging the agent-data gap and enabling more effective interaction with heterogeneous data. Code: https://github.com/ruc-datalab/EvoOntology

</details>


### [95] [KnowBench: Effort Reduction as a Unified, Deployment-Grounded Benchmark for Clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15794)
*Jocelyn Kang,Caroline Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowBench 用 "医师签署接受率" 表征 AI 替代的临床工作量（ER），并在生产部署上报告了极高的文档化 ER（≈98%），同时提出了可审计的报告协议，但伴随统计与部分协议细节被保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出现有研究性评估指标（参考相似度、专家打分）衡量的是与人工产物的相似性而非实际减少的临床工作负担，提出一个以减少实际负担为核心的可审计统一指标以方便跨系统比较和可验证的产业化评估。

Method: 将"医师审阅并签署"作为金标准：把系统生成的工作单元（如病历句子、编码、医嘱）逐单位与临床医师的接受/修正情况对应。每个被接受的单元计入已替代的工作量，任何被修改或重做的部分计入残余工作量；在多个行政性任务上以相同构造实例化 ER 并在生产环境中统计大样本结果。

Result: 在超过六个月、十三个专科、超过一百万次签署的生产窗口内，Knowtex 的专有微调临床基础模型在闭环反馈架构中对文档化任务实现了总体 ER=97.99%，并给出分专科汇总（96.8–98.9%）。论文主要贡献为基准定义、退化情形讨论与可审计的报告协议，当前发布为部分协议清单并声明有若干伴随统计被保留未公开。

Conclusion: KnowBench 提出并推广了以“Effort Reduction (ER)”为统一度量的临床 AI 基准，主张用临床医师的审阅与签署来衡量系统替代的人力工作量，旨在实现跨任务、跨系统的可比性与可审计性。论文报告了基于该基准的首批结果：Knowtex 在文档化任务上对超过一百万次签署的门诊/住院记录的生产性部署，宣称总体 ER 为 97.99%，各专科 96.8–98.9%。

Abstract: Clinical AI systems are evaluated with instruments built for research settings (reference-based similarity metrics and expert rubric panels) that measure resemblance to an artifact rather than reduction of a burden. We introduce KnowBench, pioneered by Knowtex, whose unifying metric is Effort Reduction (ER): the proportion of system-generated clinical work product accepted by the responsible clinician under expert and safety review. ER is defined once and instantiated per task across the administrative workload clinical AI automates: visit notes, diagnosis and billing codes, orders, EHR chart summarization, patient after-visit summaries, and clinical decision support. In every instantiation the construction is identical: the clinician's review-and-attestation event is the ground truth, every accepted unit is work the system completed, and every correction is residual effort returned to the clinician. The primary contribution of this paper is the benchmark itself: the metric, its degenerate cases, and a reporting protocol under which ER claims are auditable and cross-system comparable. Alongside it we report an initial headline measurement from the documentation instantiation: over one million signed encounters across a production window exceeding six months and thirteen medical specialties, Knowtex's proprietary fine-tuned clinical foundation models operating inside a closed feedback architecture achieve an aggregate ER of 97.99%, with per-specialty aggregates spanning 96.8-98.9%. This release reports the protocol's checklist partially, and states which companion statistics are withheld; the benchmark is offered so that this figure, and every figure reported after it, can be held to the same standard.

</details>


### [96] [Navigating Sparse Evidence: Agentic Visual RAG via Explicit Context Selection and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15800)
*Yucheng Shen,Lingyong Yan,Jiulong Wu,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin WU,Dawei Yin,Min Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCoRE通过在探索时保留被筛选的观测与指针并在结束时重载与合并原始图像，构建显式、有序的视觉证据集；配合轨迹蒸馏与证据感知的强化学习，实现对VRAG任务更鲁棒、可追踪的证据支持式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VRAG面临两大问题：1) 答案相关证据稀疏且可能集中或分散；2) 现有方法常基于原始探索轨迹或压缩文本记忆生成答案，导致噪声、证据不可追溯或缺乏显式支持图像集合。论文认为瓶颈在于证据发现之外的保存与组织。

Method: 提出一个统一的智能体循环：探索阶段只保留相关观测及其源指针到文本账本（ledger），保持视觉上下文受控；终止时重载被引用的原始页面图像并将其按逻辑顺序合并以供最终回答。训练上结合“过滤的冷启动轨迹蒸馏（filtered cold-start trajectory distillation）”与基于证据的强化学习（reward 促证据覆盖、合并紧凑性与答案正确性）的范式以实现端到端优化。

Result: 方法应能提供严格的图像索引的事实-图像（claim-to-image）链接、减少探索噪声对最终推理的影响、提升证据覆盖与答复准确性。训练范式预期在证据覆盖、合并紧凑性与最终答案正确率上取得综合提升。

Conclusion: SCoRE通过在探索阶段显式筛选并记录与查询相关的观测及其图像指针，并在终止时重载并合并这些图像证据，成功将最终推理与试错式探索解耦，从而提升视觉证据的保存性、一致性和可追溯性，进而改进基于视觉检索增强生成（VRAG）任务的答复质量与可证据化性。

Abstract: Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (VRAG) empowers models to navigate and answer queries about visually rich documents by retrieving relevant page images as visual evidence and reasoning over their content. However, effectively utilizing this visual evidence is usually impeded by two main challenges. First, answer-relevant evidence is sparse and may be concentrated in a small region of one page or dispersed across multiple pages. Second, existing agentic methods often generate answers based on raw exploration trajectories or compressed textual memories rather than an explicitly organized set of supporting images, making answers susceptible to exploration noise and obscuring the evidence-backed reasoning trace. We argue that the bottleneck lies not only in evidence discovery but also in its preservation and organization before answer generation. We propose SCoRE (Selection and Consolidation for Robust Evidence), a unified agent loop for explicit evidence selection and consolidation. During exploration, SCoRE retains only query-relevant observations and their source pointers in a maintained textual ledger, preserving earlier evidence while keeping the visual context bounded. At termination, it reloads the referenced original images and consolidates the visual evidence for answering, arranging it into a logical sequence. This decouples final reasoning from exploratory trial-and-error while ensuring strict visual grounding via indexed claim-to-image linkages. To enable end-to-end optimization of this unified rollout, our training paradigm combines filtered cold-start trajectory distillation with evidence-aware reinforcement learning, whose reward promotes evidence coverage, consolidation compactness, and answer correctness.

</details>


### [97] [Atria Dawn: The Dawn of Agentic Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15818)
*Honglin Guo,Tao Gui,Yicheng Chen,Guanting Dong,Qiming Ge,Yuyang Hu,Zixian Huang,Jiajie Jin,Alexander Lam,Yining Li,Jiahang Lin,Yanjiang Liu,Xinyu Lu,Haijun Lv,Junlin Shang,Qisheng Su,Guoqiang Wang,Rui Wang,Zhecan Wang,Hao Xiang,Xinchen Xie,Shuhao Xing,Xiaoyu Xing,Wanghan Xu,Xinyu Yang,Yajie Yang,Chengfeng Zhao,Haoran Zhao,Ruojun Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Yicheng Zou,Kun Cai,Qiye Cai,Xinmeng Che,Haodong Chen,Jiabei Chen,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Yujia Chen,Lizhi Cui,Youheng Dai,Xin Deng,Yi Dong,Shihan Dou,Chenya Gu,Xu Guo,Ding Han,Feiyang Hao,Haotan He,Jie Hou,Binze Hu,Zijian Hu,Junhao Huang,Huicheng Jiang,Jiazhen Jiang,Shufan Jiang,Jiahao Kuang,Bowen Lai,Bo Li,Jiaqiang Li,Peng Li,Qilong Li,Zhuoqun Li,Jiaxiang Liu,Shuainan Liu,Tong Liu,Yi Liu,Zhonghang Lu,Jianwen Luo,Yanyi Luo,Huijie Lv,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Houcheng Min,Chengjun Pan,Qiyuan Peng,Xiaoxuan Peng,Jianmin Qian,Jiantao Qiu,Wanying Ren,Huayu Sha,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Bing Shao,Zhuohui Sheng,Jiayang Shi,Yang Shu,Aierpanjiang Simayi,Sirui Song,Yuxiao Song,Zhe Sun,Zhichao Sun,Wenzhe Tan,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Hanchen Wang,Pengbo Wang,Rui Wang,Yiding Wang,Yuhui Wang,Zhiheng Xi,Caijun Xu,Chao Xu,Yongfeng Xu,Xiaolei Yang,Zhixiong Yang,Qian Yao,Shihong Yi,Yuankai Ying,Jia Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Hao Yuan,Junjie Yuan,Bo Zhang,Caixian Zhang,Qiuyinzhe Zhang,Jiyuan Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Ying Zhao,Pujun Zheng,Xiaoxue Zhong,Xiaohao Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Guanru Zhu,Yulun Zhu,Yaojie Lu,Tao Ji,Hongyu Lin,Yutao Zhu,Pengfei Cao,Guoxiu He,Xianpei Han,Ben He,Zhicheng Dou,Kang Liu,Qi Zhang,Le Sun,Jun Zhao,Ji-Rong Wen,Xuanjing Huang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并评估了通过可验证经验流水线训练的面向科研/工程的agentic基础模型，展示了在多基准上的竞争力与在实际人机协作中从任务执行向项目合伙的角色转变，并强调同时提升发现能力与人类监督能力的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理参与到其后继者的开发中，智能的生产方式与人类研究者的角色发生变化。目标是扩展智能代理在真实世界科研与工程场景中的生产力边界，同时理解和保障人类在监督与决策中的作用。

Method: 训练了一个基础的agentic语言模型（Atria Dawn Preview），采用“可验证经验流水线”（Verifiable Experience Pipeline）将工具驱动的交互映射到可执行环境并以外部可验证的结果作为监督；在16个涵盖真实研究、工程与数字工作的基准上进行评估；并以56名参与者、769条任务记录与代理日志为素材开展人-机协作的案例研究与问卷/评估。
