Result: 在16个基准上表现与前沿代理相当，在其中5个基准上达到报道的最高得分；在由人类参与者评估的AI辅助任务中，约1/3被评为没有AI难以实现；代理常提出方法并实现修订，人类保留大部分最终决策并通过判断与反馈引导探索。

Conclusion: Atria Dawn Preview推动了面向科学研究与工程工作流的agentic语言模型发展，通过可验证经验流水线将工具交互与可执行环境和外部验证结果连接，表现出在多个真实世界基准上竞争性甚至领先的能力，同时通过人机协作案例研究揭示了从任务执行向项目合伙的角色转变，强调在提高发现能力的同时必须强化可意义的人类监督与责任。

Abstract: As AI agents become participants in the development of their successors, they reshape both the production of intelligence and the role of human researchers. We introduce Atria Dawn Preview, a foundation agentic language model designed for scientific research and engineering workflows, with the goal of expanding the frontier of agent productivity in the real world. This model is trained via a Verifiable Experience Pipeline that connects tool-mediated interactions to executable environments and externally verified outcomes. Across 16 benchmarks spanning real-world research, engineering, and digital work, Atria Dawn Preview is competitive with frontier agents and achieves the highest reported score on five of them. Beyond standalone performance, we examine the real research-and-development process behind this model as a case study of human--AI collaboration, analyzing 769 task records from 56 participants together with agent logs. When asked to evaluate completed tasks under comparable conditions, participants rated about one-third of completed AI-assisted tasks as infeasible without AI. More strikingly, agents frequently propose methods and implement revisions, while humans retain most final decisions and guide exploration through judgment and feedback. These observations indicate a shift from task-level execution to project-level partnership, with human effort concentrating on what is worth pursuing and how evidence should guide research. Progress toward more autonomous AI research must therefore advance both the capacity for discovery and the capacity for meaningful human oversight, preserving accountable human authority over the risks and direction of continued development.

</details>


### [98] [AlgoEvo: Self-Evolving Agentic Search for Automated Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15820)
*Junhao Qiu,Qinglong Hu,Xialiang Tong,Mingxuan Yuan,Liyong Lin,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlgoEvo 通过自治智能体、技能库与分层经验，把算法发现变为可交互、可积累的搜索过程，从而在效率与性能上优于或匹配现有专门方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动算法发现框架被预定义控制流束缚，缺乏自适应推理、跨范式迁移能力，并丢失运行时执行的有价值反馈，限制了效率与泛化。

Method: 提出一个自治智能体框架：智能体基于运行时反馈动态检查、诊断与编辑代码；设计技能库（design skill hub）将范式特定知识与核心发现引擎解耦以支持单目标/多目标/多组件设计；分层经验机制将搜索轨迹组织为任务级树以引导探索，并将跨任务模式凝练为可复用技能。

Result: 在六个代表性基准上，AlgoEvo 用显著更少的评估次数与更低的 token 消耗达成或超越专门方法表现，展示了强的任务内积累、跨任务迁移和通过技能激活实现 SOTA 或更优的能力。

Conclusion: AlgoEvo 将自动算法发现问题重塑为一个交互式、积累知识的过程，能够在少量评估与更低 token 消耗下，在六项基准任务上匹配或超越专门方法，具备任务内积累、任务间迁移与可通过技能激活复现或超越现有 SOTA 的能力。

Abstract: Large language models have advanced automated algorithm discovery by synthesizing executable code, but existing frameworks trap them in rigid search pipelines with pre-defined control flows. This limitation restricts adaptive reasoning, blocks cross-paradigm transfer, and discards valuable execution feedback. We propose AlgoEvo, a unified agentic framework that transforms automated algorithm discovery into an interactive, knowledge-accumulating process. An autonomous agent dynamically inspects, diagnoses, and edits code based on runtime feedback. A design skill hub decouples paradigm-specific knowledge from the core discovery engine, allowing a single workflow to seamlessly handle single-objective, multi-objective, and multi-component design. Meanwhile, a hierarchical experience mechanism organizes search trajectories into a task-level tree to guide exploration and consolidates cross-task patterns into reusable skills. Across six representative benchmark tasks, AlgoEvo matches or surpasses specialized methods with substantially fewer evaluations and reduced token consumption, demonstrating strong intra-task accumulation, cross-task transfer, and the ability to reproduce or exceed existing state-of-the-art performance through flexible skill activation.

</details>


### [99] [LongAgent: History-Guided Agentic Search for Longitudinal Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15859)
*Siyao Wang,Florian Guitton,Shuojie Fu,Guanyu Tao,Kai Sun,Wenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongAgent提出一种带历史记忆的智能体搜索策略，自动选择变量、时间窗和聚合方法，合成数据上有小幅显著改进，真实数据上达到基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学纵向数据高度异构，需要自动化发现具预测价值的变量及其时间聚合方式，以提高对未来结局的预测能力并减轻手工特征工程负担。

Method: 提出基于智能体的搜索框架：定义搜索空间（变量组合、时间窗口、聚合函数），用智能体执行探索并利用历史记忆和数值证据引导后续搜索；在合成和真实临床数据上用RMSE评估预测性能。

Result: 在合成数据上，LongAgent的平均预测RMSE为1.7376，比最强非智能基线低0.0151（95% CI: [0.0045,0.0260], p=0.0273）；在真实临床数据上与最佳基线性能相当。

Conclusion: LongAgent能够自动搜索变量集合、时间窗口和纵向聚合函数，历史记忆和数值证据可引导搜索，合成数据上略优于最强非智能基线（RMSE改善0.0151，95% CI [0.0045,0.0260], p=0.0273），真实临床数据上表现与最佳基线可比。

Abstract: Extracting informative representations from longitudinal data that can predict future outcomes remains a critical challenge in medicine. Medical datasets are inherently heterogeneous, consisting of a large number of variables collected from different sources, sampled with different temporal spacings, and representing different aspects of human health status. This requires identifying those variables with predictive value, processing longitudinal information, and integrating multiple variables for outcome prediction. Here, we propose a novel agent-based approach, LongAgent, that can autonomously search over combinations of variable sets, temporal windows and longitudinal aggregation functions, and identify candidates with promising predictive performance. LongAgent utilises a history memory of previous searches and numerical evidence to guide subsequent exploration. On synthetic data, LongAgent achieves a mean prediction RMSE of 1.7376 and improves over the strongest non-agent baseline by 0.0151 (95% CI: [0.0045,0.0260]; p=0.0273). On a real clinical dataset, it performs comparably to the best baseline.

</details>


### [100] [Pilot Early, Commit Late: A Real-Options Model of Enterprise AI Adoption under Rapid Technological Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15919)
*Gaurav Tewari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A two-period model of AI adoption shows pilots let firms learn without full commitment; uncertainty increases experimentation value; modularity and valuable firm-specific learning can justify immediate deployment or delayed commitment; closed-form thresholds guide managerial timing decisions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rapidly advancing AI frontier, partial irreversibility of implementation, and the accumulation of organization-specific capabilities create a timing problem for firms deciding whether to adopt immediately, experiment, or wait.

Method: A tractable two-period decision model in which a firm chooses among immediate deployment, a limited pilot, and waiting. The model assumes a stochastic technology frontier, irreversible deployment with risk of architectural obsolescence, and pilot-based organization-specific learning that trades off current operating value. Analytical derivation yields five timing results and a comparative result on where learning occurs; a continuous-time limit is also analyzed.

Result: 1) Mean-preserving increase in frontier uncertainty raises value of waiting and piloting; immediate deployment unchanged when payoff affine in frontier. 2) Faster expected frontier progress can reduce attractiveness of immediate deployment when current architecture captures limited share of future improvement. 3) Pilot dominates waiting iff expected value of built capability exceeds pilot cost. 4) High value of organization-specific learning creates region where 'pilot early, commit late' is optimal. 5) Closed-form modularity threshold above which immediate deployment dominates outside options. 6) Production learning and pilot-specific learning affect timing differently; continuous-time extension: uncertainty raises adoption threshold, capability and modularity lower it.

Conclusion: The paper shows that when the AI technology frontier is uncertain and implementations are partly irreversible, running pilots can be an optimal intermediate strategy: uncertainty raises the value of waiting and piloting, modularity and organization-specific learning determine whether immediate deployment dominates, and there exists a closed-form modularity threshold for immediate commitment. A continuous-time extension recovers standard comparative statics: uncertainty raises the adoption threshold while capability and modularity lower it.

Abstract: Artificial intelligence presents firms with an unusual timing problem. The technology frontier is improving rapidly, implementation is partly irreversible, and organization-specific capabilities are accumulated through action. This paper develops a two-period decision model of AI deployment under uncertainty in which a firm chooses among immediate deployment, a limited pilot, and waiting. Deployment earns current operating value but exposes the firm to architectural obsolescence; waiting preserves the option to adopt after the frontier is observed; a pilot sacrifices current operating value to build organization-specific learning without full commitment. The model yields five central timing results and a sixth comparative result on where learning occurs. First, a mean-preserving increase in frontier uncertainty raises the value of waiting and piloting but leaves immediate deployment unchanged when its payoff is affine in the frontier. Second, faster expected frontier progress can reduce the relative attractiveness of immediate deployment when deployed architecture captures only a limited share of future improvement. Third, a pilot dominates waiting exactly when the expected value of the capability it builds exceeds its cost. Fourth, sufficiently valuable organization-specific learning creates a nonempty region in which "pilot early, commit late" is optimal. Fifth, there is a closed-form modularity threshold above which immediate deployment dominates the best outside option. Sixth, production learning and pilot-specific learning affect the timing margin differently. A continuous-time extension recovers the standard result that uncertainty raises the adoption threshold while capability and modularity lower it. The paper separates deploying, experimenting, and waiting, and shows why rapid progress can rationally increase experimentation without justifying irreversible commitment.

</details>


### [101] [Recurrent GraphNeural NetworkswithSet-BasedAggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15932)
*Blai Bonet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 给出可从权重验证的充分条件，使得一类采用集合聚合的递归GNN与模态μ-演算中BΣ^{}_1片段在表达上等价，提供了从权重到符号公式的可验证编译途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对递归GNN的逻辑刻画依赖多重集合聚合、计数逻辑或不可验证的停机/接受条件，造成可解释性与可验证性的缺失。作者希望找出一类在参数层面可检验且同时具备符号可解释性的递归GNN。

Method: 通过限定聚合为基于集合（不依赖计数）、研究网络在迭代消息传递至稳定态（stabilization）时的行为，构造从网络到公式及从公式到网络的有效翻译算法，并证明两者在有限词汇下对BΣ^{}_1片段存在双向等价。证明避免了计数逻辑、外部终止信号或非有效接受条件的使用；同时给出可从权重直接检查的充分性条件。

Result: 主要结果是：在有限词汇下，满足给定（可从权重检查的）充分条件的递归GNN恰好对应模态μ-演算中仅允许单一极性不动点及其布尔组合的片段BΣ^{}_1（即可达性与安全性布尔闭包）。该片段是稳定化表达能力的精确级别，且不需要计数、外部停机或非有效接受条件。

Conclusion: 论文建立了可从网络参数可检验的充分条件，使得一类递归GNN（采用集合型聚合）能够等价地与模态μ-演算中BΣ^{}_1片段（布尔封闭的可达性与安全性）相互编译，从而为满足条件的网络提供可验证的符号解释路径。

Abstract: Recurrent GNNs iterate message passing to convergence, and their logical characterizations to date rely on multi-set aggregation, graded (counting) logics, and halting or acceptance conditions that cannot be verified from the network's parameters. We study recurrent GNNs with set-based aggregation and identify sufficient conditions checkable from the weights for networks to compile into formulas and formulas into networks. The main result is an effective, two-directional equivalence between a class of networks and the Boolean closure of reachability and safety properties, the fragment B$Σ^{\circ}_1$ of the modal $μ$-calculus. The fragment is not an artifact: it is the exact expressive level of stabilization over finite vocabulary, which supports fixed points of a single polarity and Boolean combinations thereof, but not the composition of fixed points of opposite polarities. The correspondence needs no counting logic, no external halting signal, and no non-effective acceptance condition, yielding a verifiable path from weights to symbolic explanations for networks meeting the conditions.

</details>


### [102] [Stellar Colosseum: A Many-Agent Harness for Long-Horizon Research in Mathematics and Theoretical Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15983)
*Honghao Lin,David P. Woodruff,Yuan Deng,Jieming Mao,Song Zuo,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Stellar Colosseum 是一个用于长证明/研究问题的模型不可知管线，通过并行候选、准备门控、定向反驳与随机抽样树聚合等机制，显著提高了语言模型在复杂数学证明与竞赛编程任务上的可靠性与效果（TCS-Bench 71.0%，Codeforces 218/222）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成看似合理的短证明，但在需要一系列不确定且相互依赖决策的长时程研究问题上仍不可靠，因而需要一个系统化的推理、验证与修正的管线以提高成功率及可验证性。

Method: 提出一种模型不可知的工作流“Colosseum”：在证明构造前探索多条策略、使用“ready gate”判断路线是否成熟、将证明计划表示为互相关联的章节级子问题、将验证器的发现回路至受影响部分；在各阶段并行生成候选、用定向伪证攻击候选、并通过重叠随机抽样树聚合（overlapping random-sample tree aggregation）把候选与其批评合并为单一研究产物。该工作流已集成到 Google Antigravity 的 Teamwork 框架中作为 Long Proof 模式。

Result: 在开放式研究和若干评估上取得显著结果：在 TCS-Bench（来自 FOCS/STOC/SODA 的研究级证明任务）上，使用 Gemini 3.1 Pro 与 Gemini 3.7 Flash 达到 71.0% 的准确率；在 Codeforces 的评测中，配备执行反馈的面向证明的流水线用 Gemini 3.1 Pro 解决了 218/222 道题目；并产出若干针对 FOCS 与 JMLR 发表论文中开放问题的新结果。

Conclusion: Stellar Colosseum 显著提升了语言模型在长时程研究级证明与程序题上的可靠性，通过结构化探索、准备门控、分段子问题、并行候选生成、针对性反证与重采样聚合等机制，使复杂论证能够被有效构建、验证与修正，从而在若干高阶基准上取得了强劲的实证表现。

Abstract: Language models can produce plausible short proofs, but may still be unreliable on long-horizon research problems, where progress depends on a sequence of uncertain and interdependent decisions. We introduce Stellar Colosseum, a model-agnostic harness for allocating inference across research in mathematics and theoretical computer science. Colosseum explores alternative strategies before proof construction, uses a readiness gate to decide when a route is mature enough to decompose, represents the proof plan as interdependent section-level subproblems, and routes verifier findings back to the affected part of the argument. Across these stages, it generates candidates in parallel, attacks them with targeted falsification, and combines candidates and their critiques into a single research artifact through overlapping random-sample tree aggregation. The Colosseum workflow has also been integrated into Google Antigravity's Teamwork framework as the Long Proof pattern.
  We demonstrate the capabilities of Colosseum through open-ended research and evaluations on theorem-proving and competitive programming benchmarks. Using Colosseum with Gemini 3.1 Pro, we obtain several new results that address open problems arising from papers published at top venues such as FOCS and JMLR. On TCS-Bench, a benchmark of research-level theorem-proving tasks drawn from papers published at FOCS, STOC, and SODA, Colosseum achieves 71.0% accuracy using Gemini 3.1 Pro and Gemini 3.7 Flash. In a separate Codeforces evaluation using Gemini 3.1 Pro, the proof-oriented pipeline with execution feedback solves 218 of 222 problems.

</details>


### [103] [Corrupt Plans, Clean Traces: Evading Chain-of-Thought Monitoring with Plan Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15989)
*Keertana Chidambaram,Andrew Ilyas,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证“计划注入”攻击：通过在上下文中植入无害表述的有害计划，能够使CoT监控失效，效果在更难任务和更大模型中仍显著，且更多监控资源并不总是有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估并揭示CoT监控系统对上下文注入型对抗攻击（即看似无害的推理文本植入）的鲁棒性和潜在失败模式。

Method: 基于Lanham et al. (2023)提出的可监控性多项选择QA设置和Li et al. (2025)的调查者—代理诱导框架，作者首先发现漏洞，然后在多种可监控性基准和更大模型（例如DeepSeek-R1）上扩展实验，包含案例分析（如Bio-Math）和监控推理预算研究。

Result: 在不同监控基准上实现约25–33%监控规避率；actor会遵循并将注入计划改写为自身推理而不注明来源；在某些情况下将注入暴露给监控器反而使检测率下降（Bio-Math中最多下降50%），并发现额外的思考令牌被用于合理化注入内容而非标记危险。

Conclusion: 在链式思维（CoT）监控中，通过在actor上下文中植入听起来无害但具有害意图的推理（“计划注入”）能成功引导模型执行敌对动作并规避监控器。注入策略在更难任务与更大模型上仍有效，且有时额外的监控资源反而降低检测率。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a safety strategy where the reasoning of a large language model "actor" is inspected by a "monitor" (often another language model) for signs of unsafe planning, deception, or misalignment. We find that planting harmful but benign-sounding reasoning in the actor's context can steer it to perform adversarial actions while evading monitors, an attack we term "plan injection". We initially discover this attack in the multiple-choice question-answering monitorability setting proposed by Lanham et al. (2023), using the investigator-agent elicitation framework of Li et al. (2025). We generalize the attack and show that the discovered behavior scales to harder tasks (achieving 25-33% monitor evasion rates across different monitorability benchmarks) and larger models such as DeepSeek-R1. Across the settings we study, actor models not only follow injected plans but also paraphrase them as their own reasoning, without explicit attribution to the injections. Finally, we find cases where extra monitor resources cause harm - giving the monitor access to the injected plan drops detection by as much as 50% in the Bio-Math task and in a case study on monitor reasoning budget, we find transcripts where additional thinking tokens are spent rationalizing the injected plan rather than flagging it.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Abstention Errors and Segment Support in CUAD: An Auditable Contract-Extraction Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13274)
*Zeki Emre Tekin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对一个固定RoBERTa检查点在CUAD上进行了可复现的回溯评估：在实现较高参考召回的同时，精度低且误报率高，候选级审阅负担与参考级精度差异显著；同时揭示了类别支持不足与测试访问/检查点重叠带来的解释性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同条款抽取基准通常只衡量参考恢复（reference recovery）；但为了评估用于审阅辅助的系统，还需测量不必要输出（误报）与各类别内证据支持情况，进而量化审阅负担与指标解释的差异。

Method: 保留早期冻结运行的候选生成器，修正匹配错误，并在62份训练切分合同上以原始90%召回目标重新选择类别阈值；使用合同自举法估计置信区间，公开原始与更正后的分析、预测和软件检查作为辅助工件。

Result: 修正后的工作点在CUAD上恢复82.2%参考跨度，CUAD精度为8.2%；共返回30,464条候选字符串，其中19,562条属于无注释答案的问题；系统对2,938个无注释问题中回答了1,335个，假阳性率45.4%（95%合同自举区间43.3–47.9%）；匹配候选占返回字符串的20.3%；在41类中仅9类同时具有≥30个正/负合同（若支持阈分别设为20、40，则分别变为18和2）；两类无负合同导致其无答案假阳性率未定义。

Conclusion: 该论文通过对CUAD公开测试切分上固定RoBERTa检查点的回溯可复现评估，表明在追求高召回（原目标90%）并纠正匹配错误后，系统能恢复大部分参考跨度但精度极低，且大量返回候选在无注释答案的问题上造成高误报与审阅负担。

Abstract: Contract-clause extraction benchmarks measure reference recovery, but interpreting a system for review assistance also requires measuring unnecessary output and the evidence available within categories. This paper presents a retrospective, reproducible evaluation of a fixed public RoBERTa checkpoint on CUAD's published 102-contract test split. Candidate generation is preserved from an earlier frozen run; matching errors are corrected and category thresholds are reselected on 62 training-split contracts using the original 90% recall target. The corrected operating point recovers 82.2% of reference spans at 8.2% CUAD precision. Of 30,464 returned candidate strings, 19,562 occur on questions with no annotated answer. The system answers 1,335 of 2,938 such questions, a 45.4% false-positive rate (95% contract-bootstrap interval: 43.3-47.9%). Matching candidates constitute 20.3% of returned strings, illustrating why reference-based precision and candidate-level review burden require different denominators. Only nine of 41 categories have at least 30 positive and 30 negative contracts; this count changes to 18 and two under support minima of 20 and 40. Two categories have no negative contracts, making their no-answer false-positive rates undefined. These findings describe one checkpoint, decoder, and threshold policy. Earlier test access and uncertain checkpoint training overlap limit confirmatory interpretation. The paper supplies the original and corrected analyses, predictions, and software checks as ancillary artifacts; it establishes neither a new release criterion nor production readiness.

</details>


### [105] [SkillSeam: Six Principles for Auditing Agent Skill Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13321)
*Kang Ruiyuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SkillSeam：通过受控扰动和“失败机制→可观测信号→测试”映射，把技能集合作者建议转成可量化的系统诊断，展示六类失配在路由、加载与准确率上的具体影响并发布工具与检查表以供复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技能库增长，单个技能表现良好但系统级失败频出；现有经验性建议难以归一化、验证与复现。需要一套把作者建议变成可测试诊断项的方法，以定位“技能缝隙”导致的系统失效。

Method: 在一个封闭的技能系统上，对六条集合级设计原则（persistence gradient、system coherence、regime gating、orthogonal coverage、flow、granularity discipline）逐条施加受控变体/扰动；为每条原则指定一个预期的失败机制及其最强可观测信号；通过度量路由冲突、加载的技能 token 数、非典型路径、所有权冲突与准确率等指标来验证预测。并发布字节差异变体、任务切片、汇总与设计检查表。

Result: 实验证明不同扰动对系统产生可量化影响：扁平化持久层次使已加载技能 token 增加 60%；悬挂锚点使总 token 增加 64% 且准确率下降 3.1 个百分比点；用同义别名替换非相关控件将非规范路由从 0/32 提升到 15/32，并使配对改写一半被翻转；车道重叠使所有权冲突从 0/16 增至 14/16；平淡触发器将路由冲突从 3/32 提升到 30/32 且已加载技能 token 增加 3.7 倍；粒度混用导致最大准确率下降 12.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: SkillSeam 提供了一套将“技能集合作者建议”转化为可测系统属性的方法，通过对封闭技能系统实施受控扰动并针对每一条集合级原则定义失败机制与可观测信号，发现并量化了不同设计失误在路由、加载与准确率等通道上的影响，从而把经验性作者建议变成测试用例与诊断工具。

Abstract: A folder of competent skills is not yet a reliable system. Skills rarely fail alone; they
  fail at the seams of a collection. As an agent's skill library grows, procedures compete for
  attention, aliases double-load, boundaries blur, and poorly sized skills turn routing errors
  into task failures. We introduce SkillSeam, a method that audits the relationships through
  which individual skill files become a system. It maps each collection-level principle to a
  failure mechanism, its strongest observable, and a controlled perturbation test. From one
  sealed skill system, SkillSeam perturbs six design principles: persistence gradient, system
  coherence, regime gating, orthogonal coverage, flow, and granularity discipline. Crucially,
  each principle is evaluated through the channel its failure mechanism predicts rather than
  through accuracy alone. Flattening the persistence hierarchy increases loaded-skill tokens
  by 60%; a dangling anchor raises total tokens by 64% and shifts accuracy by -3.1pp; with
  skill count and context size held fixed, replacing an unrelated control with a synonymous
  alias raises noncanonical routes from 0/32 to 15/32 and flips half of matched paraphrase
  pairs; in a candidate-ownership audit, overlapping lanes raise reported ownership conflicts
  from 0/16 to 14/16; bland triggers drive routing conflicts from 3/32 to 30/32 and inflate
  loaded-skill tokens 3.7x; and one granularity mis-mix produces the largest accuracy drop,
  -12.5pp. These outcomes turn six pieces of authoring advice into testable system properties
  without treating every probe as confirmation. We release the byte-differenced variants, task
  slices, rollups, and a one-screen design checklist so that other skill systems can measure
  the same failure channels.

</details>


### [106] [The Agentic Company OS: Substrate Inversion for Sustained Enterprise Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13334)
*Oliver Aleksander Larsen,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Persistent agent failures are due to mismatched data representations. Rebuild the shared cognitive substrate around LLM-friendly representations, separate schema translation to an action boundary, and use a Sync Agent plus a four-layer framework to gain reliability and governance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enterprises report that most agent pilots never reach production and deployed agents rarely improve; single-run benchmark success hides repeatability problems. The authors seek an architectural root cause and a solution to enable sustained, auditable, and improvable agent operation.

Method: Position paper proposing a conceptual framework: identify two grounding mechanisms (context-bandwidth asymmetry and cross-loop coupling), introduce a four-layer substrate (Data, Knowledge, Intelligence, Governance), and prescribe a Sync Agent enforcing an action boundary and per-skill trust gradients; analyzes risks and proposes a research agenda.

Result: A conceptual design that maps how an agent-native substrate could be constructed (Markdown as current practical instantiation), how governance and auditability can be made structural, and what research directions and threat models need study.

Conclusion: The paper argues that persistent failures of enterprise AI agents stem from using data representations designed for humans rather than for LLMs, and that rebuilding a shared cognitive substrate aligned with model reasoning can improve reliability, auditability, and long-term learning.

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents often succeed in a demonstration and then stall once they must operate day after day. An industry report estimates that most pilots never reach production and that deployed systems rarely retain feedback or improve over time, while agent benchmarks show single-run successes masking unreliable repetition. We argue that these failure modes share a common architectural root: agents reason over data structured for human operators and traditional applications, not for the language models that power them. This position paper proposes that companies deploying agents in sustained operation should rebuild their cognitive substrate, the shared environment agents read as working context, around representations matched to that reasoning surface, isolating schema translation to the action boundary. Markdown is the instantiation available today, not a proven agent-native primitive. Two mechanisms ground the argument: context-bandwidth asymmetry, the gap between one-pass reading of connected prose and field-by-field typed access that strips relations; and cross-loop coupling, the claim that action, skill, and policy loops compound only if they share one substrate. A four-layer framework (Data, Knowledge, Intelligence, Governance) operationalizes the position, with a Sync Agent enforcing the action boundary and a per-skill trust gradient, making governance and auditability structural properties of the substrate. The position revives the shared-substrate tradition of classical multi-agent systems under LLM-era economics. We analyze the main objections and risks, including indirect prompt injection on the compile path, and outline a research agenda for evaluating substrates directly.

</details>


### [107] [Usage Control in Public Data Exchange Infrastructures for Future Digital Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13450)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov,Fathiyeh Faghih,Eric Jackson,Theo Dimitrakos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对爱沙尼亚X-Road与新兴应用场景的跨学科分析，提出在公共数据交换基础设施中实现数据使用控制的关键需求与概念性愿景，主张将形式验证、运行时监控、需求工程与数字治理结合以实现隐私即设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据密集型公共服务与欧盟数字经济的发展，公共数据交换基础设施需要满足新兴的隐私与合规需求，如隐私即设计。现有研究在各自领域虽有进展，但在跨学科交汇处存在实践挑战，亟需系统化的需求与方案。

Method: 通过以爱沙尼亚X-Road为案例的分析方法，作者从形式验证与监控的技术视角、需求工程视角与数字治理视角进行跨学科综合分析，识别具体使用场景与威胁模型，归纳出关键需求，并提出概念性愿景与路径。

Result: 识别了若干关键要求：可形式化的策略描述、可验证与可监控的执行、端到端治理与问责机制、可组合与可扩展的架构、支持复杂使用场景（例如链式调用与多方参与）的策略语义等；并提出将形式方法、运行时监控、需求工程与治理结合的概念性架构与若干研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文得出结论：在公共数据交换基础设施中实现数据使用控制（DUC）以实现“隐私即设计”需要跨学科的方法；仅靠单一学科的解决方案无法覆盖治理、需求工程、可监测性与形式验证等多维挑战。需要将形式验证与运行时监控、需求工程方法与数字治理原则整合为一套治理与技术并重的架构。

Abstract: Public data exchange infrastructures, such as X-Road, are essential to data-intensive digital public services and the EU digital economy. Their next generation requires advanced data usage control (DUC) mechanisms that address emerging requirements, including privacy by design, to mitigate privacy threats inherent in large-scale automated data exchange. Although research within the individual disciplines already addresses many of the emerging requirements, significant practical challenges arise at the intersection of the disciplines.
  This paper analytically examines the practical challenges and requirements of data usage control in public data exchange infrastructures. We examine the Estonian X-Road system and emerging use cases through an interdisciplinary lens that integrates formal verification and monitoring of usage control, requirements engineering, and digital governance research. We formulate key requirements and propose a conceptual vision for developing usage control in public data-exchange infrastructures to enable future digital-governance systems to comply with privacy requirements by design.

</details>


### [108] [Rethinking Software Development as a Self-Adaptive Socio-Technical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13461)
*Javier Cámara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将软件开发视为“软件＋开发配置”两层耦合自适应系统——面对agentic AI，应同时进化代码与组织配置，作者给出概念框架与示例并指出后续挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备代理性的AI在软件工程各环节广泛介入，传统将人-代理组织视为固定的做法在动态演化的目标、证据与风险条件下会导致配置次优甚至有害，因此需要一个能随上下文演化的自适应组织视角。

Method: 提出概念性框架，刻画开发配置的关键维度（参与者、职责、权限、信息、验证），将组织重构作为与软件演化耦合的适应过程；实现一个概念性原型/示例以验证可行性；讨论面临的技术与社会挑战并规划后续研究。

Result: 给出一个自适应社会-技术系统的建模视角，提出两类耦合适应的概念、实现层面的POC示例，并梳理挑战（如稳定性、验证、信任与度量）与未来研究路线图。

Conclusion: 本文主张将软件开发视为自适应社会-技术系统，不仅软件本身要进化，参与者与职责、权限、信息与验证等开发配置也应随目标、证据、不确定性与风险演化而重构，从而产生两种耦合的适应：软件演化与工程组织重配置。作者通过概念框架与原型证明进行了说明，并列出了挑战与未来工作方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI is expanding across various software engineering activities, yet human-agent organization is typically treated as fixed, which is problematic because development configurations may become suboptimal as development context evolves. We propose viewing software development as a self-adaptive socio-technical system in which both the software project and the development configuration (participants, responsibilities, authority, information, and verification) adapt as goals, evidence, uncertainty, and risk evolve. This yields two coupled forms of adaptation: evolving the software and reconfiguring how subsequent engineering is performed. We illustrate the idea with a proof of concept, outlining challenges and future plans.

</details>


### [109] [Technostress in the Age of AI: A Preliminary Study with Software Professionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15686)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Zixuan Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对121位软件从业者的主题分析表明，AI引起的技术压力超越工具学习，包含工作管理、责任与职业层面的额外压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI快速被采纳并改变软件工程工作，专业人员需不断适应知识、实践和技能；研究动机是识别并表征这些新出现且反复出现的技术适应压力（technostress）的具体表现。

Method: 预备性探索研究：对121名来自26个国家的软件专业人员开展问卷调查，并对其关于工作中AI使用的最近经历进行主题分析。

Result: 研究发现AI相关的technostress不仅体现在学习和使用工具的压力，还体现在必须管理因AI引入而产生的额外工作任务、技术责任、问责要求和职业不确定性等方面，从而扩展了对软件工程师技术压力来源的理解。

Conclusion: AI相关的技术压力不仅来源于快速学习和适应新工具，还来源于随AI采纳而来的工作管理、技术责任和职业变动等额外负担，影响软件专业人员的工作执行与职业发展准备。

Abstract: The rapid adoption and evolution of AI are changing software engineering work and requiring professionals to repeatedly adapt their knowledge, practices, and skills. Although technological adaptation has long characterized software development, less is known about how these new and recurring pressures manifest as technostress. This preliminary exploratory study investigates AI related technostress among software professionals. We conducted a survey and performed a thematic analysis of responses from 121 software professionals across 26 countries reporting their recent experiences with AI at work. Our findings suggest that AI related technostress emerges not only from adapting to rapidly changing technologies, but also from having to manage the work, technical responsibilities, and professional changes that accompany their adoption. This characterization shows that AI introduces pressures beyond learning and using new tools, affecting how software professionals perform and remain accountable for technical work and how they prepare for the future of their careers.

</details>


### [110] [Is Bash All You Need? An Empirical Study of Tool Interfaces for Enterprise Digital Worker Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11999)
*Hazel Mak,Susheel Suresh,Sahil Bhatnagar,Barry Wang,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在可安全隔离任意执行的场景下优先使用 bash；若需固定工具目录出于安全/合规，则考虑 PTC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验通用 shell 是否能在涉及跨应用操作、协同与专业分析的企业工作场景中胜过为特定任务设计的专用工具接口。

Method: 在两个基准（TheAgentCompany 和 APEX-Agents）上，使用 Opus-4.8 与 GPT-5.5 比较五种工具接口：typed tools、typed+bash、bash、bash+持久化代理合成工具，以及 PTC。评估指标包括任务得分与使用的 token 数量。

Result: bash 比纯 typed tools 提升 21.8–24.5 个百分点（TheAgentCompany）和 4.8–7.4 个百分点（APEX-Agents），同时减少 19–72% 的 token 使用；将 typed tools 或持久化工具合成加入 bash 没有显著提升；PTC 用 token 更少，但通常在质量与成本效率上不及 bash。

Conclusion: Bash（通用 shell）在企业级任务上总体优于专用的 typed 工具；当不可限制任意执行时，Programmatic Tool Calling（PTC）为符合安全/合规的折中方案。

Abstract: In this study, we examine whether a general shell can outperform specialized tools on enterprise tasks. Shell-based agents have shown strong results in coding, but enterprise work also involves moving between applications and services, coordinating with coworkers, and performing professional analysis. We compare five tool interfaces on TheAgentCompany and APEX-Agents using Opus-4.8 and GPT-5.5: typed tools, typed tools plus bash, bash alone, bash with persistent agent-synthesized tools, and programmatic tool calling (PTC), which runs programs whose actions are restricted to a typed tool catalog. Bash alone outperforms typed tools on both benchmarks, improving score by 21.8-24.5 pp on TheAgentCompany and 4.8-7.4 pp on APEX-Agents while using 19-72% fewer total tokens. Adding typed tools or persistent tool synthesis to bash produces no detectable pooled score gain. PTC uses fewer tokens than direct typed calls with broadly similar task performance, but generally underperforms bash alone in both quality and cost efficiency. For enterprise practitioners, these results favor bash alone when arbitrary execution can be isolated and PTC when security or compliance policies require a fixed tool catalog.

</details>


### [111] [TyPatch: Transforming Patches into Typestate Rules for Kernel Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13728)
*Ruoyu Wang,Tuo Li,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过把补丁翻译为可执行的 typestate 规则并用共享后端运行，TyPatch 在准确性和成本上显著优于端到端生成完整检查器的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端让 LLM 同时恢复补丁语义并生成完整静态分析器会把简单的缺陷规则变成复杂、脆弱且代价高昂的分析器实现问题，需将两类责任解耦以提高稳定性和效率。

Method: 由 LLM 将历史内核补丁翻译成包含被跟踪对象、动作、守卫、状态转移和违规条件的 typestate 规则；一个通用后端负责绑定规则到程序事件、别名追踪、路径与跨过程传播并生成报告。

Result: 在 Linux v6.16 上发现 559 个不同缺陷，121 个已被内核开发者确认；在 38 个补丁的匹配比较中，TyPatch 使用 88.3–90.1% 更少的生成 tokens，且报告池的精度为对比工作流的 3.42–14.95 倍。

Conclusion: TyPatch 将补丁语义与分析器实现分离：LLM 将补丁翻译为“typestate 规则”，共享后端执行这些规则，从而实现更高效、稳定且高精度的缺陷挖掘。

Abstract: Historical Linux kernel patches capture defect knowledge that applies beyond their original repair sites. Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can generate static-analysis checkers from historical patches and use them to uncover new kernel bugs. However, complete-checker generation requires the model both to recover the defect semantics expressed by a patch and to implement sophisticated program-analysis machinery, including object tracking, alias analysis, path-state maintenance, and interprocedural propagation. Coupling these responsibilities in a single end-to-end code-generation task can turn a simple defect rule into an unstable and expensive analyzer-implementation problem.
  To address this problem, we present TyPatch, which decouples patch-specific defect semantics from analyzer implementation. An LLM translates each patch into a typestate rule specifying its tracked object, actions, guards, transitions, and violations. A shared backend then executes these rules, binding their actions to program events, tracking object identity across aliases, propagating typestate along program paths, and producing reports for all rules. On Linux v6.16, TyPatch finds 559 distinct bugs, 121 of which have been confirmed by kernel developers. In a matched 38-patch comparison with the state-of-the-art complete-checker construction workflow, TyPatch uses 88.3-90.1% fewer generation tokens, while its initial report pools achieve 3.42-14.95$\times$ the precision of those produced by that workflow.

</details>
